// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 1 OF 8
//
// MEMORY FOUNDATION
// DATABASE MIGRATION
// DOCUMENT STORAGE
// FOUNDATIONAL MEMORY
// MEMORY RELATIONSHIPS
// CONTRADICTIONS
// REINFORCEMENT
// RETRIEVAL HISTORY
//
// SURGERY INSTRUCTION:
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART AFTER THE EXISTING V12 CODE.
//
// DO NOT DELETE THE EXISTING recall_memories TABLE.
// DO NOT DELETE EXISTING PLAYER DATA.
//
// This wraps V12's existing ensureRecallDatabase() and then
// safely installs the V13 schema.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// V13 MEMORY VERSION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION =
  13;


// =========================================================
// V13 MEMORY TYPES
// =========================================================

const RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES =
  Object.freeze({
    FACT:
      "fact",

    EVENT:
      "event",

    QUOTE:
      "quote",

    BELIEF:
      "belief",

    OPINION:
      "opinion",

    PREFERENCE:
      "preference",

    PROMISE:
      "promise",

    DISPUTE:
      "dispute",

    QUESTION:
      "question",

    PATTERN:
      "pattern",

    JUDGMENT:
      "judgment",

    RELATIONSHIP:
      "relationship",

    CONTRADICTION:
      "contradiction",

    SELF_POSITION:
      "self_position",

    DOCUMENT:
      "document",

    DOCUMENT_INSIGHT:
      "document_insight",

    NAME_EVENT:
      "name_event",

    COMBAT:
      "combat",

    SHOW_EVENT:
      "show_event",

    OTHER:
      "other"
  });


// =========================================================
// V13 MEMORY CONFIDENCE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_MEMORY_CONFIDENCE_MIN =
  0;

const RECALL_MEMORY_CONFIDENCE_MAX =
  1;


// =========================================================
// V13 MEMORY IMPORTANCE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MIN =
  1;

const RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MAX =
  100;


// =========================================================
// FOUNDATIONAL MEMORY
//
// The first conversation is intentionally exceptional.
//
// Ordinary memories can become enormously important,
// but foundational memories receive a permanent retrieval
// advantage and cannot simply disappear beneath newer data.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_BONUS =
  1000;

const RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE =
  90;


// =========================================================
// REINFORCEMENT
// =========================================================

const RECALL_MEMORY_REINFORCEMENT_MIN =
  0;

const RECALL_MEMORY_REINFORCEMENT_MAX =
  1_000_000;


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT LIMIT
//
// 3 MiB hard application-level ceiling.
//
// Later parts will implement the actual uploader and
// request validation.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES =
  3 * 1024 * 1024;


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT CHUNK TARGETS
//
// These are storage/indexing targets, NOT model context
// limits.
//
// Part 4 will perform actual chunking.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_TARGET_CHARACTERS =
  12_000;

const RECALL_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_OVERLAP_CHARACTERS =
  1_000;


// =========================================================
// MEMORY TEXT LIMITS
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARACTERS =
  4_000;

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_DETAIL_MAX_CHARACTERS =
  20_000;

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_QUOTE_MAX_CHARACTERS =
  8_000;

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_TAGS_MAX_CHARACTERS =
  4_000;


// =========================================================
// SAFE JSON STRINGIFY
// =========================================================

function recallV13JsonStringify(
  value,
  fallback =
    "{}"
) {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(
      value
    );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "V13 JSON stringify failed:",
      error
    );

    return fallback;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// SAFE JSON PARSE
// =========================================================

function recallV13JsonParse(
  value,
  fallback =
    null
) {
  if (
    typeof value !==
      "string" ||
    !value.trim()
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      value
    );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE V13 MEMORY TYPE
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallMemoryType(
  value
) {
  const candidate =
    String(
      value ||
      ""
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  const allowed =
    new Set(
      Object.values(
        RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES
      )
    );

  return allowed.has(
    candidate
  )
    ? candidate
    : RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER;
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE MEMORY IMPORTANCE
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallV13Importance(
  value,
  fallback =
    25
) {
  return clamp(
    integerNumber(
      value,
      fallback
    ),
    RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MIN,
    RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MAX
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE MEMORY CONFIDENCE
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
  value,
  fallback =
    0.75
) {
  return clamp(
    finiteNumber(
      value,
      fallback
    ),
    RECALL_MEMORY_CONFIDENCE_MIN,
    RECALL_MEMORY_CONFIDENCE_MAX
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE REINFORCEMENT
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallReinforcement(
  value,
  fallback =
    0
) {
  return clamp(
    integerNumber(
      value,
      fallback
    ),
    RECALL_MEMORY_REINFORCEMENT_MIN,
    RECALL_MEMORY_REINFORCEMENT_MAX
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE BOOLEAN DATABASE VALUE
// =========================================================

function recallV13BooleanInteger(
  value
) {
  return value
    ? 1
    : 0;
}


// =========================================================
// DATABASE TABLE COLUMN INSPECTION
// =========================================================

async function recallV13GetTableColumns(
  db,
  tableName
) {
  const safeTableName =
    String(
      tableName ||
      ""
    )
      .replace(
        /[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g,
        ""
      );

  if (
    !safeTableName
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(
        `PRAGMA table_info(${safeTableName})`
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// DATABASE COLUMN EXISTS
// =========================================================

async function recallV13ColumnExists(
  db,
  tableName,
  columnName
) {
  const columns =
    await recallV13GetTableColumns(
      db,
      tableName
    );

  return columns.some(
    (
      column
    ) =>
      String(
        column?.name ||
        ""
      ) ===
        String(
          columnName ||
          ""
        )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// ADD DATABASE COLUMN IF MISSING
//
// D1/SQLite does not provide a universally useful
// ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS form for our deployment.
//
// Inspect first, then ALTER only when required.
// =========================================================

async function recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
  db,
  tableName,
  columnName,
  definition
) {
  const exists =
    await recallV13ColumnExists(
      db,
      tableName,
      columnName
    );

  if (
    exists
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const safeTableName =
    String(
      tableName
    )
      .replace(
        /[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g,
        ""
      );

  const safeColumnName =
    String(
      columnName
    )
      .replace(
        /[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g,
        ""
      );

  if (
    !safeTableName ||
    !safeColumnName
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid V13 schema identifier."
    );
  }

  await db
    .prepare(
      `ALTER TABLE ${safeTableName} ADD COLUMN ${safeColumnName} ${definition}`
    )
    .run();

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// V13 CORE TABLE CREATION
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallV13Tables(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([

    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY V2
    //
    // Existing recall_memories remains untouched.
    //
    // V13 writes rich memories here.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memories_v2 (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        memory_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'other',

        subject_key TEXT,

        summary TEXT NOT NULL,

        detail TEXT,

        exact_quote TEXT,

        source_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'conversation',

        source_id TEXT,

        source_episode INTEGER,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.75,

        reinforcement_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        retrieval_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        contradiction_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        is_foundational INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        is_resolved INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        is_archived INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        first_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_retrieved_at TEXT,

        resolved_at TEXT,

        tags_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memories_v2_visitor_character
      ON recall_memories_v2(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        id
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memories_v2_importance
      ON recall_memories_v2(
        visitor_id,
        is_archived,
        is_foundational,
        importance,
        reinforcement_count
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memories_v2_type
      ON recall_memories_v2(
        visitor_id,
        memory_type,
        subject_key
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memories_v2_source
      ON recall_memories_v2(
        visitor_id,
        source_type,
        source_id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY RELATIONSHIPS
    //
    // Memories can point at other memories:
    //
    // reinforces
    // contradicts
    // explains
    // caused_by
    // resembles
    // resolves
    // continues
    // refers_to
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memory_links (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        from_memory_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        to_memory_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        relationship_type TEXT NOT NULL,

        strength REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,

        notes TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memory_links_from
      ON recall_memory_links(
        visitor_id,
        from_memory_id
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memory_links_to
      ON recall_memory_links(
        visitor_id,
        to_memory_id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY SUBJECTS
    //
    // Long-running concepts/persons/topics can accumulate
    // observations without every mention becoming a wholly
    // disconnected fact.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memory_subjects (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        subject_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        display_name TEXT,

        subject_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'topic',

        mention_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          visitor_id,
          subject_key
        )
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memory_subjects_visitor
      ON recall_memory_subjects(
        visitor_id,
        importance,
        last_seen_at
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // PLAYER / AUDIENCE PATTERNS
    //
    // This is where:
    //
    // "You always do this."
    //
    // eventually becomes evidence rather than improvisation.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_patterns (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_key TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'audience',

        pattern_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        description TEXT NOT NULL,

        evidence_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        counterexample_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        first_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_confirmed_at TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          visitor_id,
          character_key,
          pattern_key
        )
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_patterns_visitor
      ON recall_patterns(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        importance,
        evidence_count
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // CONTRADICTIONS
    //
    // Stores contradiction candidates separately from the
    // memories themselves.
    //
    // A contradiction can later be:
    //
    // confirmed
    // explained
    // retracted
    // unresolved
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_contradictions (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        earlier_memory_id INTEGER,

        later_memory_id INTEGER,

        earlier_claim TEXT NOT NULL,

        later_claim TEXT NOT NULL,

        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unresolved',

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,

        explanation TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        resolved_at TEXT
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_contradictions_visitor
      ON recall_contradictions(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        status,
        id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // UNRESOLVED THREADS
    //
    // Questions, promises, unfinished arguments, plans,
    // accusations, mysteries, etc.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_open_threads (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        thread_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'question',

        title TEXT NOT NULL,

        description TEXT,

        source_memory_id INTEGER,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',

        opened_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_touched_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        resolved_at TEXT,

        resolution TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_open_threads_visitor
      ON recall_open_threads(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        status,
        importance
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // NAMELESS JUDGMENTS
    //
    // Not generic facts.
    //
    // These are conclusions Nameless has formed about the
    // player, with evidence and revision history.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_nameless_judgments (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        judgment_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        judgment TEXT NOT NULL,

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        supporting_evidence_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        opposing_evidence_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        first_formed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_revised_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        supersedes_judgment_id INTEGER,

        active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_nameless_judgments_visitor
      ON recall_nameless_judgments(
        visitor_id,
        active,
        importance,
        confidence
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // NAMELESS SELF POSITIONS
    //
    // Nameless must remember his OWN arguments so the player
    // can accuse him of contradiction or hypocrisy.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_nameless_positions (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        topic_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        position_text TEXT NOT NULL,

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.75,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        source_conversation_id INTEGER,

        active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        first_stated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_stated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        retracted_at TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_nameless_positions_visitor
      ON recall_nameless_positions(
        visitor_id,
        topic_key,
        active,
        id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // FOUNDATIONAL CONVERSATION
    //
    // Exactly one first-conversation record per visitor.
    //
    // The transcript itself remains in conversation history.
    // This record makes its privileged status explicit.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_foundational_conversation (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,

        first_conversation_id INTEGER,

        last_conversation_id INTEGER,

        transcript_snapshot TEXT,

        summary TEXT,

        nameless_first_impression TEXT,

        established_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        locked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // DOCUMENTS
    //
    // Actual binary upload handling arrives in Part 4.
    //
    // This table stores document identity and extracted text
    // metadata.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_documents (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        document_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        original_filename TEXT NOT NULL,

        display_name TEXT,

        mime_type TEXT,

        byte_size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        storage_key TEXT,

        content_hash TEXT,

        extraction_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',

        indexing_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',

        extracted_character_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        is_foundational INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        deleted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        uploaded_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        indexed_at TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_documents_visitor
      ON recall_documents(
        visitor_id,
        deleted,
        uploaded_at
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // DOCUMENT CHUNKS
    //
    // Documents are never blindly dumped into every prompt.
    //
    // Relevant pieces will be retrieved later.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_document_chunks (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        document_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        chunk_index INTEGER NOT NULL,

        heading TEXT,

        content TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_start INTEGER,

        character_end INTEGER,

        token_estimate INTEGER,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        retrieval_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_retrieved_at TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          document_id,
          chunk_index
        )
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_chunks_document
      ON recall_document_chunks(
        document_id,
        chunk_index
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_chunks_visitor
      ON recall_document_chunks(
        visitor_id,
        importance,
        id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // DOCUMENT MEMORY CONNECTIONS
    //
    // Nameless can remember conclusions formed FROM a
    // document without confusing those conclusions with the
    // raw document text.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_document_memory_links (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        document_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        chunk_id INTEGER,

        memory_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        relationship_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'derived_from',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_memory_links_document
      ON recall_document_memory_links(
        visitor_id,
        document_id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY RETRIEVAL LOG
    //
    // Development observability.
    //
    // This lets us answer:
    //
    // WHY DID HE REMEMBER THAT?
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memory_retrieval_log (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        speaker_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        memory_id INTEGER,

        document_chunk_id INTEGER,

        query_text TEXT,

        retrieval_reason TEXT,

        relevance_score REAL,

        final_score REAL,

        foundational_bonus REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        reinforcement_bonus REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        recency_bonus REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        contradiction_bonus REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        selected INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memory_retrieval_log_visitor
      ON recall_memory_retrieval_log(
        visitor_id,
        id
      )
    `),


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY SYSTEM METADATA
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memory_system_state (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,

        schema_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 13,

        legacy_memories_migrated INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        foundational_memory_initialized INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_memory_extraction_turn INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_pattern_analysis_turn INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_contradiction_analysis_turn INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_document_analysis_at TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `)
  ]);
}


// =========================================================
// V13 EXTEND EXISTING CONVERSATION LOG
//
// These additions let future memory extraction distinguish
// giant user submissions, documents, interruptions and
// canonical/foundational material.
//
// Existing rows remain valid.
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallV13ConversationColumns(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_conversation_log",
    "message_kind",
    "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'dialogue'"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_conversation_log",
    "source_document_id",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_conversation_log",
    "is_foundational",
    "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_conversation_log",
    "character_count",
    "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_conversation_log",
    "metadata_json",
    "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'"
  );
}


// =========================================================
// ENSURE MEMORY SYSTEM STATE
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallMemorySystemState(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_memory_system_state (
        visitor_id,
        schema_version,
        legacy_memories_migrated,
        foundational_memory_initialized,
        last_memory_extraction_turn,
        last_pattern_analysis_turn,
        last_contradiction_analysis_turn,
        metadata_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        '{}',
        ?,
        ?
      )

      ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)
      DO UPDATE SET
        schema_version = excluded.schema_version,
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,
      now,
      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// LEGACY MEMORY MIGRATION
//
// V12 memory is NOT deleted.
//
// Each V12 durable memory is copied once into the richer
// V13 memory store.
//
// source_type + source_id prevent duplicate migration.
// =========================================================

async function migrateLegacyRecallMemoriesToV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await ensureRecallMemorySystemState(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const stateResult =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          legacy_memories_migrated

        FROM recall_memory_system_state

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  if (
    Number(
      stateResult
        ?.legacy_memories_migrated
    ) ===
      1
  ) {
    return {
      migrated:
        false,

      alreadyMigrated:
        true,

      count:
        0
    };
  }

  const legacy =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          character_key,
          summary,
          importance,
          created_at,
          updated_at

        FROM recall_memories

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY id ASC
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .all();

  const rows =
    Array.isArray(
      legacy?.results
    )
      ? legacy.results
      : [];

  let migratedCount =
    0;

  for (
    const row
    of rows
  ) {
    const legacyId =
      String(
        row.id
      );

    const alreadyExists =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT id

          FROM recall_memories_v2

          WHERE
            visitor_id = ?
            AND source_type = 'legacy_v12'
            AND source_id = ?

          LIMIT 1
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,
          legacyId
        )
        .first();

    if (
      alreadyExists
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const createdAt =
      normalizeString(
        row.created_at,
        isoNow(),
        100
      );

    const updatedAt =
      normalizeString(
        row.updated_at,
        createdAt,
        100
      );

    /*
     * V12 used a 1–10 importance scale.
     *
     * Preserve its relative meaning while translating into
     * the V13 1–100 scale.
     */
    const legacyImportance =
      clamp(
        integerNumber(
          row.importance,
          3
        ),
        1,
        10
      );

    const v13Importance =
      clamp(
        legacyImportance *
          10,
        RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MIN,
        RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_MAX
      );

    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_memories_v2 (
          visitor_id,
          character_key,
          memory_type,
          subject_key,
          summary,
          detail,
          exact_quote,
          source_type,
          source_id,
          source_episode,
          importance,
          confidence,
          reinforcement_count,
          retrieval_count,
          contradiction_count,
          is_foundational,
          is_resolved,
          is_archived,
          first_observed_at,
          last_observed_at,
          last_retrieved_at,
          resolved_at,
          tags_json,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          'other',
          NULL,
          ?,
          NULL,
          NULL,
          'legacy_v12',
          ?,
          NULL,
          ?,
          0.75,
          0,
          0,
          0,
          0,
          0,
          0,
          ?,
          ?,
          NULL,
          NULL,
          '["legacy-v12"]',
          '{"migrated":true}',
          ?,
          ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        normalizeString(
          row.character_key,
          "shared",
          100
        ),

        normalizeString(
          row.summary,
          "",
          RECALL_V13_MEMORY_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARACTERS
        ),

        legacyId,

        v13Importance,

        createdAt,

        updatedAt,

        createdAt,

        updatedAt
      )
      .run();

    migratedCount +=
      1;
  }

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memory_system_state

      SET
        legacy_memories_migrated = 1,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      isoNow(),
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    migrated:
      true,

    alreadyMigrated:
      false,

    count:
      migratedCount
  };
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE V13 MEMORY
//
// Part 2 will add intelligent extraction, merging,
// reinforcement and contradiction analysis.
//
// This is the durable primitive everything else will use.
// =========================================================

async function saveRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey =
      "shared",

    memoryType =
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,

    subjectKey =
      null,

    summary,

    detail =
      null,

    exactQuote =
      null,

    sourceType =
      "conversation",

    sourceId =
      null,

    sourceEpisode =
      null,

    importance =
      25,

    confidence =
      0.75,

    reinforcementCount =
      0,

    isFoundational =
      false,

    isResolved =
      false,

    tags =
      [],

    metadata =
      {},

    firstObservedAt =
      null,

    lastObservedAt =
      null
  } =
    {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const normalizedSummary =
    normalizeString(
      summary,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARACTERS
    )
      .trim();

  if (
    !normalizedSummary
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const foundational =
    Boolean(
      isFoundational
    );

  let normalizedImportance =
    normalizeRecallV13Importance(
      importance,
      25
    );

  if (
    foundational
  ) {
    normalizedImportance =
      Math.max(
        normalizedImportance,
        RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE
      );
  }

  const normalizedTags =
    Array.isArray(
      tags
    )
      ? tags
          .map(
            (
              tag
            ) =>
              String(
                tag
              )
                .trim()
          )
          .filter(
            Boolean
          )
          .slice(
            0,
            100
          )
      : [];

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_memories_v2 (
          visitor_id,
          character_key,
          memory_type,
          subject_key,
          summary,
          detail,
          exact_quote,
          source_type,
          source_id,
          source_episode,
          importance,
          confidence,
          reinforcement_count,
          retrieval_count,
          contradiction_count,
          is_foundational,
          is_resolved,
          is_archived,
          first_observed_at,
          last_observed_at,
          last_retrieved_at,
          resolved_at,
          tags_json,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          0,
          0,
          ?,
          ?,
          0,
          ?,
          ?,
          NULL,
          NULL,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          "shared",
          100
        ),

        normalizeRecallMemoryType(
          memoryType
        ),

        subjectKey
          ? normalizeString(
              subjectKey,
              "",
              500
            )
          : null,

        normalizedSummary,

        detail
          ? normalizeString(
              detail,
              "",
              RECALL_V13_MEMORY_DETAIL_MAX_CHARACTERS
            )
          : null,

        exactQuote
          ? normalizeString(
              exactQuote,
              "",
              RECALL_V13_MEMORY_QUOTE_MAX_CHARACTERS
            )
          : null,

        normalizeString(
          sourceType,
          "conversation",
          100
        ),

        sourceId !==
          null
          ? String(
              sourceId
            )
          : null,

        sourceEpisode !==
          null
          ? integerNumber(
              sourceEpisode,
              0
            )
          : null,

        normalizedImportance,

        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.75
        ),

        normalizeRecallReinforcement(
          reinforcementCount,
          0
        ),

        recallV13BooleanInteger(
          foundational
        ),

        recallV13BooleanInteger(
          isResolved
        ),

        firstObservedAt ||
          now,

        lastObservedAt ||
          now,

        normalizeString(
          recallV13JsonStringify(
            normalizedTags,
            "[]"
          ),
          "[]",
          RECALL_V13_MEMORY_TAGS_MAX_CHARACTERS
        ),

        recallV13JsonStringify(
          metadata,
          "{}"
        ),

        now,

        now
      )
      .run();

  const insertedId =
    result?.meta
      ?.last_row_id;

  if (
    insertedId ===
      undefined ||
    insertedId ===
      null
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_memories_v2

      WHERE id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      insertedId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// REINFORCE MEMORY
//
// Seeing the same important behavior repeatedly should make
// the memory stronger rather than creating infinite copies.
// =========================================================

async function reinforceRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  memoryId,
  {
    importanceDelta =
      1,

    confidenceDelta =
      0.02,

    observedAt =
      null
  } =
    {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const memory =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          id = ?
          AND visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        memoryId,
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  if (
    !memory
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const newImportance =
    normalizeRecallV13Importance(
      integerNumber(
        memory.importance,
        25
      ) +
        integerNumber(
          importanceDelta,
          1
        ),
      25
    );

  const newConfidence =
    normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
      finiteNumber(
        memory.confidence,
        0.75
      ) +
        finiteNumber(
          confidenceDelta,
          0.02
        ),
      0.75
    );

  const newReinforcement =
    normalizeRecallReinforcement(
      integerNumber(
        memory.reinforcement_count,
        0
      ) +
        1,
      1
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memories_v2

      SET
        importance = ?,
        confidence = ?,
        reinforcement_count = ?,
        last_observed_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      newImportance,
      newConfidence,
      newReinforcement,
      observedAt ||
        now,
      now,
      memoryId,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_memories_v2

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      memoryId,
      visitorId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// LINK TWO MEMORIES
// =========================================================

async function linkRecallMemoriesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  fromMemoryId,
  toMemoryId,
  relationshipType,
  {
    strength =
      0.5,

    notes =
      null
  } =
    {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_memory_links (
        visitor_id,
        from_memory_id,
        to_memory_id,
        relationship_type,
        strength,
        notes,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      integerNumber(
        fromMemoryId,
        0
      ),

      integerNumber(
        toMemoryId,
        0
      ),

      normalizeString(
        relationshipType,
        "related",
        100
      ),

      clamp(
        finiteNumber(
          strength,
          0.5
        ),
        0,
        1
      ),

      notes
        ? normalizeString(
            notes,
            "",
            4000
          )
        : null,

      now,

      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// CREATE / UPDATE MEMORY SUBJECT
// =========================================================

async function touchRecallMemorySubjectV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    subjectKey,

    displayName =
      null,

    subjectType =
      "topic",

    importance =
      25,

    metadata =
      {}
  }
) {
  const normalizedKey =
    normalizeString(
      subjectKey,
      "",
      500
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (
    !normalizedKey
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_memory_subjects (
        visitor_id,
        subject_key,
        display_name,
        subject_type,
        mention_count,
        importance,
        first_seen_at,
        last_seen_at,
        metadata_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        1,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?
      )

      ON CONFLICT(
        visitor_id,
        subject_key
      )

      DO UPDATE SET
        mention_count =
          recall_memory_subjects.mention_count + 1,

        importance =
          MAX(
            recall_memory_subjects.importance,
            excluded.importance
          ),

        display_name =
          COALESCE(
            excluded.display_name,
            recall_memory_subjects.display_name
          ),

        subject_type =
          excluded.subject_type,

        last_seen_at =
          excluded.last_seen_at,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      normalizedKey,

      displayName
        ? normalizeString(
            displayName,
            "",
            500
          )
        : null,

      normalizeString(
        subjectType,
        "topic",
        100
      ),

      normalizeRecallV13Importance(
        importance,
        25
      ),

      now,

      now,

      recallV13JsonStringify(
        metadata,
        "{}"
      ),

      now,

      now
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_memory_subjects

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND subject_key = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      normalizedKey
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// FOUNDATIONAL CONVERSATION EXISTS
// =========================================================

async function hasRecallFoundationalConversation(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const row =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT visitor_id

        FROM recall_foundational_conversation

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  return Boolean(
    row
  );
}


// =========================================================
// ESTABLISH FOUNDATIONAL CONVERSATION
//
// This is intentionally explicit.
//
// We DO NOT automatically decide that some random pre-update
// exchange is the sacred first conversation.
//
// Later, when THE LETTER / original transcript is supplied,
// Part 3/4 can deliberately call this.
//
// Calling it twice will update metadata but will not silently
// replace the locked transcript unless forceReplace is true.
// =========================================================

async function establishRecallFoundationalConversation(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    firstConversationId =
      null,

    lastConversationId =
      null,

    transcriptSnapshot =
      "",

    summary =
      "",

    namelessFirstImpression =
      "",

    metadata =
      {},

    forceReplace =
      false
  } =
    {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_foundational_conversation

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  if (
    existing &&
    Number(
      existing.locked
    ) ===
      1 &&
    !forceReplace
  ) {
    return existing;
  }

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const transcript =
    normalizeString(
      transcriptSnapshot,
      "",
      1_500_000
    );

  const normalizedSummary =
    normalizeString(
      summary,
      "",
      50_000
    );

  const firstImpression =
    normalizeString(
      namelessFirstImpression,
      "",
      50_000
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_foundational_conversation (
        visitor_id,
        first_conversation_id,
        last_conversation_id,
        transcript_snapshot,
        summary,
        nameless_first_impression,
        established_at,
        locked,
        metadata_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        1,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?
      )

      ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)

      DO UPDATE SET
        first_conversation_id =
          excluded.first_conversation_id,

        last_conversation_id =
          excluded.last_conversation_id,

        transcript_snapshot =
          excluded.transcript_snapshot,

        summary =
          excluded.summary,

        nameless_first_impression =
          excluded.nameless_first_impression,

        established_at =
          excluded.established_at,

        locked =
          1,

        metadata_json =
          excluded.metadata_json,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      firstConversationId !==
        null
        ? integerNumber(
            firstConversationId,
            0
          )
        : null,

      lastConversationId !==
        null
        ? integerNumber(
            lastConversationId,
            0
          )
        : null,

      transcript,

      normalizedSummary,

      firstImpression,

      now,

      recallV13JsonStringify(
        metadata,
        "{}"
      ),

      now,

      now
    )
    .run();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memory_system_state

      SET
        foundational_memory_initialized = 1,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      now,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_foundational_conversation

      WHERE visitor_id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE FOUNDATIONAL MEMORY
//
// Separate helper so foundational weighting can never be
// accidentally omitted by the later extraction system.
// =========================================================

async function saveFoundationalRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  memory
) {
  return saveRecallMemoryV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      ...memory,

      isFoundational:
        true,

      importance:
        Math.max(
          normalizeRecallV13Importance(
            memory
              ?.importance,
            RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE
          ),

          RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE
        ),

      tags: [
        ...(
          Array.isArray(
            memory
              ?.tags
          )
            ? memory.tags
            : []
        ),

        "foundational"
      ]
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// MEMORY RETRIEVAL OBSERVABILITY
//
// Part 3 will use this heavily.
// =========================================================

async function logRecallMemoryRetrievalV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    speakerKey =
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    memoryId =
      null,

    documentChunkId =
      null,

    queryText =
      null,

    retrievalReason =
      null,

    relevanceScore =
      null,

    finalScore =
      null,

    foundationalBonus =
      0,

    reinforcementBonus =
      0,

    recencyBonus =
      0,

    contradictionBonus =
      0,

    selected =
      false
  } =
    {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_memory_retrieval_log (
        visitor_id,
        speaker_key,
        memory_id,
        document_chunk_id,
        query_text,
        retrieval_reason,
        relevance_score,
        final_score,
        foundational_bonus,
        reinforcement_bonus,
        recency_bonus,
        contradiction_bonus,
        selected,
        created_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      normalizeString(
        speakerKey,
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
        100
      ),

      memoryId !==
        null
        ? integerNumber(
            memoryId,
            0
          )
        : null,

      documentChunkId !==
        null
        ? integerNumber(
            documentChunkId,
            0
          )
        : null,

      queryText
        ? normalizeString(
            queryText,
            "",
            20_000
          )
        : null,

      retrievalReason
        ? normalizeString(
            retrievalReason,
            "",
            4000
          )
        : null,

      relevanceScore !==
        null
        ? finiteNumber(
            relevanceScore,
            0
          )
        : null,

      finalScore !==
        null
        ? finiteNumber(
            finalScore,
            0
          )
        : null,

      finiteNumber(
        foundationalBonus,
        0
      ),

      finiteNumber(
        reinforcementBonus,
        0
      ),

      finiteNumber(
        recencyBonus,
        0
      ),

      finiteNumber(
        contradictionBonus,
        0
      ),

      recallV13BooleanInteger(
        selected
      ),

      isoNow()
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// MARK MEMORY RETRIEVED
// =========================================================

async function markRecallMemoryRetrievedV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  memoryId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memories_v2

      SET
        retrieval_count =
          retrieval_count + 1,

        last_retrieved_at = ?,

        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      now,
      now,
      memoryId,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();
}

          AND is_foundational = 1

    result?.results

    ? result.results
    : [];
