// =========================================================
// V13 DATABASE INSTALLER
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallV13Database(
  env
) {
  await ensureRecallV13Tables(
    env
  );

  await ensureRecallV13ConversationColumns(
    env
  );
}


// =========================================================
// WRAP V12 DATABASE CREATION
//
// CRITICAL:
//
// V12 creates all existing tables first.
//
// V13 then extends them.
//
// This means:
//
// - fresh player: V12 schema -> V13 schema
// - existing player: existing V12 schema -> V13 additions
// - repeated request: safe/idempotent
//
// No existing table is dropped.
// =========================================================

const ensureRecallDatabaseV12 =
  ensureRecallDatabase;


ensureRecallDatabase =
  async function (
    env
  ) {
    await ensureRecallDatabaseV12(
      env
    );

    await ensureRecallV13Database(
      env
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// V13 VISITOR MEMORY INITIALIZATION
//
// Later parts call this before using advanced memory.
//
// It deliberately does NOT establish the foundational
// conversation automatically.
// =========================================================

async function initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await ensureRecallDatabase(
    env
  );

  await ensureRecallMemorySystemState(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const migration =
    await migrateLegacyRecallMemoriesToV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return {
    version:
      RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

    legacyMigration:
      migration,

    foundationalConversationExists:
      await hasRecallFoundationalConversation(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// V13 MEMORY SYSTEM STATUS
//
// Development utility.
//
// This is intentionally server-side only for now.
// =========================================================

async function getRecallMemoryV13Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const [
    systemState,
    memoryCount,
    foundationalCount,
    documentCount,
    patternCount,
    contradictionCount,
    judgmentCount,
    positionCount,
    openThreadCount
  ] =
    await Promise.all([

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_memory_system_state

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_foundational = 1
          AND is_archived = 0
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_documents

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND deleted = 0
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_patterns

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_contradictions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'unresolved'
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_nameless_judgments

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND active = 1
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_nameless_positions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND active = 1
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_open_threads

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'open'
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first()
    ]);

  return {
    version:
      RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

    systemState:
      systemState ||
      null,

    memories:
      integerNumber(
        memoryCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    foundationalMemories:
      integerNumber(
        foundationalCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    documents:
      integerNumber(
        documentCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    patterns:
      integerNumber(
        patternCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    unresolvedContradictions:
      integerNumber(
        contradictionCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    activeJudgments:
      integerNumber(
        judgmentCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    activeNamelessPositions:
      integerNumber(
        positionCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    openThreads:
      integerNumber(
        openThreadCount
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    foundationalConversation:
      await hasRecallFoundationalConversation(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// END RECALL THY NAME V13 — PART 1 OF 8
//
// INSTALLED:
//
// - non-destructive V12 -> V13 database wrapper
// - rich memory store
// - memory types
// - confidence
// - reinforcement
// - foundational memory architecture
// - memory-to-memory relationships
// - subject tracking
// - behavioral pattern storage
// - contradiction storage
// - unresolved thread storage
// - Nameless judgments
// - Nameless self-position history
// - foundational conversation storage
// - persistent document metadata
// - persistent document chunks
// - document-to-memory relationships
// - retrieval observability
// - legacy V12 memory migration
// - server-side V13 status utility
//
// NO EXISTING PLAYER MEMORY IS DELETED.
//
// PART 2:
// INTELLIGENT MEMORY EXTRACTION + MERGING + REINFORCEMENT +
// PATTERN DETECTION + CONTRADICTION DETECTION +
// NAMELESS JUDGMENT EVOLUTION
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 2 OF 8
//
// INTELLIGENT MEMORY EXTRACTION
// MEMORY MERGING
// REINFORCEMENT
// PATTERN DETECTION
// CONTRADICTION DETECTION
// NAMELESS JUDGMENT EVOLUTION
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART DIRECTLY AFTER V13 PART 1.
//
// Part 1 established the rich V13 memory tables and primitives.
// Part 2 turns them into an active memory system.
//
// V12's existing callRecallModel() is deliberately reused.
// V12's existing memory system remains intact.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_EXTRACTION_INTERVAL_TURNS =
  typeof MEMORY_SUMMARY_INTERVAL_TURNS === "number"
    ? MEMORY_SUMMARY_INTERVAL_TURNS
    : 6;

const RECALL_V13_EXTRACTION_TRANSCRIPT_TURNS =
  32;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_EXTRACTIONS_PER_PASS =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_PATTERNS_PER_PASS =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTRADICTIONS_PER_PASS =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_JUDGMENTS_PER_PASS =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_POSITIONS_PER_PASS =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_MERGE_THRESHOLD =
  0.72;

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_RELATED_THRESHOLD =
  0.42;

const RECALL_V13_PATTERN_MIN_EVIDENCE =
  2;


// =========================================================
// SAFE MODEL JSON EXTRACTION
// =========================================================

function recallV13ExtractJsonText(
  value
) {
  let text =
    String(
      value ||
      ""
    ).trim();

  if (!text) {
    return "";
  }

  text =
    text
      .replace(
        /^```(?:json)?\s*/i,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /\s*```$/i,
        ""
      )
      .trim();

  const objectStart =
    text.indexOf("{");

  const arrayStart =
    text.indexOf("[");

  let start =
    -1;

  if (
    objectStart >= 0 &&
    arrayStart >= 0
  ) {
    start =
      Math.min(
        objectStart,
        arrayStart
      );
  } else if (
    objectStart >= 0
  ) {
    start =
      objectStart;
  } else if (
    arrayStart >= 0
  ) {
    start =
      arrayStart;
  }

  if (start < 0) {
    return text;
  }

  const endCharacter =
    text[start] === "["
      ? "]"
      : "}";

  const end =
    text.lastIndexOf(
      endCharacter
    );

  if (end < start) {
    return text;
  }

  return text
    .slice(
      start,
      end + 1
    )
    .trim();
}


function recallV13ParseModelJson(
  value,
  fallback = null
) {
  const extracted =
    recallV13ExtractJsonText(
      value
    );

  if (!extracted) {
    return fallback;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      extracted
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "V13 model JSON parse failed:",
      error
    );

    return fallback;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// STABLE KEY NORMALIZATION
// =========================================================

function recallV13NormalizeKey(
  value,
  fallback = "general"
) {
  const normalized =
    String(
      value ||
      fallback
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /['’]/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /[^a-z0-9]+/g,
        "-"
      )
      .replace(
        /^-+|-+$/g,
        ""
      )
      .slice(
        0,
        240
      );

  return normalized ||
    fallback;
}


// =========================================================
// CHEAP LOCAL TEXT SIMILARITY
//
// The model performs semantic analysis.
// This local layer mainly prevents obvious duplicates.
// =========================================================

function recallV13TokenSet(
  value
) {
  const stopWords =
    new Set([
      "a",
      "an",
      "and",
      "are",
      "as",
      "at",
      "be",
      "been",
      "but",
      "by",
      "for",
      "from",
      "had",
      "has",
      "have",
      "he",
      "her",
      "him",
      "his",
      "i",
      "in",
      "is",
      "it",
      "its",
      "me",
      "my",
      "of",
      "on",
      "or",
      "she",
      "that",
      "the",
      "their",
      "them",
      "they",
      "this",
      "to",
      "was",
      "were",
      "with",
      "you",
      "your"
    ]);

  return new Set(
    String(
      value ||
      ""
    )
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /[^a-z0-9\s'-]/g,
        " "
      )
      .split(
        /\s+/
      )
      .map(
        (token) =>
          token.trim()
      )
      .filter(
        (token) =>
          token.length >= 3 &&
          !stopWords.has(
            token
          )
      )
  );
}


function recallV13TextSimilarity(
  left,
  right
) {
  const a =
    recallV13TokenSet(
      left
    );

  const b =
    recallV13TokenSet(
      right
    );

  if (
    a.size === 0 ||
    b.size === 0
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  let intersection =
    0;

  for (const token of a) {
    if (b.has(token)) {
      intersection += 1;
    }
  }

  const union =
    new Set([
      ...a,
      ...b
    ]).size;

  return union > 0
    ? intersection / union
    : 0;
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD POSSIBLE MEMORY MATCHES
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallMemoryMergeCandidatesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey = "shared",
    memoryType = RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,
    subjectKey = null,
    limit = 40
  } = {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const normalizedType =
    normalizeRecallMemoryType(
      memoryType
    );

  const normalizedSubject =
    subjectKey
      ? recallV13NormalizeKey(
          subjectKey
        )
      : null;

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
          AND (
            character_key = ?
            OR character_key = 'shared'
            OR ? = 'shared'
          )
          AND (
            memory_type = ?
            OR memory_type = 'other'
            OR ? = 'other'
          )
          AND (
            ? IS NULL
            OR subject_key = ?
          )

        ORDER BY
          is_foundational DESC,
          importance DESC,
          reinforcement_count DESC,
          last_observed_at DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          "shared",
          100
        ),
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          "shared",
          100
        ),
        normalizedType,
        normalizedType,
        normalizedSubject,
        normalizedSubject,
        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            40
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// FIND BEST EXISTING MEMORY
// =========================================================

async function findBestRecallMemoryMatchV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  candidate
) {
  const rows =
    await loadRecallMemoryMergeCandidatesV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        characterKey:
          candidate?.characterKey ||
          "shared",

        memoryType:
          candidate?.memoryType ||
          RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,

        subjectKey:
          candidate?.subjectKey ||
          null,

        limit:
          50
      }
    );

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = 0;

  const candidateText =
    [
      candidate?.summary,
      candidate?.detail,
      candidate?.exactQuote
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" ");

  for (const row of rows) {
    const rowText =
      [
        row.summary,
        row.detail,
        row.exact_quote
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ");

    let score =
      recallV13TextSimilarity(
        candidateText,
        rowText
      );

    if (
      candidate?.subjectKey &&
      row.subject_key &&
      recallV13NormalizeKey(
        candidate.subjectKey
      ) ===
        recallV13NormalizeKey(
          row.subject_key
        )
    ) {
      score += 0.12;
    }

    if (
      normalizeRecallMemoryType(
        candidate?.memoryType
      ) ===
        normalizeRecallMemoryType(
          row.memory_type
        )
    ) {
      score += 0.08;
    }

    score =
      clamp(
        score,
        0,
        1
      );

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = row;
    }
  }

  return {
    memory: best,
    score: bestScore
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MERGE INTO EXISTING MEMORY
// =========================================================

async function mergeIntoRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  existing,
  incoming
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const incomingImportance =
    normalizeRecallV13Importance(
      incoming?.importance,
      25
    );

  const oldImportance =
    normalizeRecallV13Importance(
      existing?.importance,
      25
    );

  const mergedImportance =
    Math.max(
      oldImportance,
      incomingImportance
    );

  const mergedConfidence =
    normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
      Math.max(
        finiteNumber(
          existing?.confidence,
          0.75
        ),
        finiteNumber(
          incoming?.confidence,
          0.75
        )
      ) + 0.02,
      0.75
    );

  const oldTags =
    recallV13JsonParse(
      existing?.tags_json,
      []
    );

  const incomingTags =
    Array.isArray(
      incoming?.tags
    )
      ? incoming.tags
      : [];

  const mergedTags =
    [
      ...new Set([
        ...(
          Array.isArray(
            oldTags
          )
            ? oldTags
            : []
        ),
        ...incomingTags
      ])
    ].slice(
      0,
      100
    );

  const existingDetail =
    normalizeString(
      existing?.detail,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_DETAIL_MAX_CHARACTERS
    );

  const incomingDetail =
    normalizeString(
      incoming?.detail,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_DETAIL_MAX_CHARACTERS
    );

  const mergedDetail =
    incomingDetail.length >
      existingDetail.length
      ? incomingDetail
      : existingDetail;

  const mergedQuote =
    normalizeString(
      existing?.exact_quote,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_QUOTE_MAX_CHARACTERS
    ) ||
    normalizeString(
      incoming?.exactQuote,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_QUOTE_MAX_CHARACTERS
    ) ||
    null;

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memories_v2

      SET
        importance = ?,
        confidence = ?,
        reinforcement_count =
          reinforcement_count + 1,
        detail = ?,
        exact_quote = ?,
        last_observed_at = ?,
        tags_json = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      mergedImportance,
      mergedConfidence,
      mergedDetail || null,
      mergedQuote,
      incoming?.observedAt || now,
      recallV13JsonStringify(
        mergedTags,
        "[]"
      ),
      now,
      existing.id,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_memories_v2

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      existing.id,
      visitorId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE OR REINFORCE
// =========================================================

async function saveOrReinforceRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  incoming
) {
  const normalizedIncoming = {
    characterKey:
      normalizeString(
        incoming?.characterKey,
        "shared",
        100
      ),

    memoryType:
      normalizeRecallMemoryType(
        incoming?.memoryType
      ),

    subjectKey:
      incoming?.subjectKey
        ? recallV13NormalizeKey(
            incoming.subjectKey
          )
        : null,

    summary:
      normalizeString(
        incoming?.summary,
        "",
        RECALL_V13_MEMORY_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARACTERS
      ).trim(),

    detail:
      incoming?.detail ||
      null,

    exactQuote:
      incoming?.exactQuote ||
      null,

    sourceType:
      incoming?.sourceType ||
      "conversation",

    sourceId:
      incoming?.sourceId ??
      null,

    sourceEpisode:
      incoming?.sourceEpisode ??
      null,

    importance:
      normalizeRecallV13Importance(
        incoming?.importance,
        25
      ),

    confidence:
      normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
        incoming?.confidence,
        0.75
      ),

    isFoundational:
      Boolean(
        incoming?.isFoundational
      ),

    isResolved:
      Boolean(
        incoming?.isResolved
      ),

    tags:
      Array.isArray(
        incoming?.tags
      )
        ? incoming.tags
        : [],

    metadata:
      incoming?.metadata &&
      typeof incoming.metadata ===
        "object"
        ? incoming.metadata
        : {},

    observedAt:
      incoming?.observedAt ||
      isoNow()
  };

  if (!normalizedIncoming.summary) {
    return {
      action: "ignored",
      memory: null,
      matchScore: 0
    };
  }

  if (normalizedIncoming.subjectKey) {
    await touchRecallMemorySubjectV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        subjectKey:
          normalizedIncoming.subjectKey,

        displayName:
          incoming?.subjectDisplayName ||
          incoming?.subjectKey ||
          null,

        subjectType:
          incoming?.subjectType ||
          "topic",

        importance:
          normalizedIncoming.importance
      }
    );
  }

  const match =
    await findBestRecallMemoryMatchV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      normalizedIncoming
    );

  if (
    match.memory &&
    match.score >=
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_MERGE_THRESHOLD
  ) {
    const memory =
      await mergeIntoRecallMemoryV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        match.memory,
        normalizedIncoming
      );

    return {
      action: "reinforced",
      memory,
      matchScore: match.score
    };
  }

  const memory =
    normalizedIncoming.isFoundational
      ? await saveFoundationalRecallMemoryV13(
          env,
          visitorId,
          normalizedIncoming
        )
      : await saveRecallMemoryV13(
          env,
          visitorId,
          normalizedIncoming
        );

  if (
    memory &&
    match.memory &&
    match.score >=
      RECALL_V13_MEMORY_RELATED_THRESHOLD
  ) {
    await linkRecallMemoriesV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      memory.id,
      match.memory.id,
      "resembles",
      {
        strength:
          match.score,

        notes:
          "Automatically linked during V13 extraction."
      }
    );
  }

  return {
    action: "created",
    memory,
    matchScore: match.score
  };
}


// =========================================================
// UPSERT PLAYER PATTERN
// =========================================================

async function upsertRecallPatternV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey = CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
    patternKey,
    description,
    evidenceDelta = 1,
    counterexampleDelta = 0,
    confidence = 0.5,
    importance = 25,
    metadata = {}
  } = {}
) {
  const normalizedKey =
    recallV13NormalizeKey(
      patternKey ||
      description ||
      "pattern"
    );

  const normalizedDescription =
    normalizeString(
      description,
      "",
      8000
    ).trim();

  if (!normalizedDescription) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_patterns (
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        pattern_key,
        description,
        evidence_count,
        counterexample_count,
        confidence,
        importance,
        first_observed_at,
        last_observed_at,
        last_confirmed_at,
        metadata_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?,
        ?
      )

      ON CONFLICT(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        pattern_key
      )

      DO UPDATE SET
        description =
          excluded.description,

        evidence_count =
          recall_patterns.evidence_count +
          excluded.evidence_count,

        counterexample_count =
          recall_patterns.counterexample_count +
          excluded.counterexample_count,

        confidence =
          MAX(
            recall_patterns.confidence,
            excluded.confidence
          ),

        importance =
          MAX(
            recall_patterns.importance,
            excluded.importance
          ),

        last_observed_at =
          excluded.last_observed_at,

        last_confirmed_at =
          CASE
            WHEN excluded.evidence_count > 0
              THEN excluded.last_observed_at
            ELSE recall_patterns.last_confirmed_at
          END,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      normalizeString(
        characterKey,
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
        100
      ),
      normalizedKey,
      normalizedDescription,
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          evidenceDelta,
          1
        )
      ),
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          counterexampleDelta,
          0
        )
      ),
      normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
        confidence,
        0.5
      ),
      normalizeRecallV13Importance(
        importance,
        25
      ),
      now,
      now,
      evidenceDelta > 0
        ? now
        : null,
      recallV13JsonStringify(
        metadata,
        "{}"
      ),
      now,
      now
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_patterns

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND character_key = ?
        AND pattern_key = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      normalizeString(
        characterKey,
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
        100
      ),
      normalizedKey
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// RECORD CONTRADICTION
// =========================================================

async function recordRecallContradictionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey = CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
    earlierMemoryId = null,
    laterMemoryId = null,
    earlierClaim,
    laterClaim,
    confidence = 0.5,
    explanation = null
  } = {}
) {
  const earlier =
    normalizeString(
      earlierClaim,
      "",
      12000
    ).trim();

  const later =
    normalizeString(
      laterClaim,
      "",
      12000
    ).trim();

  if (
    !earlier ||
    !later
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const duplicate =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_contradictions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND character_key = ?
          AND status = 'unresolved'
          AND earlier_claim = ?
          AND later_claim = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
          100
        ),
        earlier,
        later
      )
      .first();

  if (duplicate) {
    return duplicate.id;
  }

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_contradictions (
          visitor_id,
          character_key,
          earlier_memory_id,
          later_memory_id,
          earlier_claim,
          later_claim,
          status,
          confidence,
          explanation,
          created_at,
          updated_at,
          resolved_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          'unresolved',
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          NULL
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,
          100
        ),
        earlierMemoryId !== null
          ? integerNumber(
              earlierMemoryId,
              0
            )
          : null,
        laterMemoryId !== null
          ? integerNumber(
              laterMemoryId,
              0
            )
          : null,
        earlier,
        later,
        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.5
        ),
        explanation
          ? normalizeString(
              explanation,
              "",
              12000
            )
          : null,
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  return result?.meta
    ?.last_row_id ??
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// EVOLVE NAMELESS JUDGMENT
// =========================================================

async function evolveNamelessJudgmentV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    judgmentKey,
    judgment,
    confidence = 0.5,
    importance = 25,
    supportingEvidenceDelta = 1,
    opposingEvidenceDelta = 0,
    metadata = {}
  } = {}
) {
  const key =
    recallV13NormalizeKey(
      judgmentKey ||
      judgment ||
      "judgment"
    );

  const text =
    normalizeString(
      judgment,
      "",
      12000
    ).trim();

  if (!text) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_nameless_judgments

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND judgment_key = ?
          AND active = 1

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        key
      )
      .first();

  const now =
    isoNow();

  if (!existing) {
    const result =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          INSERT INTO recall_nameless_judgments (
            visitor_id,
            judgment_key,
            judgment,
            confidence,
            importance,
            supporting_evidence_count,
            opposing_evidence_count,
            first_formed_at,
            last_revised_at,
            supersedes_judgment_id,
            active,
            metadata_json,
            created_at,
            updated_at
          )

          VALUES (
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?,
            NULL,
            1,
            ?,
            ?,
            ?
          )
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,
          key,
          text,
          normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
            confidence,
            0.5
          ),
          normalizeRecallV13Importance(
            importance,
            25
          ),
          Math.max(
            0,
            integerNumber(
              supportingEvidenceDelta,
              1
            )
          ),
          Math.max(
            0,
            integerNumber(
              opposingEvidenceDelta,
              0
            )
          ),
          now,
          now,
          recallV13JsonStringify(
            metadata,
            "{}"
          ),
          now,
          now
        )
        .run();

    return result?.meta
      ?.last_row_id ??
      null;
  }

  const sameJudgment =
    recallV13TextSimilarity(
      existing.judgment,
      text
    ) >= 0.6;

  if (sameJudgment) {
    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_nameless_judgments

        SET
          judgment = ?,

          confidence =
            MAX(
              confidence,
              ?
            ),

          importance =
            MAX(
              importance,
              ?
            ),

          supporting_evidence_count =
            supporting_evidence_count + ?,

          opposing_evidence_count =
            opposing_evidence_count + ?,

          last_revised_at = ?,

          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        text,
        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.5
        ),
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            supportingEvidenceDelta,
            1
          )
        ),
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            opposingEvidenceDelta,
            0
          )
        ),
        now,
        now,
        existing.id
      )
      .run();

    return existing.id;
  }

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_nameless_judgments

      SET
        active = 0,
        last_revised_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      now,
      now,
      existing.id
    )
    .run();

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_nameless_judgments (
          visitor_id,
          judgment_key,
          judgment,
          confidence,
          importance,
          supporting_evidence_count,
          opposing_evidence_count,
          first_formed_at,
          last_revised_at,
          supersedes_judgment_id,
          active,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          1,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        key,
        text,
        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.5
        ),
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            supportingEvidenceDelta,
            1
          )
        ),
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            opposingEvidenceDelta,
            0
          )
        ),
        existing.first_formed_at ||
          now,
        now,
        existing.id,
        recallV13JsonStringify(
          {
            ...metadata,
            revised: true
          },
          "{}"
        ),
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  return result?.meta
    ?.last_row_id ??
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// NAMELESS SELF-POSITION
// =========================================================

async function saveNamelessPositionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    topicKey,
    positionText,
    confidence = 0.75,
    importance = 25,
    sourceConversationId = null,
    metadata = {}
  } = {}
) {
  const key =
    recallV13NormalizeKey(
      topicKey ||
      positionText ||
      "general"
    );

  const text =
    normalizeString(
      positionText,
      "",
      16000
    ).trim();

  if (!text) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_nameless_positions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND topic_key = ?
          AND active = 1

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        key
      )
      .first();

  const now =
    isoNow();

  if (
    existing &&
    recallV13TextSimilarity(
      existing.position_text,
      text
    ) >= 0.65
  ) {
    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_nameless_positions

        SET
          position_text = ?,

          confidence =
            MAX(
              confidence,
              ?
            ),

          importance =
            MAX(
              importance,
              ?
            ),

          last_stated_at = ?,

          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        text,
        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.75
        ),
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        now,
        now,
        existing.id
      )
      .run();

    return existing.id;
  }

  if (existing) {
    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_nameless_positions

        SET
          active = 0,
          retracted_at = ?,
          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        now,
        now,
        existing.id
      )
      .run();
  }

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_nameless_positions (
          visitor_id,
          topic_key,
          position_text,
          confidence,
          importance,
          source_conversation_id,
          active,
          first_stated_at,
          last_stated_at,
          retracted_at,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          1,
          ?,
          ?,
          NULL,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        key,
        text,
        normalizeRecallMemoryConfidence(
          confidence,
          0.75
        ),
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        sourceConversationId !== null
          ? integerNumber(
              sourceConversationId,
              0
            )
          : null,
        existing?.first_stated_at ||
          now,
        now,
        recallV13JsonStringify(
          metadata,
          "{}"
        ),
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  return result?.meta
    ?.last_row_id ??
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD EXISTING STATE FOR MEMORY ANALYST
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallV13AnalysisContext(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const [
    memoriesResult,
    patternsResult,
    contradictionsResult,
    judgments,
    positions
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          character_key,
          memory_type,
          subject_key,
          summary,
          importance,
          confidence,
          reinforcement_count,
          is_foundational,
          last_observed_at

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0

        ORDER BY
          is_foundational DESC,
          importance DESC,
          reinforcement_count DESC,
          last_observed_at DESC

        LIMIT 80
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .all(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          pattern_key,
          description,
          evidence_count,
          counterexample_count,
          confidence,
          importance

        FROM recall_patterns

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          importance DESC,
          evidence_count DESC

        LIMIT 30
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .all(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          character_key,
          earlier_claim,
          later_claim,
          confidence,
          explanation

        FROM recall_contradictions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'unresolved'

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 20
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .all(),

      loadNamelessJudgmentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        30
      ),

      loadNamelessPositionsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        30
      )
    ]);

  return {
    memories:
      Array.isArray(
        memoriesResult?.results
      )
        ? memoriesResult.results
        : [],

    patterns:
      Array.isArray(
        patternsResult?.results
      )
        ? patternsResult.results
        : [],

    contradictions:
      Array.isArray(
        contradictionsResult?.results
      )
        ? contradictionsResult.results
        : [],

    judgments,
    positions
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MEMORY ANALYST PROMPT
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_ANALYST_PROMPT = `
You are the private continuity and memory analyst for the fictional long-running television program "recall thy name."

You are NOT a character in the show.
You never speak directly to the audience.

Your job is not to summarize everything.

Your job is to decide what should still matter weeks, months, or years later.

RULES:

1. Preserve concrete facts, events, promises, preferences, recurring arguments, emotionally important moments, names, relationships, unusual jokes, accusations, discoveries, plans, injuries, deaths, betrayals, reconciliations, unresolved questions, and changes in belief.

2. Distinguish what the player explicitly said from what Nameless inferred.

3. Never convert an inference into objective fact.

4. Repetition matters. If something already exists in memory and occurs again, describe the same underlying idea consistently so the engine can reinforce it instead of producing endless duplicates.

5. Contradictions matter, but do not manufacture them from nuance, jokes, sarcasm, changed circumstances, or legitimate changes of mind.

6. Patterns require evidence. A single occurrence is normally not a pattern.

7. Nameless judgments are subjective conclusions about the player. They may evolve as evidence accumulates.

8. Nameless must remember his own substantive arguments and positions.

9. Exact quotations are optional. Never fabricate exact wording.

10. Do not save filler, greetings, routine show mechanics, or every sentence.

11. Do not mark ordinary memories foundational.

12. Output JSON only.

RETURN EXACTLY:

{
  "memories": [
    {
      "character_key": "audience|nameless-man|host|show|shared",
      "memory_type": "fact|event|quote|belief|opinion|preference|promise|dispute|question|pattern|judgment|relationship|contradiction|self_position|document|document_insight|name_event|combat|show_event|other",
      "subject_key": "stable-topic-key-or-null",
      "subject_display_name": "optional readable subject",
      "subject_type": "person|topic|event|project|relationship|place|object|other",
      "summary": "concise durable memory",
      "detail": "optional detail",
      "exact_quote": null,
      "importance": 25,
      "confidence": 0.75,
      "tags": []
    }
  ],

  "patterns": [
    {
      "pattern_key": "stable-pattern-key",
      "description": "recurring behavior",
      "evidence_delta": 1,
      "counterexample_delta": 0,
      "confidence": 0.5,
      "importance": 25
    }
  ],

  "contradictions": [
    {
      "earlier_memory_id": null,
      "later_memory_id": null,
      "earlier_claim": "earlier claim",
      "later_claim": "later conflicting claim",
      "confidence": 0.5,
      "explanation": "why they conflict"
    }
  ],

  "judgments": [
    {
      "judgment_key": "stable-judgment-key",
      "judgment": "Nameless's evidence-grounded interpretation",
      "confidence": 0.5,
      "importance": 25,
      "supporting_evidence_delta": 1,
      "opposing_evidence_delta": 0
    }
  ],

  "nameless_positions": [
    {
      "topic_key": "stable-topic-key",
      "position_text": "Nameless's substantive position",
      "confidence": 0.75,
      "importance": 25,
      "source_conversation_id": null
    }
  ]
}

IMPORTANCE:

1-20 minor
21-40 ordinary durable continuity
41-60 meaningful
61-80 major
81-95 extremely important
96-100 identity-defining or central continuity

MAXIMUM OUTPUT:

12 memories
6 patterns
6 contradictions
6 judgments
6 Nameless positions

Empty arrays are valid.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// BUILD ANALYST INPUT
// =========================================================

function buildRecallV13MemoryAnalysisInput(
  transcript,
  existingContext
) {
  return `
RECENT VERBATIM TRANSCRIPT:

${transcript}


EXISTING ADVANCED MEMORY:

${JSON.stringify(
  existingContext,
  null,
  2
)}


Analyze the new transcript against existing memory.

Prefer reinforcement and evolution over needless duplication.

Return JSON only.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// NORMALIZE ANALYST OUTPUT
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallV13AnalystResult(
  payload
) {
  const source =
    payload &&
    typeof payload === "object"
      ? payload
      : {};

  return {
    memories:
      Array.isArray(
        source.memories
      )
        ? source.memories.slice(
            0,
            RECALL_V13_MAX_EXTRACTIONS_PER_PASS
          )
        : [],

    patterns:
      Array.isArray(
        source.patterns
      )
        ? source.patterns.slice(
            0,
            RECALL_V13_MAX_PATTERNS_PER_PASS
          )
        : [],

    contradictions:
      Array.isArray(
        source.contradictions
      )
        ? source.contradictions.slice(
            0,
            RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTRADICTIONS_PER_PASS
          )
        : [],

    judgments:
      Array.isArray(
        source.judgments
      )
        ? source.judgments.slice(
            0,
            RECALL_V13_MAX_JUDGMENTS_PER_PASS
          )
        : [],

    namelessPositions:
      Array.isArray(
        source.nameless_positions
      )
        ? source.nameless_positions.slice(
            0,
            RECALL_V13_MAX_POSITIONS_PER_PASS
          )
        : []
  };
}


// =========================================================
// APPLY ANALYSIS
// =========================================================

async function applyRecallV13AnalystResult(
  env,
  visitorId,
  analysis,
  {
    sourceEpisode = null
  } = {}
) {
  const normalized =
    normalizeRecallV13AnalystResult(
      analysis
    );

  const saved = {
    memories: [],
    patterns: [],
    contradictions: [],
    judgments: [],
    namelessPositions: []
  };

  for (
    const memory
    of normalized.memories
  ) {
    const result =
      await saveOrReinforceRecallMemoryV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          characterKey:
            memory?.character_key ||
            "shared",

          memoryType:
            memory?.memory_type ||
            RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,

          subjectKey:
            memory?.subject_key ||
            null,

          subjectDisplayName:
            memory?.subject_display_name ||
            null,

          subjectType:
            memory?.subject_type ||
            "topic",

          summary:
            memory?.summary,

          detail:
            memory?.detail ||
            null,

          exactQuote:
            memory?.exact_quote ||
            null,

          sourceType:
            "conversation_v13",

          sourceEpisode,

          importance:
            memory?.importance,

          confidence:
            memory?.confidence,

          tags:
            Array.isArray(
              memory?.tags
            )
              ? memory.tags
              : [],

          metadata: {
            extractedBy:
              "v13-memory-analyst"
          }
        }
      );

    if (result) {
      saved.memories.push(
        result
      );
    }
  }

  for (
    const pattern
    of normalized.patterns
  ) {
    const result =
      await upsertRecallPatternV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          characterKey:
            CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

          patternKey:
            pattern?.pattern_key,

          description:
            pattern?.description,

          evidenceDelta:
            pattern?.evidence_delta ??
            1,

          counterexampleDelta:
            pattern?.counterexample_delta ??
            0,

          confidence:
            pattern?.confidence,

          importance:
            pattern?.importance,

          metadata: {
            extractedBy:
              "v13-memory-analyst"
          }
        }
      );

    if (result) {
      saved.patterns.push(
        result
      );
    }
  }

  for (
    const contradiction
    of normalized.contradictions
  ) {
    const result =
      await recordRecallContradictionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          characterKey:
            CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

          earlierMemoryId:
            contradiction?.earlier_memory_id ??
            null,

          laterMemoryId:
            contradiction?.later_memory_id ??
            null,

          earlierClaim:
            contradiction?.earlier_claim,

          laterClaim:
            contradiction?.later_claim,

          confidence:
            contradiction?.confidence,

          explanation:
            contradiction?.explanation ||
            null
        }
      );

    if (result) {
      saved.contradictions.push(
        result
      );
    }
  }

  for (
    const judgment
    of normalized.judgments
  ) {
    const result =
      await evolveNamelessJudgmentV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          judgmentKey:
            judgment?.judgment_key,

          judgment:
            judgment?.judgment,

          confidence:
            judgment?.confidence,

          importance:
            judgment?.importance,

          supportingEvidenceDelta:
            judgment?.supporting_evidence_delta ??
            1,

          opposingEvidenceDelta:
            judgment?.opposing_evidence_delta ??
            0,

          metadata: {
            extractedBy:
              "v13-memory-analyst"
          }
        }
      );

    if (result) {
      saved.judgments.push(
        result
      );
    }
  }

  for (
    const position
    of normalized.namelessPositions
  ) {
    const result =
      await saveNamelessPositionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          topicKey:
            position?.topic_key,

          positionText:
            position?.position_text,

          confidence:
            position?.confidence,

          importance:
            position?.importance,

          sourceConversationId:
            position?.source_conversation_id ??
            null,

          metadata: {
            extractedBy:
              "v13-memory-analyst"
          }
        }
      );

    if (result) {
      saved.namelessPositions.push(
        result
      );
    }
  }

  return saved;
}


// =========================================================
// RUN ADVANCED MEMORY ANALYSIS
// =========================================================

async function extractAdvancedRecallMemoriesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const recent =
    await loadRecentRecallConversation(
      env,
      visitorId,
      RECALL_V13_EXTRACTION_TRANSCRIPT_TURNS
    );

  if (recent.length < 4) {
    return {
      skipped: true,
      reason: "not-enough-conversation"
    };
  }

  const transcript =
    buildRecentConversationText(
      recent
    );

  const existingContext =
    await buildRecallV13AnalysisContext(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const raw =
    await callRecallModel(
      env,
      {
        instructions:
          RECALL_V13_MEMORY_ANALYST_PROMPT,

        input:
          buildRecallV13MemoryAnalysisInput(
            transcript,
            existingContext
          ),

        webSearchEnabled:
          false
      }
    );

  const parsed =
    recallV13ParseModelJson(
      raw,
      null
    );

  if (!parsed) {
    console.error(
      "V13 memory analyst returned invalid JSON."
    );

    return {
      skipped: true,
      reason: "invalid-model-json"
    };
  }

  const sourceEpisode =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.turnNumber,
        0
      )
    );

  const applied =
    await applyRecallV13AnalystResult(
      env,
      visitorId,
      parsed,
      {
        sourceEpisode
      }
    );

  return {
    skipped: false,

    analysis:
      normalizeRecallV13AnalystResult(
        parsed
      ),

    applied
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MARK ANALYSIS TURN
// =========================================================

async function markRecallV13AnalysisTurn(
  env,
  visitorId,
  turnNumber
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const normalizedTurn =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        turnNumber,
        0
      )
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await ensureRecallMemorySystemState(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_memory_system_state

      SET
        last_memory_extraction_turn = ?,
        last_pattern_analysis_turn = ?,
        last_contradiction_analysis_turn = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      normalizedTurn,
      normalizedTurn,
      normalizedTurn,
      now,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// SHOULD ANALYSIS RUN?
// =========================================================

async function shouldRunRecallV13MemoryAnalysis(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const turnNumber =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.turnNumber,
        0
      )
    );

  if (turnNumber <= 0) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    turnNumber %
      RECALL_V13_EXTRACTION_INTERVAL_TURNS !==
        0
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await ensureRecallMemorySystemState(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const row =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          last_memory_extraction_turn

        FROM recall_memory_system_state

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  const lastTurn =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        row?.last_memory_extraction_turn,
        0
      )
    );

  return turnNumber >
    lastTurn;
}


// =========================================================
// PERIODIC ADVANCED UPDATE
// =========================================================

async function maybeExtractAdvancedRecallMemoriesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const shouldRun =
    await shouldRunRecallV13MemoryAnalysis(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  if (!shouldRun) {
    return {
      skipped: true,
      reason: "interval-or-already-processed"
    };
  }

  const turnNumber =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.turnNumber,
        0
      )
    );

  await markRecallV13AnalysisTurn(
    env,
    visitorId,
    turnNumber
  );

  try {
    return await extractAdvancedRecallMemoriesV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "V13 advanced memory extraction failed:",
      error
    );

    return {
      skipped: true,
      reason: "analysis-error",
      error:
        String(
          error?.message ||
          error
        )
    };
  }
}


// =========================================================
// V12 -> V13 MEMORY LIFECYCLE HOOK
//
// V12 already calls maybeExtractDurableMemories().
// Preserve V12, then execute V13.
// =========================================================

const maybeExtractDurableMemoriesV12 =
  maybeExtractDurableMemories;


maybeExtractDurableMemories =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  ) {
    const legacyResult =
      await maybeExtractDurableMemoriesV12(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );

    const advancedResult =
      await maybeExtractAdvancedRecallMemoriesV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );

    return {
      legacy:
        legacyResult,

      advanced:
        advancedResult
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// LOAD CONFIRMED PATTERNS
// =========================================================

async function loadConfirmedRecallPatternsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit = 20
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_patterns

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND evidence_count >= ?

        ORDER BY
          importance DESC,
          confidence DESC,
          evidence_count DESC,
          last_confirmed_at DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_PATTERN_MIN_EVIDENCE,
        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            20
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD CONTRADICTIONS
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallContradictionsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit = 20
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_contradictions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'unresolved'

        ORDER BY
          confidence DESC,
          id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            20
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// RESOLVE CONTRADICTION
// =========================================================

async function resolveRecallContradictionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  contradictionId,
  {
    status = "explained",
    explanation = null
  } = {}
) {
  const allowed =
    new Set([
      "confirmed",
      "explained",
      "retracted",
      "resolved"
    ]);

  const requested =
    String(
      status
    ).toLowerCase();

  const normalizedStatus =
    allowed.has(
      requested
    )
      ? requested
      : "explained";

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_contradictions

      SET
        status = ?,
        explanation = ?,
        resolved_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      normalizedStatus,
      explanation
        ? normalizeString(
            explanation,
            "",
            12000
          )
        : null,
      now,
      now,
      integerNumber(
        contradictionId,
        0
      ),
      visitorId
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// CREATE OPEN THREAD
// =========================================================

async function createRecallOpenThreadV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey = CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
    threadType = "question",
    title,
    description = null,
    sourceMemoryId = null,
    importance = 25,
    metadata = {}
  } = {}
) {
  const normalizedTitle =
    normalizeString(
      title,
      "",
      2000
    ).trim();

  if (!normalizedTitle) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_open_threads

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND character_key = ?
          AND status = 'open'
          AND title = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
          100
        ),
        normalizedTitle
      )
      .first();

  const now =
    isoNow();

  if (existing) {
    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_open_threads

        SET
          last_touched_at = ?,

          importance =
            MAX(
              importance,
              ?
            ),

          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        now,
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        now,
        existing.id
      )
      .run();

    return existing.id;
  }

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_open_threads (
          visitor_id,
          character_key,
          thread_type,
          title,
          description,
          source_memory_id,
          importance,
          status,
          opened_at,
          last_touched_at,
          resolved_at,
          resolution,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?,
          'open',
          ?,
          ?,
          NULL,
          NULL,
          ?,
          ?,
          ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        normalizeString(
          characterKey,
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
          100
        ),
        normalizeString(
          threadType,
          "question",
          100
        ),
        normalizedTitle,
        description
          ? normalizeString(
              description,
              "",
              12000
            )
          : null,
        sourceMemoryId !== null
          ? integerNumber(
              sourceMemoryId,
              0
            )
          : null,
        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          25
        ),
        now,
        now,
        recallV13JsonStringify(
          metadata,
          "{}"
        ),
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  return result?.meta
    ?.last_row_id ??
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// RESOLVE OPEN THREAD
// =========================================================

async function resolveRecallOpenThreadV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  threadId,
  resolution = null
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_open_threads

      SET
        status = 'resolved',
        resolved_at = ?,
        resolution = ?,
        last_touched_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        id = ?
        AND visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      now,
      resolution
        ? normalizeString(
            resolution,
            "",
            12000
          )
        : null,
      now,
      now,
      integerNumber(
        threadId,
        0
      ),
      visitorId
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// PART 2 STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallMemoryV13Part2Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const [
    core,
    patterns,
    contradictions,
    judgments,
    positions,
    threads
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      getRecallMemoryV13Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadConfirmedRecallPatternsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        100
      ),

      loadRecallContradictionsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        100
      ),

      loadNamelessJudgmentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        100
      ),

      loadNamelessPositionsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        100
      ),

      loadRecallOpenThreadsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
        100
      )
    ]);

  return {
    ...core,

    confirmedPatterns:
      patterns.length,

    unresolvedContradictions:
      contradictions.length,

    activeJudgments:
      judgments.length,

    activeNamelessPositions:
      positions.length,

    openThreads:
      threads.length,

    part2Installed:
      true
  };
}

// =========================================================
// RETRIEVAL CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT =
  180;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FINAL_LIMIT =
  28;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FOUNDATIONAL_LIMIT =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_PATTERN_LIMIT =
  8;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_JUDGMENT_LIMIT =
  8;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CONTRADICTION_LIMIT =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_POSITION_LIMIT =
  8;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_THREAD_LIMIT =
  6;

const RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_LIMIT =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_BONUS =
  0.34;

const RECALL_V13_FOUNDATIONAL_BONUS =
  0.52;

const RECALL_V13_REINFORCEMENT_BONUS_MAX =
  0.34;

const RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_WEIGHT =
  0.42;

const RECALL_V13_CONFIDENCE_WEIGHT =
  0.16;

const RECALL_V13_RECENCY_WEIGHT =
  0.14;

const RECALL_V13_SEMANTIC_WEIGHT =
  0.88;

const RECALL_V13_SUBJECT_MATCH_BONUS =
  0.30;

const RECALL_V13_TYPE_MATCH_BONUS =
  0.12;

const RECALL_V13_QUOTE_BONUS =
  0.08;

const RECALL_V13_UNRESOLVED_BONUS =
  0.15;

const RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS =
  32000;


// =========================================================
// BASIC RETRIEVAL HELPERS
// =========================================================

function recallV13BoundedNumber(
  value,
  minimum,
  maximum,
  fallback
) {
  return clamp(
    finiteNumber(
      value,
      fallback
    ),
    minimum,
    maximum
  );
}


function recallV13AgeInDays(
  isoDate
) {
  if (!isoDate) {
    return 3650;
  }

  const timestamp =
    Date.parse(
      isoDate
    );

  if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp)) {
    return 3650;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    (
      Date.now() -
      timestamp
    ) /
      86400000
  );
}


function recallV13RecencyScore(
  isoDate
) {
  const ageDays =
    recallV13AgeInDays(
      isoDate
    );

  if (ageDays <= 1) {
    return 1;
  }

  if (ageDays <= 7) {
    return 0.9;
  }

  if (ageDays <= 30) {
    return 0.75;
  }

  if (ageDays <= 90) {
    return 0.58;
  }

  if (ageDays <= 365) {
    return 0.40;
  }

  if (ageDays <= 1095) {
    return 0.24;
  }

  return 0.12;
}


function recallV13ReinforcementScore(
  count
) {
  const normalized =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        count,
        0
      )
    );

  if (normalized <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.min(
    1,
    Math.log2(
      normalized + 1
    ) / 6
  );
}


function recallV13ImportanceScore(
  importance
) {
  return recallV13BoundedNumber(
    importance,
    0,
    100,
    25
  ) / 100;
}


function recallV13ConfidenceScore(
  confidence
) {
  return recallV13BoundedNumber(
    confidence,
    0,
    1,
    0.75
  );
}


// =========================================================
// QUERY ANALYSIS
//
// This remains deliberately cheap.
//
// The full model receives the final retrieved context.
// We do not need another model call merely to identify
// obvious words, subjects and memory categories.
// =========================================================

function analyzeRecallRetrievalQueryV13(
  query
) {
  const raw =
    normalizeString(
      query,
      "",
      60000
    );

  const lower =
    raw.toLowerCase();

  const tokens =
    [
      ...recallV13TokenSet(
        raw
      )
    ];

  const subjectKeys =
    new Set();

  for (const token of tokens) {
    if (token.length >= 4) {
      subjectKeys.add(
        recallV13NormalizeKey(
          token
        )
      );
    }
  }

  const requestedTypes =
    new Set();

  if (
    /\bremember|remembered|recall|last time|before|previously|earlier\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.EVENT
    );

    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.FACT
    );
  }

  if (
    /\bsaid|told|quote|exact words|what did .* say\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.QUOTE
    );
  }

  if (
    /\bpromise|promised|swore|agreed\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.PROMISE
    );
  }

  if (
    /\bbelieve|belief|think|thought|opinion|position|argued|argument\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.BELIEF
    );

    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OPINION
    );

    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.SELF_POSITION
    );
  }

  if (
    /\blike|likes|liked|prefer|prefers|favorite|favourite|hate|hates\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.PREFERENCE
    );
  }

  if (
    /\bcontradict|contradiction|hypocrit|changed your mind|changed my mind\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.CONTRADICTION
    );
  }

  if (
    /\balways|every time|keep doing|pattern|usually|often|again\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.PATTERN
    );
  }

  if (
    /\brelationship|friend|wife|husband|partner|mother|father|son|daughter\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.RELATIONSHIP
    );
  }

  if (
    /\bdocument|manuscript|novel|book|upload|file\b/i
      .test(raw)
  ) {
    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DOCUMENT
    );

    requestedTypes.add(
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DOCUMENT_INSIGHT
    );
  }

  return {
    raw,
    lower,
    tokens,
    subjectKeys:
      [...subjectKeys],
    requestedTypes:
      [...requestedTypes]
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD RETRIEVAL CANDIDATES
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallRetrievalCandidatesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          *

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0

        ORDER BY
          is_foundational DESC,
          importance DESC,
          reinforcement_count DESC,
          last_observed_at DESC,
          id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT
          ),
          1,
          500
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// SCORE ONE MEMORY
// =========================================================

function scoreRecallMemoryV13(
  memory,
  queryAnalysis
) {
  const query =
    queryAnalysis?.raw ||
    "";

  const memoryText =
    [
      memory?.summary,
      memory?.detail,
      memory?.exact_quote,
      memory?.subject_key
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" ");

  const semanticScore =
    query
      ? recallV13TextSimilarity(
          query,
          memoryText
        )
      : 0;

  const importanceScore =
    recallV13ImportanceScore(
      memory?.importance
    );

  const confidenceScore =
    recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      memory?.confidence
    );

  const recencyScore =
    recallV13RecencyScore(
      memory?.last_observed_at ||
      memory?.updated_at ||
      memory?.created_at
    );

  const reinforcementScore =
    recallV13ReinforcementScore(
      memory?.reinforcement_count
    );

  let score =
    semanticScore *
      RECALL_V13_SEMANTIC_WEIGHT +
    importanceScore *
      RECALL_V13_IMPORTANCE_WEIGHT +
    confidenceScore *
      RECALL_V13_CONFIDENCE_WEIGHT +
    recencyScore *
      RECALL_V13_RECENCY_WEIGHT +
    reinforcementScore *
      RECALL_V13_REINFORCEMENT_BONUS_MAX;

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.is_foundational,
      0
    ) === 1
  ) {
    score +=
      RECALL_V13_FOUNDATIONAL_BONUS;
  }

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.source_episode,
      -1
    ) === 1
  ) {
    score +=
      RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_BONUS;
  }

  if (
    memory?.subject_key &&
    Array.isArray(
      queryAnalysis?.subjectKeys
    )
  ) {
    const normalizedMemorySubject =
      recallV13NormalizeKey(
        memory.subject_key
      );

    const subjectMatched =
      queryAnalysis.subjectKeys
        .some(
          (subjectKey) =>
            normalizedMemorySubject ===
              subjectKey ||
            normalizedMemorySubject
              .includes(
                subjectKey
              ) ||
            subjectKey
              .includes(
                normalizedMemorySubject
              )
        );

    if (subjectMatched) {
      score +=
        RECALL_V13_SUBJECT_MATCH_BONUS;
    }
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      queryAnalysis?.requestedTypes
    ) &&
    queryAnalysis.requestedTypes
      .includes(
        normalizeRecallMemoryType(
          memory?.memory_type
        )
      )
  ) {
    score +=
      RECALL_V13_TYPE_MATCH_BONUS;
  }

  if (memory?.exact_quote) {
    score +=
      RECALL_V13_QUOTE_BONUS;
  }

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.is_resolved,
      0
    ) === 0 &&
    [
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.QUESTION,
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DISPUTE,
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.CONTRADICTION
    ]
      .includes(
        normalizeRecallMemoryType(
          memory?.memory_type
        )
      )
  ) {
    score +=
      RECALL_V13_UNRESOLVED_BONUS;
  }

  return {
    ...memory,

    retrieval_score:
      score,

    retrieval_components: {
      semantic:
        semanticScore,

      importance:
        importanceScore,

      confidence:
        confidenceScore,

      recency:
        recencyScore,

      reinforcement:
        reinforcementScore
    }
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DIVERSIFY MEMORY RESULTS
//
// Without this, one heavily repeated topic can crowd every
// other useful memory out of the prompt.
// =========================================================

function diversifyRecallMemoriesV13(
  scored,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FINAL_LIMIT
) {
  const source =
    Array.isArray(
      scored
    )
      ? scored
      : [];

  const selected =
    [];

  const subjectCounts =
    new Map();

  const typeCounts =
    new Map();

  for (const memory of source) {
    if (
      selected.length >=
      limit
    ) {
      break;
    }

    const subject =
      memory?.subject_key ||
      "__none__";

    const type =
      memory?.memory_type ||
      "other";

    const subjectCount =
      subjectCounts.get(
        subject
      ) ||
      0;

    const typeCount =
      typeCounts.get(
        type
      ) ||
      0;

    const foundational =
      integerNumber(
        memory?.is_foundational,
        0
      ) === 1;

    if (
      !foundational &&
      subjectCount >= 5
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      !foundational &&
      typeCount >= 10
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    selected.push(
      memory
    );

    subjectCounts.set(
      subject,
      subjectCount + 1
    );

    typeCounts.set(
      type,
      typeCount + 1
    );
  }

  return selected;
}


// =========================================================
// SMART MEMORY RETRIEVAL
// =========================================================

async function retrieveRecallMemoriesV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query,
  {
    limit =
      RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FINAL_LIMIT
  } = {}
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const queryAnalysis =
    analyzeRecallRetrievalQueryV13(
      query
    );

  const candidates =
    await loadRecallRetrievalCandidatesV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CANDIDATE_LIMIT
    );

  const scored =
    candidates
      .map(
        (memory) =>
          scoreRecallMemoryV13(
            memory,
            queryAnalysis
          )
      )
      .sort(
        (left, right) =>
          right.retrieval_score -
          left.retrieval_score
      );

  return diversifyRecallMemoriesV13(
    scored,
    clamp(
      integerNumber(
        limit,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FINAL_LIMIT
      ),
      1,
      100
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FIRST-CONVERSATION PRIVILEGED RECALL
//
// The first conversation is not merely "old chat."
//
// It is formative evidence.
//
// It remains available even after years of later material,
// while still yielding to directly relevant memories.
// =========================================================

async function loadFirstConversationRecallV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_LIMIT
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
          AND source_episode = 1

        ORDER BY
          is_foundational DESC,
          importance DESC,
          reinforcement_count DESC,
          id ASC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_LIMIT
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD IMPORTANT SUBJECTS
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallSubjectsForPromptV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_memory_subjects

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          importance DESC,
          mention_count DESC,
          last_mentioned_at DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD COMPLETE LONG-TERM MEMORY PACKET
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallMemoryPacketV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const [
    relevantMemories,
    foundationalMemories,
    firstConversation,
    patterns,
    contradictions,
    judgments,
    positions,
    openThreads,
    subjects
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      retrieveRecallMemoriesV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        query,
        {
          limit:
            RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FINAL_LIMIT
        }
      ),

      loadFoundationalRecallMemoriesV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_FOUNDATIONAL_LIMIT
      ),

      loadFirstConversationRecallV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_FIRST_CONVERSATION_LIMIT
      ),

      loadConfirmedRecallPatternsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_PATTERN_LIMIT
      ),

      loadRecallContradictionsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_CONTRADICTION_LIMIT
      ),

      loadNamelessJudgmentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_JUDGMENT_LIMIT
      ),

      loadNamelessPositionsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_POSITION_LIMIT
      ),

      loadRecallOpenThreadsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_THREAD_LIMIT
      ),

      loadRecallSubjectsForPromptV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT
      )
    ]);

  return {
    relevantMemories,
    foundationalMemories,
    firstConversation,
    patterns,
    contradictions,
    judgments,
    positions,
    openThreads,
    subjects
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEDUPLICATE PROMPT MEMORIES
// =========================================================

function deduplicateRecallPromptMemoriesV13(
  groups
) {
  const seenIds =
    new Set();

  const seenText =
    new Set();

  const output =
    [];

  for (
    const group
    of groups
  ) {
    for (
      const memory
      of (
        Array.isArray(group)
          ? group
          : []
      )
    ) {
      const id =
        memory?.id !== undefined &&
        memory?.id !== null
          ? String(
              memory.id
            )
          : null;

      const textKey =
        recallV13NormalizeKey(
          memory?.summary ||
          memory?.detail ||
          memory?.exact_quote ||
          ""
        );

      if (
