        id &&
        seenIds.has(id)
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (
        textKey &&
        textKey !== "general" &&
        seenText.has(
          textKey
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (id) {
        seenIds.add(
          id
        );
      }

      if (
        textKey &&
        textKey !== "general"
      ) {
        seenText.add(
          textKey
        );
      }

      output.push(
        memory
      );
    }
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT ONE MEMORY
// =========================================================

function formatRecallMemoryForPromptV13(
  memory
) {
  const flags =
    [];

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.is_foundational,
      0
    ) === 1
  ) {
    flags.push(
      "FOUNDATIONAL"
    );
  }

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.source_episode,
      -1
    ) === 1
  ) {
    flags.push(
      "FIRST-CONVERSATION"
    );
  }

  const reinforcement =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        memory?.reinforcement_count,
        0
      )
    );

  if (reinforcement >= 2) {
    flags.push(
      `REINFORCED:${reinforcement}`
    );
  }

  if (
    integerNumber(
      memory?.is_resolved,
      0
    ) === 0 &&
    [
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.QUESTION,
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DISPUTE,
      RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.CONTRADICTION
    ]
      .includes(
        normalizeRecallMemoryType(
          memory?.memory_type
        )
      )
  ) {
    flags.push(
      "UNRESOLVED"
    );
  }

  const header =
    [
      memory?.memory_type ||
        "memory",

      memory?.subject_key ||
        null,

      flags.length
        ? flags.join(",")
        : null
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" | ");

  const body =
    normalizeString(
      memory?.summary,
      "",
      4000
    );

  const detail =
    normalizeString(
      memory?.detail,
      "",
      5000
    );

  const quote =
    normalizeString(
      memory?.exact_quote,
      "",
      3000
    );

  let output =
    `- [${header}] ${body}`;

  if (
    detail &&
    detail !== body
  ) {
    output +=
      `\n  Detail: ${detail}`;
  }

  if (quote) {
    output +=
      `\n  Exact quote: "${quote}"`;
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT PATTERNS
// =========================================================

function formatRecallPatternForPromptV13(
  pattern
) {
  return (
    `- ${normalizeString(
      pattern?.description,
      "",
      4000
    )}` +
    ` [evidence=${Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        pattern?.evidence_count,
        0
      )
    )}; counterexamples=${Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        pattern?.counterexample_count,
        0
      )
    )}; confidence=${recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      pattern?.confidence
    ).toFixed(2)}]`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT JUDGMENTS
// =========================================================

function formatRecallJudgmentForPromptV13(
  judgment
) {
  return (
    `- ${normalizeString(
      judgment?.judgment,
      "",
      5000
    )}` +
    ` [support=${Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        judgment?.supporting_evidence_count,
        0
      )
    )}; opposition=${Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        judgment?.opposing_evidence_count,
        0
      )
    )}; confidence=${recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      judgment?.confidence
    ).toFixed(2)}]`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT CONTRADICTIONS
// =========================================================

function formatRecallContradictionForPromptV13(
  contradiction
) {
  const earlier =
    normalizeString(
      contradiction?.earlier_claim,
      "",
      3500
    );

  const later =
    normalizeString(
      contradiction?.later_claim,
      "",
      3500
    );

  const explanation =
    normalizeString(
      contradiction?.explanation,
      "",
      2500
    );

  let output =
    `- Earlier: ${earlier}\n  Later: ${later}`;

  if (explanation) {
    output +=
      `\n  Analyst note: ${explanation}`;
  }

  output +=
    `\n  Confidence: ${recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      contradiction?.confidence
    ).toFixed(2)}`;

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT NAMELESS POSITIONS
// =========================================================

function formatNamelessPositionForPromptV13(
  position
) {
  return (
    `- [${normalizeString(
      position?.topic_key,
      "general",
      300
    )}] ` +
    normalizeString(
      position?.position_text,
      "",
      5000
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT OPEN THREADS
// =========================================================

function formatRecallOpenThreadForPromptV13(
  thread
) {
  const title =
    normalizeString(
      thread?.title,
      "",
      2000
    );

  const description =
    normalizeString(
      thread?.description,
      "",
      3000
    );

  let output =
    `- ${title}`;

  if (description) {
    output +=
      ` — ${description}`;
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT SUBJECTS
// =========================================================

function formatRecallSubjectForPromptV13(
  subject
) {
  return (
    `- ${normalizeString(
      subject?.display_name ||
      subject?.subject_key,
      "",
      1000
    )}` +
    ` [type=${normalizeString(
      subject?.subject_type,
      "topic",
      100
    )}; mentions=${Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        subject?.mention_count,
        0
      )
    )}; importance=${normalizeRecallV13Importance(
      subject?.importance,
      25
    )}]`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// EVIDENCE THRESHOLD FOR GENERALIZATIONS
//
// Nameless is allowed to notice patterns.
//
// He is NOT allowed to turn one event into:
//
// "you always..."
// "you never..."
// "every time..."
// "this is what you do..."
//
// unless stored evidence supports it.
// =========================================================

function recallV13PatternAllowsStrongGeneralization(
  pattern
) {
  const evidence =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        pattern?.evidence_count,
        0
      )
    );

  const counterexamples =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        pattern?.counterexample_count,
        0
      )
    );

  const confidence =
    recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      pattern?.confidence
    );

  if (
    evidence < 3
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    confidence < 0.58
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    counterexamples >=
    evidence
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD GENERALIZATION GUIDANCE
// =========================================================

function buildRecallGeneralizationGuidanceV13(
  patterns
) {
  const source =
    Array.isArray(
      patterns
    )
      ? patterns
      : [];

  const strong =
    source.filter(
      recallV13PatternAllowsStrongGeneralization
    );

  const weak =
    source.filter(
      (pattern) =>
        !recallV13PatternAllowsStrongGeneralization(
          pattern
        )
    );

  const lines =
    [];

  lines.push(
    "PATTERN LANGUAGE RULE:"
  );

  lines.push(
    "Do not say the player 'always', 'never', 'every time', or 'keeps doing this' merely because one remembered example resembles the present situation."
  );

  if (strong.length) {
    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    lines.push(
      "PATTERNS WITH ENOUGH EVIDENCE FOR STRONGER GENERALIZATION:"
    );

    for (const pattern of strong) {
      lines.push(
        formatRecallPatternForPromptV13(
          pattern
        )
      );
    }
  }

  if (weak.length) {
    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    lines.push(
      "TENTATIVE PATTERNS — MAY BE NOTICED, BUT SPEAK CAUTIOUSLY:"
    );

    for (const pattern of weak) {
      lines.push(
        formatRecallPatternForPromptV13(
          pattern
        )
      );
    }
  }

  return lines.join(
    "\n"
  );
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD MEMORY CONTEXT STRING
// =========================================================

function renderRecallMemoryPacketV13(
  packet
) {
  const sections =
    [];

  const mergedMemories =
    deduplicateRecallPromptMemoriesV13([
      packet?.foundationalMemories,
      packet?.firstConversation,
      packet?.relevantMemories
    ]);

  if (mergedMemories.length) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "LONG-TERM MEMORY:",
        "",
        ...mergedMemories.map(
          formatRecallMemoryForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.subjects
    ) &&
    packet.subjects.length
  ) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "IMPORTANT RECURRING SUBJECTS:",
        "",
        ...packet.subjects.map(
          formatRecallSubjectForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.judgments
    ) &&
    packet.judgments.length
  ) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "NAMELESS'S CURRENT JUDGMENTS:",
        "",
        "These are Nameless's interpretations, not objective facts.",
        "",
        ...packet.judgments.map(
          formatRecallJudgmentForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.positions
    ) &&
    packet.positions.length
  ) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "NAMELESS'S OWN PRIOR POSITIONS:",
        "",
        "These are positions Nameless himself previously argued.",
        "He may stand by them, qualify them, reverse them, or be confronted with them.",
        "",
        ...packet.positions.map(
          formatNamelessPositionForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.contradictions
    ) &&
    packet.contradictions.length
  ) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "UNRESOLVED POSSIBLE CONTRADICTIONS:",
        "",
        "These are possible conflicts detected by continuity analysis.",
        "Do not automatically accuse the player of lying.",
        "Changed circumstances, jokes, nuance, or a genuine change of mind may explain them.",
        "",
        ...packet.contradictions.map(
          formatRecallContradictionForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.openThreads
    ) &&
    packet.openThreads.length
  ) {
    sections.push(
      [
        "UNFINISHED BUSINESS:",
        "",
        ...packet.openThreads.map(
          formatRecallOpenThreadForPromptV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
    );
  }

  sections.push(
    buildRecallGeneralizationGuidanceV13(
      packet?.patterns ||
      []
    )
  );

  let output =
    sections
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(
        "\n\n==============================\n\n"
      );

  if (
    output.length >
    RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
  ) {
    output =
      output.slice(
        0,
        RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
      ) +
      "\n\n[LONG-TERM MEMORY CONTEXT TRUNCATED FOR THIS TURN]";
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD READY-TO-INJECT MEMORY CONTEXT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallMemoryContextV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const packet =
    await buildRecallMemoryPacketV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      currentUserMessage
    );

  return {
    packet,

    text:
      renderRecallMemoryPacketV13(
        packet
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MEMORY BEHAVIOR DIRECTIVE
//
// This is deliberately explicit.
//
// Merely dumping database rows into the model prompt does
// not create good long-term memory behavior.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_MEMORY_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE = `
LONG-TERM CONTINUITY RULES:

You possess persistent long-term memory of this audience member.

Use memory naturally.

Do not announce that you queried a database.
Do not say "according to my memory records."
Do not dump memories merely to prove you remember them.

A remembered fact should affect how you interpret the current conversation.

A remembered argument may be referenced when relevant.

A remembered promise may be enforced.

An unresolved dispute may return later.

An old joke may become an inside joke.

A recurring subject may acquire emotional history.

A contradiction may be challenged if the contradiction is real and relevant.

A pattern may be named only when there is enough evidence.

IMPORTANT:

One example is not "you always do this."

Two examples may suggest a tendency.

Three or more credible examples can justify stronger language if there are not equally strong counterexamples.

FOUNDATIONAL MEMORY:

Foundational memories carry exceptional continuity weight.
They should not dominate unrelated conversations, but when relevant they outrank ordinary recent trivia.

FIRST CONVERSATION:

The first conversation is formative.
It may remain psychologically important years later.

Do not constantly mention that something came from the first conversation.
Simply allow those memories to retain unusual weight.

REINFORCEMENT:

Repeated memories are stronger evidence than one-off remarks.

A statement repeated over months is more meaningful than an incidental sentence.

SUBJECTIVE JUDGMENTS:

Nameless's stored judgments are HIS judgments.
They are not guaranteed facts.

He can be wrong.
He can become more confident.
He can lose confidence.
He can revise himself.

SELF-CONTINUITY:

Nameless remembers his own substantive positions.

If he previously argued something and now argues the opposite, he should not magically forget the earlier position.

He may explain the change.
He may admit the change.
He may defend the distinction.
He may stubbornly deny inconsistency if that is dramatically appropriate.

But the underlying continuity remains available.

CONTRADICTIONS:

Do not treat every inconsistency as deception.

People change their minds.
People joke.
People exaggerate.
Circumstances change.
Memory is imperfect.

Challenge only when the conflict is meaningful enough to matter.

NATURALISM:

Memory should make the relationship feel accumulated, not mechanical.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// BUILD COMPLETE MODEL MEMORY INJECTION
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallModelMemoryInjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const context =
    await buildRecallMemoryContextV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      currentUserMessage
    );

  if (!context.text) {
    return {
      packet:
        context.packet,

      text:
        RECALL_V13_MEMORY_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE
    };
  }

  return {
    packet:
      context.packet,

    text:
      [
        RECALL_V13_MEMORY_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE,
        "",
        "==============================",
        "",
        context.text
      ].join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RELEVANT RELATIONSHIP RETRIEVAL
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallRelationshipsForPromptV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit = 12
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_relationships

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          importance DESC,
          interaction_count DESC,
          updated_at DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            12
          ),
          1,
          100
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT RELATIONSHIP STATE
// =========================================================

function formatRecallRelationshipForPromptV13(
  relationship
) {
  const character =
    normalizeString(
      relationship?.character_key,
      "unknown",
      200
    );

  const state =
    normalizeString(
      relationship?.relationship_state,
      "unknown",
      1000
    );

  const notes =
    normalizeString(
      relationship?.notes,
      "",
      3000
    );

  const interactions =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        relationship?.interaction_count,
        0
      )
    );

  let output =
    `- ${character}: ${state} [interactions=${interactions}]`;

  if (notes) {
    output +=
      ` — ${notes}`;
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// EXTENDED MEMORY PACKET WITH RELATIONSHIPS
// =========================================================

async function buildExtendedRecallMemoryPacketV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query
) {
  const [
    packet,
    relationships
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      buildRecallMemoryPacketV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        query
      ),

      loadRecallRelationshipsForPromptV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        12
      )
    ]);

  return {
    ...packet,
    relationships
  };
}


// =========================================================
// EXTENDED MEMORY RENDERER
// =========================================================

function renderExtendedRecallMemoryPacketV13(
  packet
) {
  const base =
    renderRecallMemoryPacketV13(
      packet
    );

  const relationships =
    Array.isArray(
      packet?.relationships
    )
      ? packet.relationships
      : [];

  if (!relationships.length) {
    return base;
  }

  const relationshipText =
    [
      "RELATIONSHIP CONTINUITY:",
      "",
      ...relationships.map(
        formatRecallRelationshipForPromptV13
      )
    ].join("\n");

  let output =
    [
      base,
      relationshipText
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(
        "\n\n==============================\n\n"
      );

  if (
    output.length >
    RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
  ) {
    output =
      output.slice(
        0,
        RECALL_V13_MAX_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
      ) +
      "\n\n[LONG-TERM MEMORY CONTEXT TRUNCATED FOR THIS TURN]";
  }

  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// FINAL MEMORY INJECTION BUILDER
// =========================================================

async function buildExtendedRecallModelMemoryInjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const packet =
    await buildExtendedRecallMemoryPacketV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      currentUserMessage
    );

  const contextText =
    renderExtendedRecallMemoryPacketV13(
      packet
    );

  return {
    packet,

    text:
      [
        RECALL_V13_MEMORY_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE,
        "",
        "==============================",
        "",
        contextText
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MEMORY DEBUG SUMMARY
//
// Useful during development without exposing entire private
// memory contents to the normal show UI.
// =========================================================

async function getRecallRetrievalDebugV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query
) {
  const packet =
    await buildExtendedRecallMemoryPacketV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      query
    );

  return {
    query:
      normalizeString(
        query,
        "",
        2000
      ),

    relevantMemories:
      packet.relevantMemories
        .map(
          (memory) => ({
            id:
              memory.id,

            type:
              memory.memory_type,

            subject:
              memory.subject_key,

            summary:
              memory.summary,

            score:
              memory.retrieval_score,

            components:
              memory.retrieval_components,

            foundational:
              Boolean(
                integerNumber(
                  memory.is_foundational,
                  0
                )
              ),

            reinforcementCount:
              integerNumber(
                memory.reinforcement_count,
                0
              ),

            firstConversation:
              integerNumber(
                memory.source_episode,
                -1
              ) === 1
          })
        ),

    foundationalCount:
      packet.foundationalMemories.length,

    firstConversationCount:
      packet.firstConversation.length,

    patternCount:
      packet.patterns.length,

    contradictionCount:
      packet.contradictions.length,

    judgmentCount:
      packet.judgments.length,

    positionCount:
      packet.positions.length,

    openThreadCount:
      packet.openThreads.length,

    subjectCount:
      packet.subjects.length,

    relationshipCount:
      packet.relationships.length
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MEMORY HEALTH / RETRIEVAL STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallMemoryV13Part3Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const [
    memoryCount,
    foundationalCount,
    reinforcedCount,
    firstConversationCount,
    patternCount,
    contradictionCount,
    judgmentCount,
    positionCount,
    threadCount
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_memories_v2
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_memories_v2
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
          AND is_foundational = 1
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_memories_v2
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
          AND reinforcement_count >= 2
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_memories_v2
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_archived = 0
          AND source_episode = 1
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_patterns
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND evidence_count >= ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,
          RECALL_V13_PATTERN_MIN_EVIDENCE
        )
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_contradictions
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'unresolved'
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_nameless_judgments
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND active = 1
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_nameless_positions
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND active = 1
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
        FROM recall_open_threads
        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND status = 'open'
      `)
        .bind(visitorId)
        .first()
    ]);

  return {
    memories:
      integerNumber(
        memoryCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    foundationalMemories:
      integerNumber(
        foundationalCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    reinforcedMemories:
      integerNumber(
        reinforcedCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    firstConversationMemories:
      integerNumber(
        firstConversationCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    confirmedPatterns:
      integerNumber(
        patternCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    unresolvedContradictions:
      integerNumber(
        contradictionCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    activeJudgments:
      integerNumber(
        judgmentCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    activeNamelessPositions:
      integerNumber(
        positionCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    openThreads:
      integerNumber(
        threadCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    part3Installed:
      true
  };
}


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT INTEGRATION NOTE
//
// Part 3 intentionally does NOT blindly overwrite the V12
// main prompt builder.
//
// The final injection point belongs in the generation
// surgery later in V13, where we can see the exact final
// host/Nameless prompt assembly and insert:
//
// const memoryV13 =
//   await buildExtendedRecallModelMemoryInjectionV13(
//     env,
//     visitorId,
//     userMessage
//   );
//
// then include:
//
// memoryV13.text
//
// in the private model instructions/context.
//
// Keeping the retrieval engine independent here prevents
// accidental damage to the working V12 generation path.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// END RECALL THY NAME V13 — PART 3 OF 8
//
// INSTALLED:
//
// - smart long-term memory retrieval
// - query-aware relevance scoring
// - importance weighting
// - confidence weighting
// - recency weighting
// - reinforcement weighting
// - foundational-memory priority
// - first-conversation privileged recall
// - memory-result diversification
// - important-subject retrieval
// - relationship continuity retrieval
// - unresolved contradiction injection
// - Nameless judgment injection
// - Nameless self-position injection
// - open-thread injection
// - evidence thresholds for "you always..." behavior
// - naturalistic long-term-memory behavior directive
// - ready-to-inject memory packet
// - retrieval debugging/status tools
//
// PART 4:
//
// DOCUMENT / MANUSCRIPT UPLOAD PIPELINE
// 2–3 MB DOCUMENT SUPPORT
// DOCUMENT TEXT EXTRACTION CONTRACT
// PERSISTENT DOCUMENT STORAGE
// DOCUMENT CHUNKING
// DOCUMENT MEMORY
// LATER RECALL OF UPLOADED MANUSCRIPTS
// DOCUMENT-AWARE NAMELESS RESPONSES
//
// APPEND PART 4 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 4 OF 8
//
// DOCUMENT / MANUSCRIPT SYSTEM
//
// 2–3 MB DOCUMENT SUPPORT
// PERSISTENT DOCUMENT STORAGE
// DOCUMENT CHUNKING
// DOCUMENT INDEXING
// DOCUMENT MEMORY
// DOCUMENT RECALL
// DOCUMENT-AWARE NAMELESS RESPONSES
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART DIRECTLY AFTER V13 PART 3.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES =
  3 * 1024 * 1024;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES =
  2 * 1024 * 1024;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_TITLE_LENGTH =
  300;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_FILENAME_LENGTH =
  500;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_TARGET =
  6000;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_OVERLAP =
  700;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS =
  800;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS =
  24000;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_SUMMARY_MAX =
  12000;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_LIMIT =
  12;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS =
  new Set([
    "txt",
    "md",
    "markdown",
    "text",
    "csv",
    "json",
    "html",
    "htm",
    "rtf"
  ]);


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT DATABASE MIGRATION
//
// Safe to run repeatedly.
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallDocumentSchemaV13(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_documents (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        document_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        title TEXT NOT NULL,

        filename TEXT,

        mime_type TEXT,

        extension TEXT,

        byte_size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        character_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        content_hash TEXT,

        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ready',

        document_summary TEXT,

        first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        last_accessed_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          visitor_id,
          document_key
        )
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_documents_visitor
      ON recall_documents(
        visitor_id,
        updated_at DESC
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_documents_hash
      ON recall_documents(
        visitor_id,
        content_hash
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_document_chunks (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        document_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        chunk_index INTEGER NOT NULL,

        start_character INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        end_character INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        content TEXT NOT NULL,

        normalized_content TEXT,

        token_hint INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          document_id,
          chunk_index
        )
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_chunks_document
      ON recall_document_chunks(
        document_id,
        chunk_index
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_chunks_visitor
      ON recall_document_chunks(
        visitor_id,
        document_id
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_document_insights (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        document_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

        insight_key TEXT,

        insight_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'observation',

        subject_key TEXT,

        insight_text TEXT NOT NULL,

        confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.75,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        evidence_chunk_index INTEGER,

        reinforcement_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_insights_visitor
      ON recall_document_insights(
        visitor_id,
        importance DESC,
        updated_at DESC
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_document_insights_document
      ON recall_document_insights(
        document_id,
        importance DESC
      )
    `)
  ]);

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// INITIALIZE DOCUMENT SYSTEM
// =========================================================

async function initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await ensureRecallDocumentSchemaV13(
    env
  );

  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT UTILITIES
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallDocumentTitleV13(
  value,
  fallback = "Untitled Document"
) {
  const normalized =
    normalizeString(
      value,
      fallback,
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_TITLE_LENGTH
    );

  return normalized ||
    fallback;
}


function normalizeRecallDocumentFilenameV13(
  value
) {
  return normalizeString(
    value,
    "",
    RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_FILENAME_LENGTH
  );
}


function getRecallDocumentExtensionV13(
  filename
) {
  const normalized =
    normalizeRecallDocumentFilenameV13(
      filename
    );

  const match =
    normalized
      .toLowerCase()
      .match(
        /\.([a-z0-9]+)$/
      );

  return match
    ? match[1]
    : "";
}


function recallDocumentExtensionAllowedV13(
  filename
) {
  const extension =
    getRecallDocumentExtensionV13(
      filename
    );

  if (!extension) {
    return true;
  }

  return RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS
    .has(
      extension
    );
}


function normalizeRecallDocumentTextV13(
  value
) {
  let text =
    typeof value === "string"
      ? value
      : "";

  text =
    text.replace(
      /\r\n/g,
      "\n"
    );

  text =
    text.replace(
      /\r/g,
      "\n"
    );

  text =
    text.replace(
      /\u0000/g,
      ""
    );

  text =
    text.replace(
      /[ \t]+\n/g,
      "\n"
    );

  text =
    text.replace(
      /\n{6,}/g,
      "\n\n\n\n"
    );

  return text.trim();
}


function estimateRecallDocumentBytesV13(
  text
) {
  const normalized =
    typeof text === "string"
      ? text
      : "";

  try {
    return new TextEncoder()
      .encode(
        normalized
      )
      .byteLength;
  } catch {
    return normalized.length;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT HASH
// =========================================================

async function hashRecallDocumentV13(
  text
) {
  const normalized =
    typeof text === "string"
      ? text
      : "";

  try {
    const encoded =
      new TextEncoder()
        .encode(
          normalized
        );

    const digest =
      await crypto.subtle.digest(
        "SHA-256",
        encoded
      );

    return Array.from(
      new Uint8Array(
        digest
      )
    )
      .map(
        (byte) =>
          byte
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(
              2,
              "0"
            )
      )
      .join("");
  } catch {
    return recallV13NormalizeKey(
      normalized.slice(
        0,
        10000
      )
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT KEY
// =========================================================

function buildRecallDocumentKeyV13(
  title,
  filename,
  contentHash
) {
  const base =
    [
      title,
      filename,
      contentHash
        ? contentHash.slice(
            0,
            20
          )
        : ""
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" ");

  return recallV13NormalizeKey(
    base
  );
}


// =========================================================
// VALIDATE DOCUMENT
// =========================================================

function validateRecallDocumentV13({
  filename,
  text,
  byteSize
}) {
  const normalizedText =
    normalizeRecallDocumentTextV13(
      text
    );

  const effectiveBytes =
    Number.isFinite(
      Number(
        byteSize
      )
    )
      ? Number(
          byteSize
        )
      : estimateRecallDocumentBytesV13(
          normalizedText
        );

  if (!normalizedText) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_EMPTY"
    };
  }

  if (
    effectiveBytes >
    RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE",
      maximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES,
      actualBytes:
        effectiveBytes
    };
  }

  if (
    !recallDocumentExtensionAllowedV13(
      filename
    )
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_SUPPORTED",
      extension:
        getRecallDocumentExtensionV13(
          filename
        )
    };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    text:
      normalizedText,
    byteSize:
      effectiveBytes,
    recommendedMaximum:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,
    hardMaximum:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES
  };
}


// =========================================================
// TEXT EXTRACTION CONTRACT
//
// IMPORTANT:
//
// Worker code cannot magically extract every proprietary
// document format.
//
// The browser/frontend should convert supported uploads to
// plain text before calling storeRecallDocumentV13().
//
// TXT / MD / JSON / CSV / HTML are straightforward.
//
// PDF / DOCX support can later be added through a dedicated
// parser or frontend extraction layer without changing the
// persistent memory architecture below.
//
// The storage system receives:
//
// {
//   filename,
//   title,
//   mimeType,
//   byteSize,
//   text
// }
//
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallDocumentPayloadV13(
  payload
) {
  const source =
    payload &&
    typeof payload === "object"
      ? payload
      : {};

  const filename =
    normalizeRecallDocumentFilenameV13(
      source.filename
    );

  const title =
    normalizeRecallDocumentTitleV13(
      source.title ||
      filename ||
      "Untitled Document"
    );

  const mimeType =
    normalizeString(
      source.mimeType ||
      source.mime_type,
      "text/plain",
      300
    );

  const text =
    normalizeRecallDocumentTextV13(
      source.text
    );

  const byteSize =
    Number.isFinite(
      Number(
        source.byteSize ??
        source.byte_size
      )
    )
      ? Number(
          source.byteSize ??
          source.byte_size
        )
      : estimateRecallDocumentBytesV13(
          text
        );

  return {
    filename,
    title,
    mimeType,
    text,
    byteSize,
    extension:
      getRecallDocumentExtensionV13(
        filename
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// CHUNK DOCUMENT
//
// Tries to break on paragraph boundaries first.
//
// Falls back to sentence/newline/character boundaries.
// =========================================================

function chunkRecallDocumentV13(
  text,
  {
    target =
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_TARGET,

    overlap =
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_OVERLAP
  } = {}
) {
  const source =
    normalizeRecallDocumentTextV13(
      text
    );

  if (!source) {
    return [];
  }

  const safeTarget =
    clamp(
      integerNumber(
        target,
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_TARGET
      ),
      1500,
      16000
    );

  const safeOverlap =
    clamp(
      integerNumber(
        overlap,
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CHUNK_OVERLAP
      ),
      0,
      Math.floor(
        safeTarget / 3
      )
    );

  const chunks =
    [];

  let cursor = 0;

  while (
    cursor <
    source.length
  ) {
    if (
      chunks.length >=
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
    ) {
      break;
    }

    let desiredEnd =
      Math.min(
        source.length,
        cursor +
          safeTarget
      );

    let end =
      desiredEnd;

    if (
      desiredEnd <
      source.length
    ) {
      const searchFloor =
        Math.max(
          cursor +
            Math.floor(
              safeTarget * 0.55
            ),
          cursor + 1
        );

      const candidateText =
        source.slice(
          searchFloor,
          desiredEnd
        );

      const paragraphBreak =
        candidateText
          .lastIndexOf(
            "\n\n"
          );

      if (
        paragraphBreak >= 0
      ) {
        end =
          searchFloor +
          paragraphBreak +
          2;
      } else {
        const newlineBreak =
          candidateText
            .lastIndexOf(
              "\n"
            );

        if (
          newlineBreak >= 0
        ) {
          end =
            searchFloor +
            newlineBreak +
            1;
        } else {
          const sentenceMatches =
            [
              ...candidateText
                .matchAll(
                  /[.!?]["')\]]?\s+/g
                )
            ];

          const lastSentence =
            sentenceMatches.length
              ? sentenceMatches[
                  sentenceMatches.length -
                  1
                ]
              : null;

          if (lastSentence) {
            end =
              searchFloor +
              lastSentence.index +
              lastSentence[0].length;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (
      end <=
      cursor
    ) {
      end =
        Math.min(
          source.length,
          cursor +
            safeTarget
        );
    }

    const content =
      source
        .slice(
          cursor,
          end
        )
        .trim();

    if (content) {
      chunks.push({
        chunkIndex:
          chunks.length,

        startCharacter:
          cursor,

        endCharacter:
          end,

        content,

        normalizedContent:
          recallV13NormalizeKey(
            content
          ),

        tokenHint:
          Math.ceil(
            content.length /
            4
          )
      });
    }

    if (
      end >=
      source.length
    ) {
      break;
    }

    const nextCursor =
      Math.max(
        cursor + 1,
        end -
          safeOverlap
      );

    cursor =
      nextCursor;
  }

  return chunks;
}


// =========================================================
// FIND EXISTING DOCUMENT BY HASH
// =========================================================

async function findRecallDocumentByHashV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  contentHash
) {
  if (!contentHash) {
    return null;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_documents

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND content_hash = ?

      ORDER BY
        updated_at DESC

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      contentHash
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD DOCUMENT
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallDocumentV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  documentId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_documents

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      documentId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD DOCUMENT CHUNKS
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallDocumentChunksV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  documentId,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_document_chunks

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND document_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          chunk_index ASC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
          ),
          1,
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// STORE DOCUMENT
//
// This is persistent.
//
// Re-uploading the exact same document does not create
// endless duplicates.
// =========================================================

async function storeRecallDocumentV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  payload
) {
  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const normalized =
    normalizeRecallDocumentPayloadV13(
      payload
    );

  const validation =
    validateRecallDocumentV13({
      filename:
        normalized.filename,

      text:
        normalized.text,

      byteSize:
        normalized.byteSize
    });

  if (!validation.ok) {
    return validation;
  }

  const contentHash =
    await hashRecallDocumentV13(
      validation.text
    );

  const existing =
    await findRecallDocumentByHashV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      contentHash
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  if (existing) {
    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_documents

        SET
          last_accessed_at = ?,
          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        now,
        now,
        existing.id
      )
      .run();

    return {
      ok: true,
      duplicate: true,
      documentId:
        existing.id,
      documentKey:
        existing.document_key,
      title:
        existing.title,
      filename:
        existing.filename,
      chunkCount:
        integerNumber(
          existing.chunk_count,
          0
        ),
      byteSize:
        integerNumber(
          existing.byte_size,
          0
        )
    };
  }

  const chunks =
    chunkRecallDocumentV13(
      validation.text
    );

  if (!chunks.length) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_CHUNKING_FAILED"
    };
  }

  const documentKey =
    buildRecallDocumentKeyV13(
      normalized.title,
      normalized.filename,
      contentHash
    );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const insertResult =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_documents (
          visitor_id,
          document_key,
          title,
          filename,
          mime_type,
          extension,
          byte_size,
          character_count,
          chunk_count,
          content_hash,
          status,
          document_summary,
          first_seen_at,
          last_accessed_at,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
          ?, ?, ?, ?,
          'ready',
          NULL,
          ?, ?, ?, ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentKey,
        normalized.title,
        normalized.filename,
        normalized.mimeType,
        normalized.extension,
        validation.byteSize,
        validation.text.length,
        chunks.length,
        contentHash,
        now,
        now,
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  const documentId =
    insertResult?.meta?.last_row_id;

  if (!documentId) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_INSERT_FAILED"
    };
  }

  const statements =
    chunks.map(
      (chunk) =>
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO recall_document_chunks (
            visitor_id,
            document_id,
            chunk_index,
            start_character,
            end_character,
            content,
            normalized_content,
            token_hint,
            created_at,
            updated_at
          )

          VALUES (
            ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
            ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
          )
        `)
          .bind(
            visitorId,
            documentId,
            chunk.chunkIndex,
            chunk.startCharacter,
            chunk.endCharacter,
            chunk.content,
            chunk.normalizedContent,
            chunk.tokenHint,
            now,
            now
          )
    );

  const batchSize =
    50;

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index <
    statements.length;
    index +=
      batchSize
  ) {
    await db.batch(
      statements.slice(
        index,
        index +
          batchSize
      )
    );
  }

  await createRecallMemoryV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      memoryType:
        RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DOCUMENT,

      subjectKey:
        documentKey,

      summary:
        `The audience member uploaded a document titled "${normalized.title}".`,

      detail:
        normalized.filename
          ? `Persistent uploaded document: ${normalized.filename}.`
          : `Persistent uploaded document: ${normalized.title}.`,

      importance:
        55,

      confidence:
        1,

      reinforcementCount:
        1,

      isFoundational:
        false
    }
  );

  await upsertRecallSubjectV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      subjectKey:
        documentKey,

      displayName:
        normalized.title,

      subjectType:
        "document",

      importance:
        55
    }
  );

  return {
    ok: true,
    duplicate: false,
    documentId,
    documentKey,
    title:
      normalized.title,
    filename:
      normalized.filename,
    chunkCount:
      chunks.length,
    characterCount:
      validation.text.length,
    byteSize:
      validation.byteSize,
    recommendedMaximum:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,
    hardMaximum:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LIST DOCUMENTS
// =========================================================

async function listRecallDocumentsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit = 100
) {
  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          document_key,
          title,
          filename,
          mime_type,
          extension,
          byte_size,
          character_count,
          chunk_count,
          status,
          document_summary,
          first_seen_at,
          last_accessed_at,
          created_at,
          updated_at

        FROM recall_documents

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          updated_at DESC,
          id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            100
          ),
          1,
          500
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT RETRIEVAL SCORING
// =========================================================

function scoreRecallDocumentChunkV13(
  chunk,
  document,
  query
) {
  const content =
    chunk?.content ||
    "";

  const documentMetadata =
    [
      document?.title,
      document?.filename,
      document?.document_summary
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(" ");

  const contentScore =
    recallV13TextSimilarity(
      query,
      content
    );

  const metadataScore =
    recallV13TextSimilarity(
      query,
      documentMetadata
    );

  const queryTokens =
    recallV13TokenSet(
      query
    );

  const contentTokens =
    recallV13TokenSet(
      content
    );

  let exactTokenHits =
    0;

  for (
    const token
    of queryTokens
  ) {
    if (
      token.length >= 4 &&
      contentTokens.has(
        token
      )
    ) {
      exactTokenHits++;
    }
  }

  const tokenHitScore =
    queryTokens.size
      ? Math.min(
          1,
          exactTokenHits /
            Math.max(
              1,
              queryTokens.size
            )
        )
      : 0;

  return (
    contentScore *
      0.72 +
    metadataScore *
      0.18 +
    tokenHitScore *
      0.30
  );
}


// =========================================================
// SEARCH DOCUMENT CHUNKS
//
// This implementation is deliberately database-simple.
//
// For a few manuscripts it works without introducing an
// external vector database.
//
// If the archive eventually grows enormous, this function
// can later be replaced with embeddings/vector search while
// leaving the rest of the architecture untouched.
// =========================================================

async function searchRecallDocumentsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query,
  {
    documentId = null,

    limit =
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
  } = {}
) {
  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  let documents =
    [];

  if (documentId) {
    const document =
      await loadRecallDocumentV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        documentId
      );

    if (document) {
      documents = [
        document
      ];
    }
  } else {
    documents =
      await listRecallDocumentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        30
      );
  }

  const candidates =
    [];

  for (
    const document
    of documents
  ) {
    const chunkResult =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT *

          FROM recall_document_chunks

          WHERE
            visitor_id = ?
            AND document_id = ?

          ORDER BY
            chunk_index ASC

          LIMIT ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,
          document.id,
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
        )
        .all();

    const chunks =
      Array.isArray(
        chunkResult?.results
      )
        ? chunkResult.results
        : [];

    for (
      const chunk
      of chunks
    ) {
      candidates.push({
        document,
        chunk,

        retrievalScore:
          scoreRecallDocumentChunkV13(
            chunk,
            document,
            query
          )
      });
    }
  }

  candidates.sort(
    (left, right) =>
      right.retrievalScore -
      left.retrievalScore
  );

  return candidates.slice(
    0,
    clamp(
      integerNumber(
        limit,
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
      ),
      1,
      50
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT QUERY DETECTION
//
// We do not want manuscript text flooding every unrelated
// conversation.
//
// Documents are retrieved aggressively when:
// - the user names a document
// - the user says manuscript/book/document/file
// - the current query overlaps strongly with document data
// =========================================================

function recallV13QueryExplicitlyRequestsDocuments(
  query
) {
  return /\b(document|documents|manuscript|manuscripts|book|novel|file|upload|uploaded|chapter|essay|paper|text)\b/i
    .test(
      normalizeString(
        query,
        "",
        60000
      )
    );
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD DOCUMENT CONTEXT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallDocumentContextV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query
) {
  const documents =
    await listRecallDocumentsV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      30
    );

  if (!documents.length) {
    return {
      documents: [],
      matches: [],
      text: ""
    };
  }

  const explicit =
    recallV13QueryExplicitlyRequestsDocuments(
      query
    );

  const matches =
    await searchRecallDocumentsV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      query,
      {
        limit:
          explicit
            ? RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
            : 6
      }
    );

  const usefulMatches =
    explicit
      ? matches
      : matches.filter(
          (match) =>
            match.retrievalScore >=
            0.18
        );

  if (!usefulMatches.length) {
    return {
      documents,
      matches: [],
      text: ""
    };
  }

  const lines =
    [];

  lines.push(
    "RELEVANT MATERIAL FROM PERSISTENTLY UPLOADED DOCUMENTS:"
  );

  lines.push(
    ""
  );

  lines.push(
    "Treat document text as material supplied by the audience member."
  );

  lines.push(
    "Do not pretend Nameless personally witnessed events merely because they appear in a manuscript."
  );

  lines.push(
    "Nameless may remember having read the document previously."
  );

  lines.push(
    ""
  );

  let currentLength =
    lines.join("\n").length;

  for (
    const match
    of usefulMatches
  ) {
    const document =
      match.document;

    const chunk =
      match.chunk;

    const block =
      [
        `DOCUMENT: ${document.title}`,
        document.filename
          ? `FILE: ${document.filename}`
          : null,
        `CHUNK: ${integerNumber(
          chunk.chunk_index,
          0
        ) + 1} / ${integerNumber(
          document.chunk_count,
          0
        )}`,
        `RELEVANCE: ${finiteNumber(
          match.retrievalScore,
          0
        ).toFixed(3)}`,
        "",
        normalizeString(
          chunk.content,
          "",
          9000
        )
      ]
        .filter(
          (value) =>
            value !== null &&
            value !== undefined
        )
        .join("\n");

    if (
      currentLength +
        block.length >
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
    ) {
      break;
    }

    lines.push(
      block
    );

    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    lines.push(
      "------------------------------"
    );

    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    currentLength =
      lines.join("\n").length;
  }

  return {
    documents,
    matches:
      usefulMatches,
    text:
      lines.join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// STORE DOCUMENT SUMMARY
// =========================================================

async function setRecallDocumentSummaryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  documentId,
  summary
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const normalizedSummary =
    normalizeString(
      summary,
      "",
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_SUMMARY_MAX
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_documents

      SET
        document_summary = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      normalizedSummary ||
        null,
      now,
      visitorId,
      documentId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    ok: true,
    documentId,
    summary:
      normalizedSummary
  };
}


// =========================================================
// STORE DOCUMENT INSIGHT
//
// These are persistent conclusions about a manuscript.
//
// Example:
//
// "The narrator repeatedly associates mirrors with identity."
//
// This allows Nameless to remember not only raw text but
// conclusions reached while discussing the text.
// =========================================================

async function storeRecallDocumentInsightV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    documentId,
    insightKey = null,
    insightType =
      "observation",
    subjectKey =
      null,
    insightText,
    confidence =
      0.75,
    importance =
      40,
    evidenceChunkIndex =
      null
  }
) {
  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const text =
    normalizeString(
      insightText,
      "",
      10000
    );

  if (!text) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_EMPTY"
    };
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  const normalizedInsightKey =
    recallV13NormalizeKey(
      insightKey ||
      `${documentId} ${insightType} ${subjectKey || ""} ${text}`
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_document_insights

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND document_id = ?
          AND insight_key = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId,
        normalizedInsightKey
      )
      .first();

  if (existing) {
    const reinforcementCount =
      Math.max(
        1,
        integerNumber(
          existing.reinforcement_count,
          1
        ) + 1
      );

    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_document_insights

        SET
          insight_text = ?,
          confidence = ?,
          importance = ?,
          evidence_chunk_index = ?,
          reinforcement_count = ?,
          updated_at = ?

        WHERE id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        text,

        recallV13ConfidenceScore(
          confidence
        ),

        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          40
        ),

        evidenceChunkIndex,

        reinforcementCount,

        now,

        existing.id
      )
      .run();

    return {
      ok: true,
      updated: true,
      insightId:
        existing.id,
      reinforcementCount
    };
  }

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_document_insights (
          visitor_id,
          document_id,
          insight_key,
          insight_type,
          subject_key,
          insight_text,
          confidence,
          importance,
          evidence_chunk_index,
          reinforcement_count,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
          ?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId,
        normalizedInsightKey,

        normalizeString(
          insightType,
          "observation",
          100
        ),

        subjectKey
          ? recallV13NormalizeKey(
              subjectKey
            )
          : null,

        text,

        recallV13ConfidenceScore(
          confidence
        ),

        normalizeRecallV13Importance(
          importance,
          40
        ),

        evidenceChunkIndex,

        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  return {
    ok: true,
    updated: false,
    insightId:
      result?.meta?.last_row_id ||
      null,
    reinforcementCount:
      1
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD DOCUMENT INSIGHTS
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallDocumentInsightsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    documentId = null,

    limit =
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_LIMIT
  } = {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  let result;

  if (documentId) {
    result =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT
            i.*,
            d.title AS document_title,
            d.filename AS document_filename

          FROM recall_document_insights i

          JOIN recall_documents d
            ON d.id = i.document_id

          WHERE
            i.visitor_id = ?
            AND i.document_id = ?

          ORDER BY
            i.importance DESC,
            i.reinforcement_count DESC,
            i.updated_at DESC

          LIMIT ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,
          documentId,

          clamp(
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_LIMIT
            ),
            1,
            100
          )
        )
        .all();
  } else {
    result =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT
            i.*,
            d.title AS document_title,
            d.filename AS document_filename

          FROM recall_document_insights i

          JOIN recall_documents d
            ON d.id = i.document_id

          WHERE
            i.visitor_id = ?

          ORDER BY
            i.importance DESC,
            i.reinforcement_count DESC,
            i.updated_at DESC

          LIMIT ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,

          clamp(
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_LIMIT
            ),
            1,
            100
          )
        )
        .all();
  }

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT DOCUMENT INSIGHT
// =========================================================

function formatRecallDocumentInsightV13(
  insight
) {
  return (
    `- [${normalizeString(
      insight?.document_title,
      "Document",
      500
    )}] ` +
    normalizeString(
      insight?.insight_text,
      "",
      5000
    ) +
    ` [confidence=${recallV13ConfidenceScore(
      insight?.confidence
    ).toFixed(2)}; reinforcement=${Math.max(
      1,
      integerNumber(
        insight?.reinforcement_count,
        1
      )
    )}]`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD DOCUMENT INSIGHT CONTEXT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallDocumentInsightContextV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const insights =
    await loadRecallDocumentInsightsV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        limit:
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_INSIGHT_LIMIT
      }
    );

  if (!insights.length) {
    return {
      insights: [],
      text: ""
    };
  }

  return {
    insights,

    text:
      [
        "PERSISTENT INTERPRETATIONS OF PREVIOUSLY READ DOCUMENTS:",
        "",
        "These are conclusions previously formed while discussing uploaded material.",
        "They may be revised when new evidence appears.",
        "",
        ...insights.map(
          formatRecallDocumentInsightV13
        )
      ].join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MARK DOCUMENT AS ACCESSED
// =========================================================

async function touchRecallDocumentV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  documentId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_documents

      SET
        last_accessed_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      now,
      now,
      visitorId,
      documentId
    )
    .run();

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// REMEMBER DOCUMENT DISCUSSION
//
// Call this after a substantial discussion of a document.
//
// It creates a normal long-term memory linking the current
// conversation to the persistent manuscript.
// =========================================================

async function rememberRecallDocumentDiscussionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    documentId,
    summary,
    detail = "",
    importance = 50
  }
) {
  const document =
    await loadRecallDocumentV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      documentId
    );

  if (!document) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND"
    };
  }

  await touchRecallDocumentV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    documentId
  );

  const memory =
    await createRecallMemoryV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        memoryType:
          RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DOCUMENT_INSIGHT,

        subjectKey:
          document.document_key,

        summary:
          normalizeString(
            summary,
            `Discussion of "${document.title}".`,
            6000
          ),

        detail:
          normalizeString(
            detail,
            "",
            10000
          ),

        importance:
          normalizeRecallV13Importance(
            importance,
            50
          ),

        confidence:
          0.9,

        reinforcementCount:
          1,

        isFoundational:
          false
      }
    );

  return {
    ok: true,
    document,
    memory
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT-AWARE MEMORY INJECTION
//
// Part 3 built the long-term memory packet.
//
// Part 4 extends it with manuscript context.
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallDocumentAwareInjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const [
    memoryInjection,
    documentContext,
    documentInsights
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      buildExtendedRecallModelMemoryInjectionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        currentUserMessage
      ),

      buildRecallDocumentContextV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        currentUserMessage
      ),

      buildRecallDocumentInsightContextV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  const sections =
    [
      memoryInjection?.text ||
        "",

      documentInsights?.text ||
        "",

      documentContext?.text ||
        ""
    ]
      .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    packet:
      memoryInjection?.packet ||
      null,

    documents:
      documentContext?.documents ||
      [],

    documentMatches:
      documentContext?.matches ||
      [],

    documentInsights:
      documentInsights?.insights ||
      [],

    text:
      sections.join(
        "\n\n==============================\n\n"
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT RESPONSE DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE = `
DOCUMENT / MANUSCRIPT CONTINUITY:

The audience member may upload substantial written material.

Uploaded documents persist across sessions.

When relevant, remember that you have previously encountered the document.

Do not act as though the manuscript vanishes when the conversation ends.

Distinguish carefully between:

1. What the document literally says.
2. What the audience member says about the document.
3. What Nameless previously concluded about the document.
4. What Nameless currently thinks about the document.

These are not automatically identical.

When a relevant passage is available, ground discussion in that passage.

Do not fabricate passages that are not present.

Do not claim to remember exact wording unless exact wording is present in retrieved document context or persistent memory.

A manuscript may change Nameless's opinion of its author.

A manuscript may reinforce or weaken an existing judgment.

A manuscript may create recurring subjects, jokes, disputes, promises, suspicions, or unfinished arguments.

Those consequences may persist even when the manuscript itself is not currently loaded into the immediate prompt.

If the audience member asks about an earlier uploaded manuscript, treat that as ordinary long-term continuity rather than a brand-new introduction.

Large documents should be discussed selectively.

Do not attempt to recite an entire manuscript into conversation.

Retrieve the portions relevant to the current question.

If insufficient relevant text has been retrieved, admit uncertainty rather than inventing the missing passage.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// COMPLETE MEMORY + DOCUMENT INJECTION
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallCompleteContinuityInjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const injection =
    await buildRecallDocumentAwareInjectionV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      currentUserMessage
    );

  return {
    ...injection,

    text:
      [
        injection.text,
        "",
        "==============================",
        "",
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// OPTIONAL SIMPLE TEXT-UPLOAD REQUEST HANDLER
//
// This is intentionally framework-neutral.
//
// Existing router surgery comes later.
//
// Feed it the parsed JSON body:
//
// {
//   title,
//   filename,
//   mimeType,
//   byteSize,
//   text
// }
//
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentUploadV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  body
) {
  try {
    const result =
      await storeRecallDocumentV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        body
      );

    if (!result.ok) {
      const status =
        result.error ===
        "DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE"
          ? 413
          : 400;

      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify(
          result
        ),
        {
          status,

          headers: {
            "content-type":
              "application/json; charset=utf-8",

            "cache-control":
              "no-store"
          }
        }
      );
    }

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify(
        result
      ),
      {
        status: 200,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Recall document upload failed:",
      error
    );

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        ok: false,
        error:
          "DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_FAILED"
      }),
      {
        status: 500,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// OPTIONAL DOCUMENT LIST REQUEST HANDLER
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentListV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  try {
    const documents =
      await listRecallDocumentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        100
      );

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        ok: true,
        documents
      }),
      {
        status: 200,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Recall document list failed:",
      error
    );

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        ok: false,
        error:
          "DOCUMENT_LIST_FAILED"
      }),
      {
        status: 500,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// OPTIONAL DOCUMENT SEARCH REQUEST HANDLER
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentSearchV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  body
) {
  try {
    const query =
      normalizeString(
        body?.query,
        "",
        60000
      );

    if (!query) {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          ok: false,
          error:
            "QUERY_REQUIRED"
        }),
        {
          status: 400,

          headers: {
            "content-type":
              "application/json; charset=utf-8",

            "cache-control":
              "no-store"
          }
        }
      );
    }

    const matches =
      await searchRecallDocumentsV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        query,
        {
          documentId:
            body?.documentId ||
            null,

          limit:
            body?.limit ||
            RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
        }
      );

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        ok: true,

        matches:
          matches.map(
            (match) => ({
              documentId:
                match.document.id,

              documentTitle:
                match.document.title,

              filename:
                match.document.filename,

              chunkIndex:
                match.chunk.chunk_index,

              content:
                match.chunk.content,

              score:
                match.retrievalScore
            })
          )
      }),
      {
        status: 200,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Recall document search failed:",
      error
    );

    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({
        ok: false,
        error:
          "DOCUMENT_SEARCH_FAILED"
      }),
      {
        status: 500,

        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "application/json; charset=utf-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT DELETE
//
// This deletes the uploaded manuscript itself.
//
// It intentionally does NOT automatically erase every
// conversational memory ever formed while discussing it.
//
// Reading something can have lasting consequences even if
// the original document is later removed.
// =========================================================

async function deleteRecallDocumentV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  documentId
) {
  const document =
    await loadRecallDocumentV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      documentId
    );

  if (!document) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND"
    };
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      DELETE FROM recall_document_insights

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND document_id = ?
    `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId
      ),

    db.prepare(`
      DELETE FROM recall_document_chunks

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND document_id = ?
    `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId
      ),

    db.prepare(`
      DELETE FROM recall_documents

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND id = ?
    `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        documentId
      )
  ]);

  return {
    ok: true,
    deletedDocumentId:
      documentId,
    title:
      document.title
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallDocumentV13Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const [
    documents,
    chunks,
    insights
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          COUNT(*) AS count,
          COALESCE(
            SUM(byte_size),
            0
          ) AS bytes

        FROM recall_documents

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_document_chunks

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT
          COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_document_insights

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first()
    ]);

  return {
    documentCount:
      integerNumber(
        documents?.count,
        0
      ),

    totalDocumentBytes:
      integerNumber(
        documents?.bytes,
        0
      ),

    documentChunkCount:
      integerNumber(
        chunks?.count,
        0
      ),

    documentInsightCount:
      integerNumber(
        insights?.count,
        0
      ),

    maximumUploadBytes:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES,

    recommendedUploadBytes:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,

    part4Installed:
      true
  };
}


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT FRONTEND CONTRACT
//
// The future UI can use:
//
// <input type="file">
//
// Read textual files in the browser.
//
// Then POST:
//
// {
//   title,
//   filename,
//   mimeType,
//   byteSize,
//   text
// }
//
// to the V13 document-upload route installed during router
// surgery.
//
// The frontend should show:
//
// UPLOAD DOCUMENT
// [filename]
// [size]
// [upload]
//
// followed by:
//
// DOCUMENT REMEMBERED
//
// The actual route and UI integration are intentionally
// deferred until the main worker/router surgery so we do
// not create duplicate fetch handlers or collide with V12.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT MEMORY PRINCIPLE
//
