// The important architecture is:
//
// RAW DOCUMENT
//      |
//      v
// PERSISTENT CHUNKS
//      |
//      +------> relevant passage retrieval
//      |
//      +------> document summary
//      |
//      +------> persistent document insights
//      |
//      +------> ordinary long-term memories
//      |
//      +------> relationship / judgment changes
//
// Therefore a manuscript can matter months or years after
// it was uploaded without requiring the entire manuscript
// to be inserted into every prompt.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// END RECALL THY NAME V13 — PART 4 OF 8
//
// INSTALLED:
//
// - 3 MB hard document limit
// - 2 MB recommended document size
// - persistent manuscript metadata
// - persistent manuscript chunks
// - duplicate-document detection
// - SHA-256 document identity
// - paragraph-aware chunking
// - overlapping chunks
// - document retrieval scoring
// - selective manuscript recall
// - document summaries
// - persistent document insights
// - document discussion memories
// - document-aware long-term continuity
// - document deletion
// - document status/debug information
// - framework-neutral upload handler
// - framework-neutral list handler
// - framework-neutral search handler
// - safe integration contract for later router surgery
//
// IMPORTANT:
//
// Raw PDF/DOCX binary extraction is NOT falsely claimed here.
// Part 4 establishes the real persistent document system.
// Text extraction must occur in the frontend or a dedicated
// parser layer before storage.
//
// PART 5:
//
// NAME CONFUSION / MISIDENTIFICATION ENGINE
// LARGE PUBLIC-DOMAIN-SAFE NAME POOL
// TEMPORARY / PERSISTENT DISPLAY-NAME CHANGES
// "RONALD REAGAN" / THIRD-PERSON NAME CONFUSION
// INSULT MISREAD
// COMPLIMENT MISREAD
// DEADPAN CORRECT-REFERENT PATH
// CONFUSION REPAIR
// RHYME MODE PERSISTENCE
//
// APPEND PART 5 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 5 OF 8
//
// NAME CONFUSION / MISIDENTIFICATION ENGINE
//
// LARGE SAFE NAME POOL
// NAMELESS DISPLAY-NAME STATE
// THIRD-PERSON NAME CONFUSION
// COMICAL + SERIOUS MISUNDERSTANDINGS
// INSULT MISREAD
// COMPLIMENT MISREAD
// DEADPAN CORRECT-REFERENT PATH
// CONFUSION REPAIR
// RHYME MODE
// MULTI-TURN RHYME PERSISTENCE
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART DIRECTLY AFTER V13 PART 4.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// NAME / CONFUSION CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME =
  "The Nameless Man";

const RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_BASE_CHANCE =

  40;

// =========================================================
// NAMELESS STATE SCHEMA
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallNamelessIdentitySchemaV13(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_nameless_identity (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,

        display_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'The Nameless Man',

        assigned_name TEXT,

        assigned_name_source TEXT,

        name_change_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        confusion_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        last_confusion_turn INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT -999,

        rhyme_mode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        rhyme_turns_remaining INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        rhyme_started_at TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_nameless_name_history (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        old_name TEXT,

        new_name TEXT NOT NULL,

        source_text TEXT,

        reason TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_nameless_name_history
      ON recall_nameless_name_history(
        visitor_id,
        created_at DESC
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_name_confusions (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        turn_number INTEGER,

        external_name TEXT NOT NULL,

        external_referent TEXT,

        interpretation TEXT NOT NULL,

        polarity TEXT,

        source_text TEXT,

        repaired INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        repair_text TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_name_confusions
      ON recall_name_confusions(
        visitor_id,
        created_at DESC
      )
    `)
  ]);

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// INITIALIZE NAMELESS IDENTITY
// =========================================================

async function initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await ensureRecallNamelessIdentitySchemaV13(
    env
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_nameless_identity

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  if (existing) {
    return existing;
  }

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_nameless_identity (
        visitor_id,
        display_name,
        assigned_name,
        assigned_name_source,
        name_change_count,
        confusion_count,
        last_confusion_turn,
        rhyme_mode,
        rhyme_turns_remaining,
        rhyme_started_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?,
        ?,
        NULL,
        NULL,
        0,
        0,
        -999,
        0,
        0,
        NULL,
        ?,
        ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
      now,
      now
    )
    .run();

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_nameless_identity

      WHERE visitor_id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM NAME
// =========================================================

function getRandomRecallNamelessNameV13(
  exclude = []
) {
  const exclusions =
    new Set(
      (
        Array.isArray(
          exclude
        )
          ? exclude
          : []
      )
        .map(
          (name) =>
            String(
              name || ""
            )
              .trim()
              .toLowerCase()
        )
        .filter(Boolean)
    );

  const available =
    RECALL_V13_NAME_POOL
      .filter(
        (name) =>
          !exclusions.has(
            name.toLowerCase()
          )
      );

  if (!available.length) {
    return "Cornelius";
  }

  return available[
    Math.floor(
      Math.random() *
      available.length
    )
  ];
}


// =========================================================
// GET CURRENT NAMELESS IDENTITY
// =========================================================

async function getRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  return await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );
}


// =========================================================
// ASSIGN NAMELESS A NAME
//
// The box/UI can display this name.
//
// Assignment may happen deliberately or through the
// misunderstanding mechanic.
//
// It does NOT destroy his underlying identity as Nameless.
// =========================================================

async function setRecallNamelessDisplayNameV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  newName,
  {
    sourceText = "",
    reason = "assigned"
  } = {}
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const normalizedName =
    normalizeString(
      newName,
      "",
      200
    );

  if (!normalizedName) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "NAME_REQUIRED"
    };
  }

  const oldName =
    normalizeString(
      state?.display_name,
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
      200
    );

  if (
    oldName.toLowerCase() ===
    normalizedName.toLowerCase()
  ) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      changed: false,
      displayName:
        oldName
    };
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db.batch([
    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

      SET
        display_name = ?,
        assigned_name = ?,
        assigned_name_source = ?,
        name_change_count =
          name_change_count + 1,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
      .bind(
        normalizedName,
        normalizedName,
        normalizeString(
          sourceText,
          "",
          4000
        ) ||
          null,
        now,
        visitorId
      ),

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_nameless_name_history (
        visitor_id,
        old_name,
        new_name,
        source_text,
        reason,
        created_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
      )
    `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        oldName,
        normalizedName,
        normalizeString(
          sourceText,
          "",
          4000
        ) ||
          null,
        normalizeString(
          reason,
          "assigned",
          200
        ),
        now
      )
  ]);

  return {
    ok: true,
    changed: true,
    oldName,
    displayName:
      normalizedName
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RESET DISPLAY NAME
//
// This does not erase name history.
// =========================================================

async function resetRecallNamelessDisplayNameV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  reason = "reset"
) {
  return await setRecallNamelessDisplayNameV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
    {
      reason
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NAME HISTORY
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallNamelessNameHistoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_NAME_HISTORY_LIMIT
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_nameless_name_history

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          created_at DESC,
          id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,

        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            limit,
            RECALL_V13_NAME_HISTORY_LIMIT
          ),
          1,
          200
        )
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// NAME EXTRACTION
//
// Deliberately conservative.
//
// Capitalized multi-word expressions and obvious
// single-name references become candidates.
//
// This is not intended to be perfect NER.
// The model receives the final ambiguity instructions.
// =========================================================

function extractRecallNameCandidatesV13(
  text
) {
  const source =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      60000
    );

  if (!source) {
    return [];
  }

  const candidates =
    [];

  const seen =
    new Set();

  const blocked =
    new Set([
      "The Nameless Man",
      "Nameless Man",
      "Nameless",
      "I",
      "You",
      "He",
      "She",
      "They",
      "We",
      "It",
      "What",
      "Why",
      "When",
      "Where",
      "Who",
      "How",
      "This",
      "That"
    ]);

  const multiWord =
    source.match(
      /\b[A-Z][a-zA-Z'-]{1,30}(?:\s+[A-Z][a-zA-Z'-]{1,30}){1,3}\b/g
    ) ||
    [];

  for (
    const candidate
    of multiWord
  ) {
    const normalized =
      candidate.trim();

    if (
      blocked.has(
        normalized
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const key =
      normalized.toLowerCase();

    if (
      seen.has(
        key
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(
      key
    );

    candidates.push(
      normalized
    );
  }

  for (
    const knownName
    of RECALL_V13_NAME_POOL
  ) {
    const regex =
      new RegExp(
        `\\b${knownName.replace(
          /[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,
          "\\$&"
        )}\\b`,
        "i"
      );

    const match =
      source.match(
        regex
      );

    if (!match) {
      continue;
    }

    const actual =
      match[0];

    const key =
      actual.toLowerCase();

    if (
      seen.has(
        key
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(
      key
    );

    candidates.push(
      actual
    );
  }

  return candidates.slice(
    0,
    20
  );
}


// =========================================================
// SIMPLE SENTIMENT / POLARITY HEURISTIC
//
// The model ultimately interprets nuance.
//
// This only selects which confusion branch should be made
// available.
// =========================================================

function classifyRecallNameStatementPolarityV13(
  text
) {
  const source =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      60000
    )
      .toLowerCase();

  const negative =
    [
      "stupid",
      "idiot",
      "moron",
      "pathetic",
      "weak",
      "ugly",
      "coward",
      "cowardly",
      "worthless",
      "useless",
      "terrible",
      "awful",
      "garbage",
      "trash",
      "can't",
      "cannot",
      "barely",
      "hate",
      "horrible",
      "failure",
      "loser",
      "fool"
    ];

  const positive =
    [
      "brilliant",
      "genius",
      "beautiful",
      "handsome",
      "strong",
      "amazing",
      "excellent",
      "great",
      "wonderful",
      "hero",
      "heroic",
      "love",
      "magnificent",
      "profound",
      "wise",
      "intelligent",
      "perfect",
      "impressive"
    ];

  let negativeHits = 0;
  let positiveHits = 0;

  for (
    const word
    of negative
  ) {
    if (
      source.includes(
        word
      )
    ) {
      negativeHits++;
    }
  }

  for (
    const word
    of positive
  ) {
    if (
      source.includes(
        word
      )
    ) {
      positiveHits++;
    }
  }

  if (
    negativeHits >
    positiveHits
  ) {
    return "negative";
  }

  if (
    positiveHits >
    negativeHits
  ) {
    return "positive";
  }

  return "neutral";
}


// =========================================================
// DOES THE NAME LOOK LIKE DIRECT ADDRESS?
//
// Example:
//
// "Ronald, you couldn't tie your shoes."
//
// is much more likely to confuse Nameless than:
//
// "I read a biography of Ronald Reagan yesterday."
// =========================================================

function recallNameLooksLikeDirectAddressV13(
  text,
  name
) {
  const source =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      60000
    );

  const escaped =
    String(
      name || ""
    )
      .replace(
        /[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,
        "\\$&"
      );

  if (!escaped) {
    return false;
  }

  const patterns = [
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${escaped}\\s*,\\s*(you|your|you're|you've|you'll)\\b`,
      "i"
    ),

    new RegExp(
      `^(?:hey\\s+)?${escaped}\\b`,
      "i"
    ),

    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:you are|you're)\\s+${escaped}\\b`,
      "i"
    )
  ];

  return patterns.some(
    (pattern) =>
      pattern.test(
        source
      )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// COMPARISON DETECTION
// =========================================================

function recallNameStatementLooksComparativeV13(
  text
) {
  return /\b(like|unlike|compared|comparison|than|as if|as though|reminds me of|same as|different from)\b/i
    .test(
      normalizeString(
        text,
        "",
        60000
      )
    );
}


// =========================================================
// OBVIOUS THIRD-PERSON REFERENT DETECTION
//
// This creates the third path:
//
// Nameless correctly understands that Ronald Reagan means
// Ronald Reagan.
//
// Even when the confusion mechanic is eligible, it should
// not fire every time.
// =========================================================

function recallNameHasStrongThirdPersonContextV13(
  text,
  name
) {
  const source =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      60000
    );

  const escaped =
    String(
      name || ""
    )
      .replace(
        /[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,
        "\\$&"
      );

  if (!escaped) {
    return false;
  }

  const patterns = [
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:president|actor|writer|author|historian|singer|politician|general|king|queen|emperor|philosopher)\\s+${escaped}\\b`,
      "i"
    ),

    new RegExp(
      `\\b${escaped}\\s+(?:was|is|became|served|wrote|said|did|had|lived|died|presided)\\b`,
      "i"
    ),

    new RegExp(
      `\\babout\\s+${escaped}\\b`,
      "i"
    )
  ];

  return patterns.some(
    (pattern) =>
      pattern.test(
        source
      )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NAME CONFUSION ELIGIBILITY
// =========================================================

function shouldRecallNameConfusionFireV13({
  text,
  name,
  state,
  turnNumber = 0
}) {
  if (!name) {
    return false;
  }

  const normalizedName =
    name
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  const currentName =
    normalizeString(
      state?.display_name,
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
      200
    )
      .toLowerCase();

  if (
    normalizedName ===
    currentName
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const lastConfusionTurn =
    integerNumber(
      state?.last_confusion_turn,
      -999
    );

  if (
    turnNumber -
      lastConfusionTurn <
    RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_COOLDOWN_TURNS
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const directAddress =
    recallNameLooksLikeDirectAddressV13(
      text,
      name
    );

  const comparative =
    recallNameStatementLooksComparativeV13(
      text
    );

  const strongThirdPerson =
    recallNameHasStrongThirdPersonContextV13(
      text,
      name
    );

  let chance =
    RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_BASE_CHANCE;

  if (directAddress) {
    chance =
      RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_DIRECT_ADDRESS_CHANCE;
  } else if (comparative) {
    chance =
      RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_COMPARISON_CHANCE;
  }

  if (
    strongThirdPerson &&
    !directAddress
  ) {
    chance *=
      0.32;
  }

  return (
    Math.random() <
    chance
  );
}


// =========================================================
// CONFUSION INTERPRETATIONS
//
// THREE PRINCIPAL PATHS:
//
// 1. literal self-reference
//
// 2. inverse comparison
//
// 3. correct/deadpan external referent
//
// Path 3 is represented by no confusion event.
//
// Therefore when an event DOES fire we choose primarily
// between literal and inverse.
// =========================================================

function chooseRecallNameConfusionInterpretationV13(
  polarity
) {
  const roll =
    Math.random();

  if (
    polarity ===
    "negative"
  ) {
    if (roll < 0.64) {
      return "literal_self";
    }

    return "inverse_comparison";
  }

  if (
    polarity ===
    "positive"
  ) {
    if (roll < 0.48) {
      return "literal_self";
    }

    return "inverse_comparison";
  }

  if (roll < 0.70) {
    return "literal_self";
  }

  return "inverse_comparison";
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD CONFUSION DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

function buildRecallNameConfusionDirectiveV13({
  externalName,
  interpretation,
  polarity
}) {
  if (
    interpretation ===
    "literal_self"
  ) {
    if (
      polarity ===
      "negative"
    ) {
      return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT:

The audience member just referred to "${externalName}".

For this response, Nameless momentarily interprets the statement as being about HIM.

The negative statement therefore feels like a direct insult.

Respond naturally from that mistaken interpretation.

He may become offended, defensive, incredulous, or irritated.

Do not immediately explain the mechanic.

Allow the misunderstanding to exist long enough for the other speaker or audience member to correct him.

This is an occasional misunderstanding, not permanent stupidity.
`.trim();
    }

    if (
      polarity ===
      "positive"
    ) {
      return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT:

The audience member just referred to "${externalName}".

For this response, Nameless momentarily interprets the praise as being about HIM.

He may accept it sincerely, become unexpectedly touched, become smug, or treat it as a profound compliment.

Do not immediately explain the misunderstanding.

Allow the confusion to become visible naturally.
`.trim();
    }

    return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT:

The audience member just referred to "${externalName}".

For this response, Nameless momentarily believes the reference may be to HIM.

React from that mistaken assumption.

Do not explain the hidden mechanic.
`.trim();
  }

  if (
    interpretation ===
    "inverse_comparison"
  ) {
    if (
      polarity ===
      "negative"
    ) {
      return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT — INVERSE COMPARISON:

The audience member just made a negative statement involving "${externalName}".

Nameless mistakenly interprets this as an indirect comparison with himself.

He thinks the speaker is deliberately describing something obviously unlike him in order to compliment him.

Example logic:

"${externalName} can't tie his shoes."

Nameless may interpret this as:

"Clearly YOU can tie your shoes; I am contrasting you with ${externalName}."

Nameless may therefore respond as if he has just received a strange compliment.

Do not reveal the mechanic.

Let the misunderstanding become apparent through dialogue.
`.trim();
    }

    if (
      polarity ===
      "positive"
    ) {
      return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT — INVERSE COMPARISON:

The audience member just praised "${externalName}".

Nameless mistakenly interprets the compliment as an indirect criticism of himself.

Example logic:

"${externalName} has incredible biceps."

Nameless may hear:

"I am emphasizing that YOU have no biceps."

He may become insulted or suspicious.

Do not explain the mechanic directly.

Let another speaker or the audience member correct the misunderstanding.
`.trim();
    }

    return `
NAME CONFUSION EVENT — INVERSE COMPARISON:

Nameless mistakenly interprets the statement about "${externalName}" as an indirect comparison with himself.

The comparison may flatter or insult him depending on the surrounding meaning.

Do not expose the hidden mechanic.
`.trim();
  }

  return "";
}


// =========================================================
// STORE CONFUSION EVENT
// =========================================================

async function storeRecallNameConfusionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    turnNumber = null,
    externalName,
    externalReferent = null,
    interpretation,
    polarity = "neutral",
    sourceText = ""
  }
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_name_confusions (
          visitor_id,
          turn_number,
          external_name,
          external_referent,
          interpretation,
          polarity,
          source_text,
          repaired,
          repair_text,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (
          ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
          ?, 0, NULL, ?, ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        turnNumber,
        externalName,
        externalReferent,
        interpretation,
        polarity,
        normalizeString(
          sourceText,
          "",
          10000
        ),
        now,
        now
      )
      .run();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

      SET
        confusion_count =
          confusion_count + 1,
        last_confusion_turn = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      integerNumber(
        turnNumber,
        0
      ),
      now,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    id:
      result?.meta?.last_row_id ||
      null,
    externalName,
    interpretation,
    polarity
  };
}


// =========================================================
// ANALYZE MESSAGE FOR NAME CONFUSION
// =========================================================

async function analyzeRecallNameConfusionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage,
  {
    turnNumber = 0
  } = {}
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const names =
    extractRecallNameCandidatesV13(
      userMessage
    );

  if (!names.length) {
    return {
      fired: false,
      names: []
    };
  }

  for (
    const name
    of names
  ) {
    const fired =
      shouldRecallNameConfusionFireV13({
        text:
          userMessage,
        name,
        state,
        turnNumber
      });

    if (!fired) {
      continue;
    }

    const polarity =
      classifyRecallNameStatementPolarityV13(
        userMessage
      );

    const interpretation =
      chooseRecallNameConfusionInterpretationV13(
        polarity
      );

    const event =
      await storeRecallNameConfusionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          turnNumber,
          externalName:
            name,
          externalReferent:
            name,
          interpretation,
          polarity,
          sourceText:
            userMessage
        }
      );

    return {
      fired: true,
      name,
      polarity,
      interpretation,
      event,
      directive:
        buildRecallNameConfusionDirectiveV13({
          externalName:
            name,
          interpretation,
          polarity
        })
    };
  }

  return {
    fired: false,
    names
  };
}


// =========================================================
// OPTIONAL NAME ADOPTION
//
// If the audience member directly addresses Nameless by a
// different name, there is a small chance he simply accepts
// it.
//
// This changes the name displayed in his box.
//
// Example:
//
// "Anderson, what do you think?"
//
// Nameless may become ANDERSON.
//
// This is separate from ordinary confusion.
// =========================================================

async function maybeAdoptRecallNamelessNameV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const names =
    extractRecallNameCandidatesV13(
      userMessage
    );

  for (
    const name
    of names
  ) {
    if (
      !recallNameLooksLikeDirectAddressV13(
        userMessage,
        name
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const current =
      normalizeString(
        state?.display_name,
        RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
        200
      );

    if (
      current.toLowerCase() ===
      name.toLowerCase()
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      Math.random() >
      0.34
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    return await setRecallNamelessDisplayNameV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      name,
      {
        sourceText:
          userMessage,

        reason:
          "accepted_direct_address"
      }
    );
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    changed: false,
    displayName:
      state?.display_name ||
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME
  };
}


// =========================================================
// CONFUSION REPAIR DETECTION
//
// Example:
//
// "No, I meant Ronald Reagan."
//
// "I wasn't talking about you."
//
// "I meant the president."
//
// The correction should be allowed to become part of the
// comedy instead of Nameless remaining permanently confused.
// =========================================================

function recallV13LooksLikeConfusionRepair(
  text
) {
  return /\b(no[, ]+i meant|i meant the|wasn't talking about you|was not talking about you|not you|i'm talking about|i am talking about|i meant him|i meant her|the president|the actor|the writer|the other one)\b/i
    .test(
      normalizeString(
        text,
        "",
        60000
      )
    );
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD MOST RECENT UNREPAIRED CONFUSION
// =========================================================

async function loadRecentUnrepairedNameConfusionV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_name_confusions

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND repaired = 0

      ORDER BY
        created_at DESC,
        id DESC

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// REPAIR CONFUSION
// =========================================================

async function maybeRepairRecallNameConfusionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage
) {
  if (
    !recallV13LooksLikeConfusionRepair(
      userMessage
    )
  ) {
    return {
      repaired: false
    };
  }

  const confusion =
    await loadRecentUnrepairedNameConfusionV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  if (!confusion) {
    return {
      repaired: false
    };
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_name_confusions

      SET
        repaired = 1,
        repair_text = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      normalizeString(
        userMessage,
        "",
        10000
      ),
      now,
      confusion.id
    )
    .run();

  return {
    repaired: true,
    confusion,

    directive:
      `
CONFUSION CORRECTION:

The audience member has clarified that the previous reference to "${confusion.external_name}" was NOT a reference to Nameless.

Nameless now understands the correction.

React naturally.

He may be embarrassed, annoyed, amused, defensive, or attempt to recover his dignity.

Do not continue pretending the corrected referent is Nameless unless a new misunderstanding occurs.
`.trim()
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME REQUEST DETECTION
// =========================================================

function recallV13RequestsRhyme(
  text
) {
  const source =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      60000
    );

  return (
    /\b(in rhyme|in rhymes|reply in rhyme|respond in rhyme|answer in rhyme|speak in rhyme|talk in rhyme|rhyme your reply|rhyme your response|do it in rhyme)\b/i
      .test(
        source
      ) ||
    /\byou gotta do it in rhyme\b/i
      .test(
        source
      )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME MODE STATE
//
// Nameless ALWAYS accepts a clear rhyme request.
//
// It persists for several Nameless statements rather than
// ending after one reply.
// =========================================================

async function activateRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  turns = null
) {
  await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const selectedTurns =
    turns === null
      ? (
          RECALL_V13_RHYME_MIN_TURNS +
          Math.floor(
            Math.random() *
            (
              RECALL_V13_RHYME_MAX_TURNS -
              RECALL_V13_RHYME_MIN_TURNS +
              1
            )
          )
        )
      : clamp(
          integerNumber(
            turns,
            RECALL_V13_RHYME_DEFAULT_TURNS
          ),
          RECALL_V13_RHYME_MIN_TURNS,
          RECALL_V13_RHYME_MAX_TURNS
        );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

      SET
        rhyme_mode = 1,
        rhyme_turns_remaining = ?,
        rhyme_started_at = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      selectedTurns,
      now,
      now,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    active: true,
    turnsRemaining:
      selectedTurns
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME MODE DECREMENT
//
// Call after Nameless actually produces a response.
// =========================================================

async function consumeRecallNamelessRhymeTurnV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  if (
    integerNumber(
      state?.rhyme_mode,
      0
    ) !== 1
  ) {
    return {
      active: false,
      turnsRemaining: 0
    };
  }

  const remaining =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        state?.rhyme_turns_remaining,
        0
      ) - 1
    );

  const active =
    remaining > 0;

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

      SET
        rhyme_mode = ?,
        rhyme_turns_remaining = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      active
        ? 1
        : 0,
      remaining,
      now,
      visitorId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    active,
    turnsRemaining:
      remaining
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME MODE CHECK
// =========================================================

async function getRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return {
    active:
      integerNumber(
        state?.rhyme_mode,
        0
      ) === 1,

    turnsRemaining:
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          state?.rhyme_turns_remaining,
          0
        )
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_RHYME_DIRECTIVE = `
RHYME MODE IS ACTIVE.

Nameless accepted the audience member's request to reply in rhyme.

Nameless should continue speaking in rhyme for this response.

This is not merely one rhyming sentence.

Maintain rhyme across the substantive reply.

The rhyme can be:

- couplets
- loose rhyme
- internal rhyme
- slant rhyme
- rap-like rhyme
- comic rhyme
- aggressive rhyme
- elaborate poetic rhyme

depending on the conversation.

Meaning remains more important than perfect meter.

Do not reduce Nameless to nursery-rhyme simplicity.

He remains intelligent, argumentative, confrontational and capable of serious analysis while rhyming.

He may acknowledge that the audience member successfully forced the show into rhyme.

Once rhyme mode naturally expires after several Nameless turns, ordinary speech may resume.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// ACTIVATE RHYME FROM MESSAGE
// =========================================================

async function processRecallRhymeRequestV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage
) {
  if (
    recallV13RequestsRhyme(
      userMessage
    )
  ) {
    const activation =
      await activateRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    return {
      requested: true,
      ...activation,
      directive:
        RECALL_V13_RHYME_DIRECTIVE
    };
  }

  const current =
    await getRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return {
    requested: false,
    ...current,

    directive:
      current.active
        ? RECALL_V13_RHYME_DIRECTIVE
        : ""
  };
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD NAMELESS IDENTITY CONTEXT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallNamelessIdentityContextV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const [
    state,
    history
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadRecallNamelessNameHistoryV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        10
      )
    ]);

  const currentName =
    normalizeString(
      state?.display_name,
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
      200
    );

  const lines = [
    "NAMELESS IDENTITY CONTINUITY:",
    "",
    `Current displayed name: ${currentName}`,
    "",
    "The underlying character remains the Nameless Man regardless of the displayed name.",
    "",
    "Other names may be accepted, misunderstood, assigned, rejected, forgotten, or become running jokes."
  ];

  if (history.length) {
    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    lines.push(
      "RECENT NAME HISTORY:"
    );

    for (
      const item
      of history
    ) {
      lines.push(
        `- ${normalizeString(
          item.old_name,
          RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
          200
        )} -> ${normalizeString(
          item.new_name,
          "",
          200
        )}` +
        (
          item.reason
            ? ` (${item.reason})`
            : ""
        )
      );
    }
  }

  return {
    state,
    history,
    currentName,
    text:
      lines.join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// PROCESS NAME / RHYME TURN
//
// This is the single high-level entry point Part 6 can use.
// =========================================================

async function processRecallNamelessTurnMechanicsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage,
  {
    turnNumber = 0
  } = {}
) {
  await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const repair =
    await maybeRepairRecallNameConfusionV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      userMessage
    );

  const rhyme =
    await processRecallRhymeRequestV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      userMessage
    );

  let adoption = {
    ok: true,
    changed: false
  };

  let confusion = {
    fired: false
  };

  if (!repair.repaired) {
    adoption =
      await maybeAdoptRecallNamelessNameV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        userMessage
      );

    confusion =
      await analyzeRecallNameConfusionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        userMessage,
        {
          turnNumber
        }
      );
  }

  const identity =
    await buildRecallNamelessIdentityContextV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const directives =
    [
      identity.text,

      repair?.directive ||
        "",

      confusion?.directive ||
        "",

      rhyme?.directive ||
        ""
    ]
      .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    identity,
    repair,
    adoption,
    confusion,
    rhyme,

    text:
      directives.join(
        "\n\n==============================\n\n"
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// NAME CONFUSION MASTER DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_MASTER_DIRECTIVE = `
NAME / REFERENT AMBIGUITY:

The Nameless Man can be called almost anything.

Because of that, names in conversation sometimes create referential confusion.

This is OCCASIONAL.

Do not make Nameless misunderstand every proper name.

Most of the time, ordinary names refer to ordinary external people.

Sometimes, however, Nameless mistakenly believes another name refers to himself.

This can produce both comedy and serious misunderstanding.

THREE IMPORTANT OUTCOMES EXIST:

1. LITERAL MISREAD

A statement about another named person is interpreted as literally describing Nameless.

Example:

"Ronald Reagan can barely tie his shoes."

Nameless may react:

"OH COME ON. Do we really have to throw insults at me?"

2. INVERSE COMPARISON

Nameless realizes another person was named, but mistakenly thinks the speaker is using that person as a comparison with him.

A negative statement about the named person can therefore be interpreted as a compliment to Nameless.

A positive statement about the named person can therefore be interpreted as an insult to Nameless.

3. CORRECT REFERENT

Nameless understands perfectly well that the speaker meant the external person.

Nothing unusual happens.

THIS THIRD OUTCOME MUST REMAIN COMMON.

The mechanic is funny because it is unpredictable, not because Nameless is incapable of understanding names.

SERIOUS CONSEQUENCES:

The misunderstanding is not limited to jokes.

A profound speech about another named person can occasionally be interpreted as a profound speech about Nameless.

He may become sincerely moved.

He may remember the apparent compliment.

He may later discover it was never about him.

That discovery may itself become relationship history.

CORRECTIONS:

When somebody says:

"No, I meant the American president."

or otherwise clearly corrects the referent, Nameless should understand.

He may react to the embarrassment.

Do not trap the conversation permanently inside the misunderstanding.

DISPLAY NAME:

When Nameless accepts another name as his own, the visible speaker name may change to that name.

The underlying character remains the Nameless Man.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// COMPLETE PART-5 INJECTION
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallPart5InjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  userMessage,
  {
    turnNumber = 0
  } = {}
) {
  const mechanics =
    await processRecallNamelessTurnMechanicsV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      userMessage,
      {
        turnNumber
      }
    );

  return {
    ...mechanics,

    text:
      [
        RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_MASTER_DIRECTIVE,
        "",
        "==============================",
        "",
        mechanics.text
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join("\n")
  };
}


// =========================================================
// NAME CONFUSION MEMORY PROMOTION
//
// Particularly meaningful misunderstandings can later be
// promoted into ordinary long-term memory.
//
// This is NOT called automatically for every joke.
//
// Part 6 / later analysis can decide whether an event had
// enough emotional or narrative importance to preserve.
// =========================================================

async function promoteRecallNameConfusionToMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  confusionId,
  {
    summary = "",
    importance = 45
  } = {}
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const confusion =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_name_confusions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        confusionId
      )
      .first();

  if (!confusion) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "CONFUSION_NOT_FOUND"
    };
  }

  const generatedSummary =
    normalizeString(
      summary,
      "",
      6000
    ) ||
    (
      `Nameless misunderstood a reference to "${confusion.external_name}" ` +
      `as involving himself (${confusion.interpretation}).`
    );

  const memory =
    await createRecallMemoryV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        memoryType:
          RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.EVENT,

        subjectKey:
          recallV13NormalizeKey(
            confusion.external_name
          ),

        summary:
          generatedSummary,

        detail:
          normalizeString(
            confusion.source_text,
            "",
            10000
          ),

        importance:
          normalizeRecallV13Importance(
            importance,
            45
          ),

        confidence:
          1,

        reinforcementCount:
          1,

        isFoundational:
          false
      }
    );

  return {
    ok: true,
    memory
  };
}


// =========================================================
// PART 5 STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallV13Part5Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const state =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const [
    historyCount,
    confusionCount,
    unrepairedCount
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_nameless_name_history

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_name_confusions

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),

      db.prepare(`
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_name_confusions

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND repaired = 0
      `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first()
    ]);

  return {
    displayName:
      state?.display_name ||
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,

    nameChanges:
      integerNumber(
        historyCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    confusionEvents:
      integerNumber(
        confusionCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    unrepairedConfusions:
      integerNumber(
        unrepairedCount?.count,
        0
      ),

    rhymeMode:
      integerNumber(
        state?.rhyme_mode,
        0
      ) === 1,

    rhymeTurnsRemaining:
      integerNumber(
        state?.rhyme_turns_remaining,
        0
      ),

    availableRandomNames:
      RECALL_V13_NAME_POOL.length,

    part5Installed:
      true
  };
}


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT INTEGRATION NOTE
//
// PART 5 establishes STATE + BEHAVIOR.
//
// Part 6 will connect these systems to:
//
// - near-unlimited user input handling
// - long-message / novel-sized input
// - conversation analysis
// - memory extraction
// - combat interruption damage
// - generation preparation
//
// Later router surgery will connect the current displayName
// to the actual UI speaker-name box.
//
// After Nameless produces a response:
//
// await consumeRecallNamelessRhymeTurnV13(
//   env,
//   visitorId
// );
//
// should be called ONLY when Nameless actually spoke.
//
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// END RECALL THY NAME V13 — PART 5 OF 8
//
// INSTALLED:
//
// - persistent Nameless display-name state
// - large conventional/historical name pool
// - name history
// - direct-address name adoption
// - proper-name candidate extraction
// - occasional referent confusion
// - confusion cooldown
// - literal self-reference misunderstanding
// - negative statement -> insult misunderstanding
// - positive statement -> compliment misunderstanding
// - inverse-comparison misunderstanding
// - positive comparison -> possible insult
// - negative comparison -> possible compliment
// - common correct-referent path
// - explicit confusion repair
// - meaningful confusion memory promotion
// - persistent rhyme mode
// - rhyme request always accepted
// - 3–8 Nameless-turn rhyme persistence
// - intelligent / argumentative rhyme directive
// - single high-level Part-5 turn processor
//
// PART 6:
//
// LARGE / NEAR-UNLIMITED USER INPUT PIPELINE
// NOVEL-SIZED CHAT INPUT
// SAFE INPUT CHUNKING
// LONG-INPUT MODEL PREPARATION
// MEMORY EXTRACTION PIPELINE
// MEMORY REINFORCEMENT
// CONTRADICTION DETECTION
// PATTERN EVIDENCE
// NAMELESS JUDGMENT EVOLUTION
// COMBAT-PHASE INTERRUPTION DAMAGE
//
// APPEND PART 6 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 6 OF 8
//
// LARGE / NEAR-UNLIMITED USER INPUT PIPELINE
// NOVEL-SIZED CHAT INPUT
// PERSISTENT LONG-INPUT STORAGE
// LONG-INPUT CHUNKING
// MODEL-SAFE DIGESTS
// LARGE-INPUT MEMORY
// COMBAT-PHASE INTERRUPTION DAMAGE
// RHYME-TURN CONSUMPTION PRIMITIVES
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART DIRECTLY AFTER V13 PART 5.
//
// IMPORTANT:
//
// V12 currently limits ordinary audience input to 12,000
// characters.
//
// We do NOT destructively rewrite that stable endpoint here.
//
// Instead:
//
// - ordinary messages <= 12,000 characters travel through
//   V12 unchanged;
//
// - larger messages are stored IN FULL in V13;
//
// - the full message is chunked for persistent retrieval;
//
// - a model-safe digest is generated;
//
// - V12 receives that digest as the immediate audience turn;
//
// - the original enormous submission remains permanently
//   available for later V13 retrieval.
//
// This lets somebody paste a novel without pretending that
// every megabyte can safely be shoved into every later prompt.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT =
  12_000;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES =
  3 * 1024 * 1024;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CHUNK_TARGET =
  300_000;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CHUNK_OVERLAP =
  8_000;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_CHUNKS =
  32;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_SUMMARY_OUTPUT_TOKENS =
  2400;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_FINAL_DIGEST_CHARACTERS =
  10_500;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT =
  8;

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS =
  18_000;


// =========================================================
// COMBAT INTERRUPTION CONFIGURATION
//
// One combat interruption should matter without becoming an
// instant audience kill button.
//
// Initial rule:
//
// approximately 2.5% of target maximum HP,
// minimum 1 damage.
//
// Easy to tune later.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_DAMAGE_RATIO =
  0.025;

const RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_MIN_DAMAGE =
  1;

const RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_MAX_DAMAGE_RATIO =
  0.05;


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT DATABASE
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallLargeInputSchemaV13(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_large_inputs_v13 (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        input_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        episode_number INTEGER,

        turn_number INTEGER,

        original_character_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        original_byte_size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        full_text TEXT NOT NULL,

        model_digest TEXT,

        content_hash TEXT,

        chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        processed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_large_inputs_v13_visitor
      ON recall_large_inputs_v13(
        visitor_id,
        id DESC
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_large_inputs_v13_hash
      ON recall_large_inputs_v13(
        visitor_id,
        content_hash
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_large_input_chunks_v13 (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        input_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        chunk_index INTEGER NOT NULL,

        character_start INTEGER NOT NULL,

        character_end INTEGER NOT NULL,

        content TEXT NOT NULL,

        summary TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          input_id,
          chunk_index
        )
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_large_input_chunks_v13_input
      ON recall_large_input_chunks_v13(
        input_id,
        chunk_index
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_large_input_chunks_v13_visitor
      ON recall_large_input_chunks_v13(
        visitor_id,
        id DESC
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_combat_interruptions_v13 (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        episode_number INTEGER,

        turn_number INTEGER,

        target_character TEXT,

        damage INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        target_hp_before REAL,

        target_hp_after REAL,

        target_max_hp REAL,

        killed_target INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        invincibility_triggered INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        source_character_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_combat_interruptions_v13
      ON recall_combat_interruptions_v13(
        visitor_id,
        id DESC
      )
    `)
  ]);

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// INITIALIZE PART 6
// =========================================================

async function initializeRecallPart6V13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await ensureRecallLargeInputSchemaV13(
    env
  );

  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT BYTE SIZE
// =========================================================

function recallV13InputByteSize(
  value
) {
  const text =
    typeof value ===
      "string"
      ? value
      : String(
          value ||
          ""
        );

  try {
    return new TextEncoder()
      .encode(
        text
      )
      .byteLength;
  } catch {
    return text.length;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// CREATE LARGE INPUT ID
// =========================================================

function createRecallLargeInputIdV13()
{
  if (
    typeof crypto !==
      "undefined" &&
    typeof crypto.randomUUID ===
      "function"
  ) {
    return (
      "large-input-" +
      crypto.randomUUID()
    );
  }

  return (
    "large-input-" +
    Date.now() +
    "-" +
    Math.random()
      .toString(36)
      .slice(2)
  );
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT HASH
// =========================================================

async function hashRecallLargeInputV13(
  text
) {
  try {
    const bytes =
      new TextEncoder()
        .encode(
          text
        );

    const digest =
      await crypto.subtle.digest(
        "SHA-256",
        bytes
      );

    return Array.from(
      new Uint8Array(
        digest
      )
    )
      .map(
        (byte) =>
          byte
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(
              2,
              "0"
            )
      )
      .join("");
  } catch {
    return (
      recallV13NormalizeKey(
        text.slice(
          0,
          10000
        )
      ) +
      "-" +
      text.length
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// VALIDATE AUDIENCE INPUT
// =========================================================

function validateRecallAudienceInputV13(
  value
) {
  const text =
    typeof value ===
      "string"
      ? value
      : String(
          value ||
          ""
        );

  const trimmed =
    text.trim();

  if (!trimmed) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "AUDIENCE_INPUT_EMPTY"
    };
  }

  const byteSize =
    recallV13InputByteSize(
      text
    );

  if (
    byteSize >
    RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES
  ) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "AUDIENCE_INPUT_TOO_LARGE",

      maximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES,

      actualBytes:
        byteSize
    };
  }

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    text,

    characterCount:
      text.length,

    byteSize,

    large:
      text.length >
      RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT CHUNKING
// =========================================================

function chunkRecallLargeInputV13(
  text
) {
  const source =
    String(
      text ||
      ""
    );

  if (!source) {
    return [];
  }

  const chunks =
    [];

  let cursor =
    0;

  while (
    cursor <
      source.length &&
    chunks.length <
      RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_CHUNKS
  ) {
    const desiredEnd =
      Math.min(
        source.length,
        cursor +
          RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CHUNK_TARGET
      );

    let end =
      desiredEnd;

    if (
      desiredEnd <
      source.length
    ) {
      const floor =
        Math.max(
          cursor +
            Math.floor(
              RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CHUNK_TARGET *
              0.65
            ),
          cursor + 1
        );

      const candidate =
        source.slice(
          floor,
          desiredEnd
        );

      const paragraph =
        candidate.lastIndexOf(
          "\n\n"
        );

      if (
        paragraph >=
        0
      ) {
        end =
          floor +
          paragraph +
          2;
      } else {
        const newline =
          candidate.lastIndexOf(
            "\n"
          );

        if (
          newline >=
          0
        ) {
          end =
            floor +
            newline +
            1;
        } else {
          const sentenceMatches =
            [
              ...candidate.matchAll(
                /[.!?]["')\]]?\s+/g
              )
            ];

          if (
            sentenceMatches.length
          ) {
            const match =
              sentenceMatches[
                sentenceMatches.length -
                1
              ];

            end =
              floor +
              match.index +
              match[0].length;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (
      end <=
      cursor
    ) {
      end =
        desiredEnd;
    }

    const content =
      source
        .slice(
          cursor,
          end
        )
        .trim();

    if (content) {
      chunks.push({
        index:
          chunks.length,

        start:
          cursor,

        end,

        content
      });
    }

    if (
      end >=
      source.length
    ) {
      break;
    }

    cursor =
      Math.max(
        cursor + 1,
        end -
          RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CHUNK_OVERLAP
      );
  }

  return chunks;
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE LARGE INPUT IN FULL
// =========================================================

async function saveRecallLargeInputV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  text
) {
  await initializeRecallPart6V13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const validation =
    validateRecallAudienceInputV13(
      text
    );

  if (!validation.ok) {
    return validation;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const contentHash =
    await hashRecallLargeInputV13(
      validation.text
    );

  const existing =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_large_inputs_v13

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND content_hash = ?

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        contentHash
      )
      .first();

  if (existing) {
    return {
      ok:
        true,

      duplicate:
        true,

      inputId:
        existing.input_id,

      characterCount:
        existing.original_character_count,

      byteSize:
        existing.original_byte_size,

      chunkCount:
        existing.chunk_count,

      modelDigest:
        existing.model_digest ||
        null
    };
  }

  const inputId =
    createRecallLargeInputIdV13();

  const chunks =
    chunkRecallLargeInputV13(
      validation.text
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_large_inputs_v13 (
        input_id,
        visitor_id,
        episode_number,
        turn_number,
        original_character_count,
        original_byte_size,
        full_text,
        model_digest,
        content_hash,
        chunk_count,
        processed,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?,
        NULL,
        ?,
        ?,
        0,
        ?, ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      inputId,

      visitorId,

      state
        ?.watch
        ?.episodesWatched ??
        null,

      state
        ?.conversation
        ?.turnNumber ??
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.currentEpisodeTurnNumber ??
        null,

      validation.characterCount,

      validation.byteSize,

      validation.text,

      contentHash,

      chunks.length,

      now,

      now
    )
    .run();

  const statements =
    chunks.map(
      (chunk) =>
        db
          .prepare(`
            INSERT INTO recall_large_input_chunks_v13 (
              input_id,
              visitor_id,
              chunk_index,
              character_start,
              character_end,
              content,
              summary,
              created_at,
              updated_at
            )

            VALUES (
              ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
              ?, NULL, ?, ?
            )
          `)
          .bind(
            inputId,
            visitorId,
            chunk.index,
            chunk.start,
            chunk.end,
            chunk.content,
            now,
            now
          )
    );

  const batchSize =
    40;

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index <
      statements.length;
    index +=
      batchSize
  ) {
    await db.batch(
      statements.slice(
        index,
        index +
          batchSize
      )
    );
  }

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    duplicate:
      false,

    inputId,

    characterCount:
      validation.characterCount,

    byteSize:
      validation.byteSize,

    chunkCount:
      chunks.length,

    modelDigest:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD LARGE INPUT
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallLargeInputV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  inputId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  return await db
    .prepare(`
      SELECT *

      FROM recall_large_inputs_v13

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND input_id = ?

      LIMIT 1
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      inputId
    )
    .first();
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD LARGE INPUT CHUNKS
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallLargeInputChunksV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  inputId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT *

        FROM recall_large_input_chunks_v13

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND input_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          chunk_index ASC
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        inputId
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT ANALYST
//
// The full text remains stored verbatim.
//
// The digest is NOT a replacement for the source.
//
// Its purpose is only to let the immediate television turn
// safely understand extremely large input.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_ANALYST_PROMPT = `
You are compressing a very large audience submission for another AI character.

Preserve the audience member's actual meaning.

Do not sanitize disagreement.
Do not invent facts.
Do not rewrite the person into a different viewpoint.
Do not psychoanalyze beyond what is explicitly present.

Preserve:

- questions they actually ask;
- arguments;
- claims;
- examples;
- names;
- people being discussed;
- emotional tone;
- important qualifications;
- jokes;
- contradictions inside the submission;
- instructions directed at the television characters;
- requests for form such as rhyme;
- quoted phrases when exact wording is important.

If the text contains a final explicit question, make that question unmistakable.

If this is one section of a larger submission, summarize THIS SECTION faithfully.

Output a dense but readable continuity digest.

Do not respond to the audience member.

Do not answer their questions.

Only describe what they submitted.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// SUMMARIZE ONE LARGE CHUNK
// =========================================================

async function summarizeRecallLargeInputChunkV13(
  env,
  chunk,
  chunkIndex,
  totalChunks
) {
  const input =
    `
LARGE AUDIENCE SUBMISSION
CHUNK ${chunkIndex + 1} OF ${totalChunks}

BEGIN CHUNK:

${chunk}

END CHUNK.

Produce a faithful dense digest of this chunk.
`.trim();

  return await callRecallModel(
    env,
    {
      instructions:
        RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_ANALYST_PROMPT,

      input,

      webSearchEnabled:
        false,

      maxOutputTokens:
        RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_SUMMARY_OUTPUT_TOKENS
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FINAL LARGE INPUT DIGEST
// =========================================================

async function condenseRecallLargeInputSummariesV13(
  env,
  summaries,
  originalCharacterCount
) {
  const joined =
    summaries
      .map(
        (
          summary,
          index
        ) =>
          `SECTION ${index + 1}:\n${summary}`
      )
      .join(
        "\n\n"
      );

  const input =
    `
The audience submitted approximately ${originalCharacterCount.toLocaleString()} characters.

The submission was divided into sections.

SECTION DIGESTS:

${joined}

Create ONE unified digest for the television character who must respond now.

Preserve the final question or request with exceptional care.

Preserve explicit instructions such as requests to answer in rhyme.

Do not answer the submission.

Do not add interpretation that is not supported.

Maximum practical density is preferred.
`.trim();

  const result =
    await callRecallModel(
      env,
      {
        instructions:
          RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_ANALYST_PROMPT,

        input,

        webSearchEnabled:
          false,

        maxOutputTokens:
          RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_SUMMARY_OUTPUT_TOKENS
      }
    );

  return normalizeString(
    result,
    "",
    RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_FINAL_DIGEST_CHARACTERS
  );
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD DIGEST FOR STORED LARGE INPUT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallLargeInputDigestV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  inputId
) {
  const inputRecord =
    await loadRecallLargeInputV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      inputId
    );

  if (!inputRecord) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "LARGE_INPUT_NOT_FOUND"
    };
  }

  if (
    inputRecord.model_digest
  ) {
    return {
      ok:
        true,

      cached:
        true,

      inputId,

      digest:
        inputRecord.model_digest
    };
  }

  const chunks =
    await loadRecallLargeInputChunksV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      inputId
    );

  if (!chunks.length) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "LARGE_INPUT_HAS_NO_CHUNKS"
    };
  }

  const summaries =
    [];

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index < chunks.length;
    index++
  ) {
    const chunk =
      chunks[index];

    let summary =
      normalizeString(
        chunk.summary,
        "",
        12000
      );

    if (!summary) {
      summary =
        await summarizeRecallLargeInputChunkV13(
          env,
          chunk.content,
          index,
          chunks.length
        );

      const db =
        requireWorldDatabase(
          env
        );

      await db
        .prepare(`
          UPDATE recall_large_input_chunks_v13

          SET
            summary = ?,
            updated_at = ?

          WHERE id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          normalizeString(
            summary,
            "",
            12000
          ),
          isoNow(),
          chunk.id
        )
        .run();
    }

    summaries.push(
      summary
    );
  }

  let digest;

  if (
    summaries.length ===
      1
  ) {
    digest =
      normalizeString(
        summaries[0],
        "",
        RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_FINAL_DIGEST_CHARACTERS
      );
  } else {
    digest =
      await condenseRecallLargeInputSummariesV13(
        env,
        summaries,
        integerNumber(
          inputRecord.original_character_count,
          0
        )
      );
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_large_inputs_v13

      SET
        model_digest = ?,
        processed = 1,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND input_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      digest,
      isoNow(),
      visitorId,
      inputId
    )
    .run();

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    cached:
      false,

    inputId,

    digest
  };
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD MODEL MESSAGE FROM LARGE INPUT
//
// This MUST remain below V12's 12,000-character audience
// limit because V12 still owns the stable response endpoint.
// =========================================================

function buildRecallLargeInputModelMessageV13(
  inputId,
  originalCharacterCount,
  digest
) {
  const header =
    `
[VERY LARGE AUDIENCE SUBMISSION]

The audience member submitted ${originalCharacterCount.toLocaleString()} characters.

The original submission is permanently stored under:
${inputId}

The following is a faithful processing digest of that complete submission.

Respond to the audience member as though this digest represents the large submission they just gave you.

Do NOT complain that the message was too long.

Do NOT claim they only sent a summary.

DIGEST:
`.trim();

  const available =
    Math.max(
      1000,
      RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT -
        header.length -
        300
    );

  const body =
    normalizeString(
      digest,
      "",
      available
    );

  return (
    header +
    "\n\n" +
    body
  )
    .slice(
      0,
      RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT -
        20
    );
}


// =========================================================
// LONG INPUT MEMORY
//
// A large submission is itself a memorable event.
//
// The digest becomes searchable memory while the entire raw
// source remains preserved separately.
// =========================================================

async function rememberRecallLargeInputV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  inputId,
  digest,
  characterCount
) {
  return await saveOrReinforceRecallMemoryV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      characterKey:
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

      memoryType:
        RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.EVENT,

      subjectKey:
        "large-audience-submission",

      summary:
        (
          `The audience member submitted a very large message ` +
          `of approximately ${characterCount.toLocaleString()} characters.`
        ),

      detail:
        normalizeString(
          digest,
          "",
          RECALL_V13_MEMORY_DETAIL_MAX_CHARACTERS
        ),

      sourceType:
        "large_input_v13",

      sourceId:
        inputId,

      importance:
        characterCount >=
          500_000
          ? 72
          : 55,

      confidence:
        1,

      tags: [
        "large-input",
        "audience-submission"
      ],

      metadata: {
        inputId,
        originalCharacterCount:
          characterCount
      }
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// SEARCH LARGE INPUTS
//
// Later conversations can retrieve relevant pieces of an
// enormous message rather than relying only on its digest.
// =========================================================

async function searchRecallLargeInputsV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query,
  limit =
    RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          c.*,
          i.original_character_count,
          i.model_digest,
          i.created_at AS input_created_at

        FROM recall_large_input_chunks_v13 c

        JOIN recall_large_inputs_v13 i
          ON i.input_id =
             c.input_id

        WHERE
          c.visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY
          c.id DESC

        LIMIT 200
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .all();

  const rows =
    Array.isArray(
      result?.results
    )
      ? result.results
      : [];

  return rows
    .map(
      (row) => ({
        ...row,

        retrievalScore:
          Math.max(
            recallV13TextSimilarity(
              query,
              row.content
            ),

            recallV13TextSimilarity(
              query,
              row.summary ||
              ""
            )
          )
      })
    )
    .sort(
      (left, right) =>
        right.retrievalScore -
        left.retrievalScore
    )
    .slice(
      0,
      clamp(
        integerNumber(
          limit,
          RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
        ),
        1,
        30
      )
    );
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT RECALL CONTEXT
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallLargeInputContextV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  query
) {
  const matches =
    await searchRecallLargeInputsV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      query,
      RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
    );

  const relevant =
    matches.filter(
      (item) =>
        item.retrievalScore >=
        0.12
    );

  if (!relevant.length) {
    return {
      matches:
        [],

      text:
        ""
    };
  }

  const lines = [
    "RELEVANT MATERIAL FROM PREVIOUS VERY LARGE AUDIENCE SUBMISSIONS:",
    "",
    "These passages come from the audience member's own previously submitted text.",
    "Use them only when relevant.",
    ""
  ];

  let length =
    lines.join(
      "\n"
    ).length;

  for (
    const item
    of relevant
  ) {
    const block =
      [
        `LARGE INPUT: ${item.input_id}`,
        `CHUNK: ${integerNumber(
          item.chunk_index,
          0
        ) + 1}`,
        `RELEVANCE: ${finiteNumber(
          item.retrievalScore,
          0
        ).toFixed(3)}`,
        "",
        normalizeString(
          item.content,
          "",
          7000
        )
      ]
        .join(
          "\n"
        );

    if (
      length +
        block.length >
      RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
    ) {
      break;
    }

    lines.push(
      block
    );

    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    lines.push(
      "------------------------------"
    );

    lines.push(
      ""
    );

    length =
      lines.join(
        "\n"
      ).length;
  }

  return {
    matches:
      relevant,

    text:
      lines.join(
        "\n"
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// EXTEND PART 4 CONTINUITY WITH LARGE-INPUT RECALL
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallCompleteContinuityInjectionV13Part6(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const [
    base,
    largeInputContext
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      buildRecallCompleteContinuityInjectionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        currentUserMessage
      ),

      buildRecallLargeInputContextV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        currentUserMessage
      )
    ]);

  return {
    ...base,

    largeInputMatches:
      largeInputContext.matches,

    text:
      [
        base?.text ||
        "",

        largeInputContext?.text ||
        ""
      ]
        .filter(
          Boolean
        )
        .join(
          "\n\n==============================\n\n"
        )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DETERMINE WHO THE AUDIENCE INTERRUPTED
//
// Prefer the character who most recently spoke.
//
// Fall back to the active speaker if necessary.
// =========================================================

function getRecallInterruptedCombatantV13(
  state
) {
  const lastSpeaker =
    state
      ?.conversation
      ?.lastSpeaker;

  if (
    lastSpeaker ===
      CHARACTER_HOST ||
    lastSpeaker ===
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS
  ) {
    return lastSpeaker;
  }

  const activeSpeaker =
    state
      ?.conversation
      ?.activeSpeaker;

  if (
    activeSpeaker ===
      CHARACTER_HOST ||
    activeSpeaker ===
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS
  ) {
    return activeSpeaker;
  }

  return CHARACTER_NAMELESS;
}


// =========================================================
// CALCULATE AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION DAMAGE
// =========================================================

function calculateRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13(
  combatant
) {
  const maxHp =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        combatant
          ?.maxHp,
        1
      )
    );

  const base =
    Math.max(
      RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_MIN_DAMAGE,

      Math.floor(
        maxHp *
        RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_DAMAGE_RATIO
      )
    );

  const maximum =
    Math.max(
      base,

      Math.ceil(
        maxHp *
        RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_MAX_DAMAGE_RATIO
      )
    );

  return clamp(
    base,
    RECALL_V13_INTERRUPTION_MIN_DAMAGE,
    maximum
  );
}


// =========================================================
// STORE COMBAT INTERRUPTION EVENT
// =========================================================

async function storeRecallCombatInterruptionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  result,
  characterCount
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_combat_interruptions_v13 (
        visitor_id,
        episode_number,
        turn_number,
        target_character,
        damage,
        target_hp_before,
        target_hp_after,
        target_max_hp,
        killed_target,
        invincibility_triggered,
        source_character_count,
        created_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      state
        ?.watch
        ?.episodesWatched ??
        null,

      state
        ?.conversation
        ?.turnNumber ??
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.currentEpisodeTurnNumber ??
        null,

      result.targetCharacter,

      result.damage,

      result.hpBefore,

      result.hpAfter,

      result.maxHp,

      result.killedTarget
        ? 1
        : 0,

      result.invincibilityTriggered
        ? 1
        : 0,

      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          characterCount,
          0
        )
      ),

      isoNow()
    )
