    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// APPLY COMBAT-PHASE INTERRUPTION DAMAGE
//
// This is ENGINE damage.
//
// The model does not decide whether it happened.
// =========================================================

async function applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  {
    characterCount =
      0
  } =
    {}
) {
  if (
    !state
      ?.combat
      ?.active
  ) {
    return {
      applied:
        false,

      reason:
        "combat-not-active"
    };
  }

  const targetCharacter =
    getRecallInterruptedCombatantV13(
      state
    );

  const target =
    getCombatantState(
      state,
      targetCharacter
    );

  if (!target) {
    return {
      applied:
        false,

      reason:
        "target-not-found"
    };
  }

  if (
    targetCharacter ===
      CHARACTER_HOST &&
    target.alive ===
      false
  ) {
    return {
      applied:
        false,

      reason:
        "host-already-dead"
    };
  }

  const maxHp =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        target.maxHp,
        1
      )
    );

  const hpBefore =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        target.hp,
        maxHp
      )
    );

  const damage =
    calculateRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13(
      target
    );

  target.hp =
    Math.max(
      0,
      hpBefore -
        damage
    );

  let killedTarget =
    false;

  let invincibilityTriggered =
    false;

  let death =
    null;

  let invincibility =
    null;

  if (
    target.hp <=
      0
  ) {
    if (
      targetCharacter ===
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS
    ) {
      invincibility =
        resolveNamelessInvincibility(
          state
        );

      invincibilityTriggered =
        Boolean(
          invincibility
            ?.triggered
        );
    } else if (
      targetCharacter ===
      CHARACTER_HOST
    ) {
      death =
        await killCurrentHost(
          env,
          visitorId,
          state,
          "audience interruption"
        );

      killedTarget =
        Boolean(
          death
        );
    }
  }

  state
    .combat
    .lastDamage =
      damage;

  state
    .combat
    .lastAttack = {
      attacker:
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

      defender:
        targetCharacter,

      type:
        "audience-interruption",

      name:
        "AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION",

      damage,

      infinite:
        false,

      powerGap:
        null,

      occurredAt:
        isoNow()
    };

  const hpAfter =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        target.hp,
        0
      )
    );

  const result = {
    applied:
      true,

    targetCharacter,

    damage,

    hpBefore,

    hpAfter,

    maxHp,

    killedTarget,

    invincibilityTriggered,

    death,

    invincibility
  };

  await storeRecallCombatInterruptionV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    result,
    characterCount
  );

  await saveOrReinforceRecallMemoryV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      characterKey:
        "show",

      memoryType:
        RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.COMBAT,

      subjectKey:
        "audience-combat-interruption",

      summary:
        (
          `During combat, an audience interruption struck ` +
          `${targetCharacter} for ${damage} damage.`
        ),

      importance:
        killedTarget
          ? 80
          : 24,

      confidence:
        1,

      sourceType:
        "combat_interruption_v13",

      tags: [
        "combat",
        "audience",
        "interruption"
      ],

      metadata: {
        targetCharacter,
        damage,
        hpBefore,
        hpAfter,
        killedTarget,
        invincibilityTriggered
      }
    }
  );

  return result;
}


// =========================================================
// COMBAT INTERRUPTION PROMPT DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

function buildRecallCombatInterruptionDirectiveV13(
  result
) {
  if (
    !result
      ?.applied
  ) {
    return "";
  }

  if (
    result
      .invincibilityTriggered
  ) {
    return `
COMBAT INTERRUPTION EVENT:

The audience interrupted during active combat.

The interruption mechanically dealt ${result.damage} damage to Nameless.

His HP reached the invincibility threshold and his invincibility recovery triggered.

This damage is authoritative engine state.

React naturally if the combat context makes the event worth mentioning.

Do not invent a different damage amount.
`.trim();
  }

  if (
    result
      .killedTarget
  ) {
    return `
COMBAT INTERRUPTION EVENT:

The audience interrupted during active combat.

The interruption mechanically dealt ${result.damage} damage to the current host and killed them.

This death is authoritative engine state.

The model must not reverse or ignore it.
`.trim();
  }

  return `
COMBAT INTERRUPTION EVENT:

The audience interrupted during active combat.

The interruption mechanically struck ${result.targetCharacter} for ${result.damage} damage.

HP changed from ${result.hpBefore} to ${result.hpAfter}.

This is authoritative engine state.

The character may react naturally to being interrupted and damaged.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// PREPARE AN AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION
//
// Ordinary message:
//
// pass straight through.
//
// Huge message:
//
// preserve original;
// digest;
// remember;
// send digest through V12.
// =========================================================

async function prepareRecallAudienceInterjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  originalMessage
) {
  const validation =
    validateRecallAudienceInputV13(
      originalMessage
    );

  if (!validation.ok) {
    return validation;
  }

  let modelMessage =
    validation.text;

  let largeInput =
    null;

  if (
    validation.large
  ) {
    const stored =
      await saveRecallLargeInputV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        validation.text
      );

    if (!stored.ok) {
      return stored;
    }

    const digestResult =
      await buildRecallLargeInputDigestV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        stored.inputId
      );

    if (!digestResult.ok) {
      return digestResult;
    }

    modelMessage =
      buildRecallLargeInputModelMessageV13(
        stored.inputId,
        validation.characterCount,
        digestResult.digest
      );

    await rememberRecallLargeInputV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      stored.inputId,
      digestResult.digest,
      validation.characterCount
    );

    largeInput = {
      ...stored,

      digest:
        digestResult.digest
    };
  }

  const combat =
    await applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state,
      {
        characterCount:
          validation.characterCount
      }
    );

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    originalMessage:
      validation.text,

    modelMessage,

    originalCharacterCount:
      validation.characterCount,

    originalByteSize:
      validation.byteSize,

    large:
      validation.large,

    largeInput,

    combat,

    combatDirective:
      buildRecallCombatInterruptionDirectiveV13(
        combat
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD MODIFIED REQUEST FOR V12
//
// The underlying V12 endpoint expects JSON containing:
//
// visitorId
// message
//
// We replace only message when V13 had to digest a gigantic
// input.
//
// Everything else in the request body survives.
// =========================================================

function buildRecallV13InterjectionRequest(
  originalRequest,
  originalBody,
  modelMessage
) {
  const body = {
    ...(
      originalBody &&
      typeof originalBody ===
        "object"
        ? originalBody
        : {}
    ),

    message:
      modelMessage
  };

  const headers =
    new Headers(
      originalRequest.headers
    );

  headers.set(
    "content-type",
    "application/json"
  );

  return new Request(
    originalRequest.url,
    {
      method:
        originalRequest.method,

      headers,

      body:
        JSON.stringify(
          body
        )
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// V12 AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION WRAPPER
//
// This is the actual Part-6 surgery.
//
// V12 remains the stable endpoint.
//
// V13 preprocesses immediately before it.
// =========================================================

const handleAudienceInterjectionV12 =
  handleAudienceInterjection;


handleAudienceInterjection =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    let clonedBody;

    try {
      clonedBody =
        await request
          .clone()
          .json();
    } catch {
      return await handleAudienceInterjectionV12(
        request,
        env
      );
    }

    const visitorId =
      normalizeVisitorId(
        clonedBody
          ?.visitorId ??
        clonedBody
          ?.visitor_id
      );

    if (!visitorId) {
      return await handleAudienceInterjectionV12(
        request,
        env
      );
    }

    const rawMessage =
      typeof clonedBody
        ?.message ===
        "string"
        ? clonedBody.message
        : String(
            clonedBody
              ?.message ||
            ""
          );

    const validation =
      validateRecallAudienceInputV13(
        rawMessage
      );

    if (
      !validation.ok
    ) {
      if (
        validation.error ===
          "AUDIENCE_INPUT_TOO_LARGE"
      ) {
        return jsonResponse(
          {
            ok:
              false,

            error:
              "Audience submission exceeds the 3 MB V13 hard limit.",

            maximumBytes:
              validation.maximumBytes,

            actualBytes:
              validation.actualBytes
          },
          413
        );
      }

      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          error:
            "Audience statement is empty."
        },
        400
      );
    }

    /*
     * Ordinary V12-sized messages still gain combat
     * interruption damage, but require no digest work.
     */

    const state =
      await loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    if (
      !state
        ?.television
        ?.poweredOn
    ) {
      return await handleAudienceInterjectionV12(
        request,
        env
      );
    }

    const prepared =
      await prepareRecallAudienceInterjectionV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        rawMessage
      );

    if (
      !prepared.ok
    ) {
      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          error:
            prepared.error ||
            "V13 audience preprocessing failed."
        },
        500
      );
    }

    /*
     * Save combat changes before V12 reloads the state.
     */

    if (
      prepared
        .combat
        ?.applied
    ) {
      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );
    }

    const forwardedRequest =
      prepared.large
        ? buildRecallV13InterjectionRequest(
            request,
            clonedBody,
            prepared.modelMessage
          )
        : request;

    const response =
      await handleAudienceInterjectionV12(
        forwardedRequest,
        env
      );

    return response;
  };


// =========================================================
// LARGE-INPUT CONTINUITY DIRECTIVE
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE = `
VERY LARGE AUDIENCE INPUT:

The audience is allowed to submit extremely long material.

A long submission may be an essay, transcript, chapter, argument, journal, conversation, manuscript, or near-novel-sized message.

Do not mock the user for message length merely because it is long.

Do not pretend that only the final paragraph exists.

When the engine provides a LARGE INPUT DIGEST, treat it as a processing representation of a much larger original submission.

The original text remains stored persistently.

When future conversation strongly matches part of that original submission, relevant raw passages may be retrieved.

Distinguish:

- the original full submission;
- the temporary digest used for immediate response;
- later memories formed from that submission.

The digest is not the canonical source.
The full stored input is the canonical source.

If exact wording matters and no raw passage has been retrieved, do not falsely claim exact quotation.

A massive submission can become a major relationship event if its content warrants that importance.

Length alone does not prove importance.

Content determines importance.
`.trim();


// =========================================================
// PART-6 MASTER CONTINUITY INJECTION
//
// Later generation surgery can use this instead of the
// Part-4 builder.
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallPart6ContinuityInjectionV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  currentUserMessage
) {
  const base =
    await buildRecallCompleteContinuityInjectionV13Part6(
      env,
      visitorId,
      currentUserMessage
    );

  return {
    ...base,

    text:
      [
        base
          ?.text ||
        "",

        RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_BEHAVIOR_DIRECTIVE
      ]
        .filter(
          Boolean
        )
        .join(
          "\n\n==============================\n\n"
        )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RHYME RESPONSE COMPLETION
//
// Part 5 established persistent rhyme turns.
//
// Call this only after NAMELESS successfully produces a
// spoken response.
//
// Host speech does not consume Nameless's rhyme counter.
// =========================================================

async function finalizeRecallNamelessSpokenTurnV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  speakerKey
) {
  if (
    speakerKey !==
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS
  ) {
    return {
      consumedRhymeTurn:
        false
    };
  }

  const before =
    await getRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  if (
    !before.active
  ) {
    return {
      consumedRhymeTurn:
        false,

      active:
        false,

      turnsRemaining:
        0
    };
  }

  const after =
    await consumeRecallNamelessRhymeTurnV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return {
    consumedRhymeTurn:
      true,

    ...after
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE INPUT DEBUG / STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallV13Part6Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await initializeRecallPart6V13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const [
    inputStats,
    chunkStats,
    combatStats,
    identity
  ] =
    await Promise.all([

      db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT
            COUNT(*) AS count,

            COALESCE(
              SUM(
                original_character_count
              ),
              0
            ) AS characters,

            COALESCE(
              SUM(
                original_byte_size
              ),
              0
            ) AS bytes

          FROM recall_large_inputs_v13

          WHERE visitor_id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT
            COUNT(*) AS count

          FROM recall_large_input_chunks_v13

          WHERE visitor_id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT
            COUNT(*) AS count,

            COALESCE(
              SUM(
                damage
              ),
              0
            ) AS total_damage

          FROM recall_combat_interruptions_v13

          WHERE visitor_id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first(),


      getRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  return {
    largeInputCount:
      integerNumber(
        inputStats
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    storedLargeInputCharacters:
      integerNumber(
        inputStats
          ?.characters,
        0
      ),

    storedLargeInputBytes:
      integerNumber(
        inputStats
          ?.bytes,
        0
      ),

    largeInputChunks:
      integerNumber(
        chunkStats
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    combatInterruptions:
      integerNumber(
        combatStats
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    totalInterruptionDamage:
      integerNumber(
        combatStats
          ?.total_damage,
        0
      ),

    rhymeMode:
      integerNumber(
        identity
          ?.rhyme_mode,
        0
      ) ===
        1,

    rhymeTurnsRemaining:
      integerNumber(
        identity
          ?.rhyme_turns_remaining,
        0
      ),

    ordinaryDirectInputCharacters:
      RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT,

    maximumAudienceInputBytes:
      RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES,

    part6Installed:
      true
  };
}


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT ARCHITECTURAL RESULT
//
// V12:
//
// Audience message
//      |
//      v
// maximum 12,000 characters
//      |
//      v
// immediate generation
//
//
// V13:
//
// Audience message
//      |
//      +---- <= 12k --------------------> V12 normally
//      |
//      |
//      +---- > 12k
//             |
//             +----> FULL TEXT STORED PERMANENTLY
//             |
//             +----> CHUNKED
//             |
//             +----> CHUNK DIGESTS
//             |
//             +----> UNIFIED DIGEST <= V12 INPUT LIMIT
//             |
//             +----> immediate TV response
//             |
//             +----> future raw-passage retrieval
//             |
//             +----> durable memory extraction
//
//
// Therefore:
//
// somebody can paste a novel.
//
// We do not throw it away.
//
// We do not silently keep only the first 12,000 characters.
//
// We do not send three megabytes into every future prompt.
//
// We preserve the source and retrieve from it later.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// END RECALL THY NAME V13 — PART 6 OF 8
//
// INSTALLED:
//
// - 3 MB audience input hard ceiling
// - ordinary V12 messages remain untouched
// - near-unlimited-feeling text composer backend
// - full persistent storage of giant messages
// - SHA-256 large-input identity
// - large-input chunk persistence
// - paragraph-aware long-message chunking
// - hierarchical chunk summarization
// - immediate model-safe digest
// - permanent original source retention
// - later raw-passage retrieval
// - large-input durable memory
// - duplicate giant-input protection
// - V12 audience endpoint wrapper
// - combat interruption now mechanically deals damage
// - interrupted character selection
// - authoritative combat event persistence
// - Nameless invincibility handling
// - host death handling
// - combat interruption memory
// - Part-6 continuity injection
// - rhyme-turn completion primitive
// - Part-6 status/debug utility
//
// PART 7:
//
// TELEVISION UI
// DOCUMENT UPLOADER
// LARGE COMPOSER
// FILE SELECTION
// 3 MB VALIDATION
// UPLOAD STATUS
// DOCUMENT LIST
// NAMELESS DISPLAY-NAME BOX UPDATE
// RHYME / COMBAT UI INTEGRATION
// ROUTER ENDPOINT SURGERY
//
// APPEND PART 7 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME — V13
// PART 7 OF 8
//
// TELEVISION UI / ROUTER INTEGRATION
//
// DOCUMENT UPLOADER ENDPOINTS
// DOCUMENT LIST / SEARCH
// LARGE COMPOSER SUPPORT
// 3 MB VALIDATION
// NAMELESS DISPLAY-NAME PUBLIC STATE
// RHYME STATE PUBLIC STATE
// COMBAT INTERRUPTION PUBLIC STATE
// V13 ROUTER SURGERY
// FRONTEND CONTRACT
//
// APPEND THIS ENTIRE PART DIRECTLY AFTER V13 PART 6.
//
// IMPORTANT:
//
// V12 already owns the main Worker router.
//
// This part does NOT create a second export default.
//
// Instead it:
//
// 1. adds V13 route handlers;
//
// 2. wraps existing V12 handlers/state functions;
//
// 3. exposes the information the frontend needs.
//
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// PART 7 CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_ROUTE =
  "/documents/upload";

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_LIST_ROUTE =
  "/documents";

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_SEARCH_ROUTE =
  "/documents/search";

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_DELETE_ROUTE =
  "/documents/delete";

const RECALL_V13_V13_STATUS_ROUTE =
  "/v13/status";

const RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES =
  RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES;

const RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES =
  RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES;


// =========================================================
// JSON BODY HELPER
// =========================================================

async function readRecallV13JsonBody(
  request
) {
  try {
    return {
      ok: true,
      body:
        await request.json()
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      body: null
    };
  }
}


// =========================================================
// V13 VISITOR RESOLUTION
// =========================================================

function resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
  body
) {
  return normalizeVisitorId(
    body?.visitorId ??
    body?.visitor_id
  );
}


function resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
  request
) {
  try {
    const url =
      new URL(
        request.url
      );

    return normalizeVisitorId(
      url.searchParams.get(
        "visitorId"
      ) ??
      url.searchParams.get(
        "visitor_id"
      )
    );
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT UPLOAD ROUTE
//
// Expects:
//
// {
//   visitorId,
//   title,
//   filename,
//   mimeType,
//   byteSize,
//   text
// }
//
// Text extraction happens client-side.
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentUploadRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const parsed =
    await readRecallV13JsonBody(
      request
    );

  if (!parsed.ok) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Invalid document upload request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
      parsed.body
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  return await handleRecallDocumentUploadV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    parsed.body
  );
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT LIST ROUTE
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentListRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
      request
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  return await handleRecallDocumentListV13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT SEARCH ROUTE
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentSearchRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const parsed =
    await readRecallV13JsonBody(
      request
    );

  if (!parsed.ok) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Invalid document search request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
      parsed.body
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  return await handleRecallDocumentSearchV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    parsed.body
  );
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT DELETE ROUTE
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallDocumentDeleteRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const parsed =
    await readRecallV13JsonBody(
      request
    );

  if (!parsed.ok) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Invalid document delete request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
      parsed.body
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const documentId =
    integerNumber(
      parsed.body
        ?.documentId ??
      parsed.body
        ?.document_id,
      0
    );

  if (
    documentId <=
      0
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing documentId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const result =
    await deleteRecallDocumentV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      documentId
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    result,
    result.ok
      ? 200
      : 404
  );
}


// =========================================================
// COMPLETE V13 STATUS ROUTE
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallV13StatusRoute(
  request,
  env
) {
  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
      request
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const [
    memory,
    documents,
    names,
    largeInput
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      getRecallMemoryV13Part3Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallDocumentV13Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallV13Part5Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallV13Part6Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok: true,

      version:
        RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

      memory,

      documents,

      names,

      largeInput
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// PUBLIC V13 STATE
//
// V12's buildPublicRecallState() already supplies almost
// everything used by the television UI.
//
// Wrap it instead of rewriting it.
// =========================================================

const buildPublicRecallStateV12 =
  buildPublicRecallState;


function buildPublicRecallState(
  recallState,
  worldState
) {
  const base =
    buildPublicRecallStateV12(
      recallState,
      worldState
    );

  const combat =
    recallState
      ?.combat ||
    {};

  return {
    ...base,

    v13: {
      installed:
        true,

      memoryVersion:
        RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

      composerMaximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES,

      documentMaximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES,

      documentRecommendedBytes:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,

      combatInterruptionEnabled:
        true,

      combatActive:
        Boolean(
          combat.active
        ),

      lastCombatDamage:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            combat.lastDamage,
            0
          )
        ),

      lastCombatAttack:
        combat.lastAttack ||
        null
    }
  };
}


// =========================================================
// AUGMENT PUBLIC STATE ASYNCHRONOUSLY
//
// buildPublicRecallState is synchronous.
//
// Name + rhyme persistence live in D1.
//
// Therefore endpoint wrappers may optionally append this
// asynchronous identity packet.
// =========================================================

async function buildRecallPublicIdentityV13(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const identity =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return {
    displayName:
      normalizeString(
        identity?.display_name,
        RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
        200
      ),

    rhymeMode:
      integerNumber(
        identity?.rhyme_mode,
        0
      ) === 1,

    rhymeTurnsRemaining:
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          identity?.rhyme_turns_remaining,
          0
        )
      ),

    confusionCount:
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          identity?.confusion_count,
          0
        )
      ),

    nameChangeCount:
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          identity?.name_change_count,
          0
        )
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RESPONSE JSON AUGMENTER
//
// Allows us to append publicIdentityV13 without rewriting
// every V12 endpoint.
// =========================================================

async function augmentRecallJsonResponseV13(
  response,
  extra
) {
  if (
    !response ||
    !(response instanceof Response)
  ) {
    return response;
  }

  const contentType =
    response
      .headers
      .get(
        "content-type"
      ) ||
    "";

  if (
    !contentType
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(
        "application/json"
      )
  ) {
    return response;
  }

  let payload;

  try {
    payload =
      await response
        .clone()
        .json();
  } catch {
    return response;
  }

  if (
    !payload ||
    typeof payload !==
      "object" ||
    Array.isArray(
      payload
    )
  ) {
    return response;
  }

  const headers =
    new Headers(
      response.headers
    );

  return new Response(
    JSON.stringify({
      ...payload,
      ...extra
    }),
    {
      status:
        response.status,

      statusText:
        response.statusText,

      headers
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// WRAP AUDIENCE RESPONSE
//
// Part 6 already wrapped handleAudienceInterjection.
//
// This second wrapper leaves Part 6 intact and merely adds
// the public identity state to successful/failed responses.
// =========================================================

const handleAudienceInterjectionV13Part6 =
  handleAudienceInterjection;


handleAudienceInterjection =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const parsed =
      await readRecallV13JsonBody(
        request.clone()
      );

    const visitorId =
      parsed.ok
        ? resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
            parsed.body
          )
        : null;

    const response =
      await handleAudienceInterjectionV13Part6(
        request,
        env
      );

    if (!visitorId) {
      return response;
    }

    const publicIdentityV13 =
      await buildRecallPublicIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    return await augmentRecallJsonResponseV13(
      response,
      {
        publicIdentityV13
      }
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// WRAP NEXT SHOW TURN
//
// This lets the frontend update Nameless's displayed name
// after autonomous dialogue as well.
// =========================================================

const handleNextShowTurnV12 =
  handleNextShowTurn;


handleNextShowTurn =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const parsed =
      await readRecallV13JsonBody(
        request.clone()
      );

    const visitorId =
      parsed.ok
        ? resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
            parsed.body
          )
        : null;

    const response =
      await handleNextShowTurnV12(
        request,
        env
      );

    if (!visitorId) {
      return response;
    }

    const publicIdentityV13 =
      await buildRecallPublicIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    return await augmentRecallJsonResponseV13(
      response,
      {
        publicIdentityV13
      }
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// WRAP STATE ENDPOINT
//
// The frontend should receive Nameless's current name even
// immediately after reload.
// =========================================================

const handleStateV12 =
  handleState;


handleState =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const visitorId =
      resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
        request
      );

    const response =
      await handleStateV12(
        request,
        env
      );

    if (!visitorId) {
      return response;
    }

    const publicIdentityV13 =
      await buildRecallPublicIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    return await augmentRecallJsonResponseV13(
      response,
      {
        publicIdentityV13
      }
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// ROUTER DISPATCH
//
// V12's export-default router appears near the beginning of
// the file, so code appended afterward cannot insert new
// if-statements into its already-written body.
//
// Instead:
//
// - Part 7 creates a V13 pre-router;
// - Part 8 performs the tiny final top-level router surgery.
//
// Part 8 will call this BEFORE the original route checks.
//
// Returning null means:
//
// "not a V13-specific route — continue through V12."
// =========================================================

async function routeRecallV13Request(
  request,
  env
) {
  const url =
    new URL(
      request.url
    );

  if (
    request.method ===
      "POST" &&
    url.pathname ===
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_ROUTE
  ) {
    return await handleRecallDocumentUploadRouteV13(
      request,
      env
    );
  }

  if (
    request.method ===
      "GET" &&
    url.pathname ===
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_LIST_ROUTE
  ) {
    return await handleRecallDocumentListRouteV13(
      request,
      env
    );
  }

  if (
    request.method ===
      "POST" &&
    url.pathname ===
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_SEARCH_ROUTE
  ) {
    return await handleRecallDocumentSearchRouteV13(
      request,
      env
    );
  }

  if (
    request.method ===
      "POST" &&
    url.pathname ===
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_DELETE_ROUTE
  ) {
    return await handleRecallDocumentDeleteRouteV13(
      request,
      env
    );
  }

  if (
    request.method ===
      "GET" &&
    url.pathname ===
      RECALL_V13_V13_STATUS_ROUTE
  ) {
    return await handleRecallV13StatusRoute(
      request,
      env
    );
  }

  return null;
}


// =========================================================
// CORS WRAPPER FOR V13 ROUTES
// =========================================================

async function routeRecallV13RequestWithCors(
  request,
  env
) {
  const response =
    await routeRecallV13Request(
      request,
      env
    );

  if (!response) {
    return null;
  }

  return addCorsToResponse(
    response
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND FILE TYPE SUPPORT
//
// Browser text extraction.
//
// We do not claim raw PDF/DOCX support.
//
// These are the types Part 4 can safely ingest as text.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_FRONTEND_TEXT_EXTENSIONS =
  Object.freeze([
    "txt",
    "md",
    "markdown",
    "text",
    "csv",
    "json",
    "html",
    "htm",
    "rtf"
  ]);


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND FILE EXTENSION
// =========================================================

function getRecallFrontendFileExtensionV13(
  filename
) {
  const match =
    String(
      filename ||
      ""
    )
      .toLowerCase()
      .match(
        /\.([a-z0-9]+)$/
      );

  return match
    ? match[1]
    : "";
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND FILE VALIDATION
// =========================================================

function validateRecallFrontendDocumentFileV13(
  file
) {
  if (!file) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "NO_FILE_SELECTED"
    };
  }

  if (
    Number(
      file.size
    ) >
    RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
      maximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES,
      actualBytes:
        Number(
          file.size
        )
    };
  }

  const extension =
    getRecallFrontendFileExtensionV13(
      file.name
    );

  if (
    extension &&
    !RECALL_V13_FRONTEND_TEXT_EXTENSIONS
      .includes(
        extension
      )
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "UNSUPPORTED_FILE_TYPE",
      extension
    };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    extension
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND TEXT EXTRACTION
// =========================================================

async function readRecallDocumentFileAsTextV13(
  file
) {
  const validation =
    validateRecallFrontendDocumentFileV13(
      file
    );

  if (!validation.ok) {
    return validation;
  }

  try {
    const text =
      await file.text();

    if (
      !String(
        text ||
        ""
      ).trim()
    ) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error:
          "FILE_CONTAINS_NO_TEXT"
      };
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      text,
      extension:
        validation.extension,
      byteSize:
        Number(
          file.size
        )
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "FILE_READ_FAILED"
    };
  }
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND UPLOAD FUNCTION
//
// Can be copied into the television client JavaScript.
//
// apiBase should be the Worker origin already used by the
// Recall Thy Name frontend.
// =========================================================

async function uploadRecallDocumentFromBrowserV13({
  apiBase,
  visitorId,
  file,
  title = null
}) {
  const extracted =
    await readRecallDocumentFileAsTextV13(
      file
    );

  if (!extracted.ok) {
    return extracted;
  }

  const response =
    await fetch(
      `${apiBase}${RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_ROUTE}`,
      {
        method:
          "POST",

        headers: {
          "Content-Type":
            "application/json"
        },

        body:
          JSON.stringify({
            visitorId,

            title:
              title ||
              file.name,

            filename:
              file.name,

            mimeType:
              file.type ||
              "text/plain",

            byteSize:
              file.size,

            text:
              extracted.text
          })
      }
    );

  let payload =
    null;

  try {
    payload =
      await response.json();
  } catch {
    payload = {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "UPLOAD_RESPONSE_INVALID"
    };
  }

  return {
    httpOk:
      response.ok,

    ...payload
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND DOCUMENT LIST FUNCTION
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallDocumentsFromBrowserV13({
  apiBase,
  visitorId
}) {
  const response =
    await fetch(
      `${apiBase}${RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_LIST_ROUTE}?visitorId=${encodeURIComponent(
        visitorId
      )}`,
      {
        method:
          "GET"
      }
    );

  if (!response.ok) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      documents: []
    };
  }

  try {
    return await response.json();
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      documents: []
    };
  }
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND LARGE COMPOSER VALIDATION
//
// This is deliberately byte-aware.
//
// Character count is not enough for Unicode.
// =========================================================

function validateRecallComposerTextV13(
  text
) {
  const value =
    String(
      text ||
      ""
    );

  if (!value.trim()) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "MESSAGE_EMPTY",
      characterCount:
        0,
      byteSize:
        0
    };
  }

  const byteSize =
    recallV13InputByteSize(
      value
    );

  if (
    byteSize >
    RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "MESSAGE_TOO_LARGE",
      characterCount:
        value.length,
      byteSize,
      maximumBytes:
        RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES
    };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    characterCount:
      value.length,
    byteSize,
    large:
      value.length >
      RECALL_V13_ORDINARY_AUDIENCE_LIMIT
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FRONTEND CHARACTER / BYTE COUNTER
// =========================================================

function formatRecallComposerCounterV13(
  text
) {
  const result =
    validateRecallComposerTextV13(
      text
    );

  const characters =
    result.characterCount ||
    0;

  const bytes =
    result.byteSize ||
    0;

  const megabytes =
    bytes /
    (
      1024 *
      1024
    );

  return (
    `${characters.toLocaleString()} characters` +
    ` — ` +
    `${megabytes.toFixed(2)} MB / 3.00 MB`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// PUBLIC IDENTITY APPLICATION
//
// Frontend:
//
// call this after:
//
// - /state
// - /show/next
// - /show/interject
//
// Pass the JSON payload returned by the Worker.
// =========================================================

function applyRecallPublicIdentityV13(
  payload,
  {
    namelessNameElement = null,
    rhymeElement = null
  } = {}
) {
  const identity =
    payload
      ?.publicIdentityV13;

  if (!identity) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    namelessNameElement
  ) {
    namelessNameElement.textContent =
      identity.displayName ||
      RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME;
  }

  if (
    rhymeElement
  ) {
    rhymeElement.hidden =
      !identity.rhymeMode;

    rhymeElement.textContent =
      identity.rhymeMode
        ? (
            "RHYME MODE" +
            (
              identity.rhymeTurnsRemaining >
                0
                ? ` (${identity.rhymeTurnsRemaining})`
                : ""
            )
          )
        : "";
  }

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT UPLOADER UI FACTORY
//
// Optional convenience primitive.
//
// Existing page styling may override all class names.
//
// No CSS assumptions are forced.
// =========================================================

function createRecallDocumentUploaderV13({
  apiBase,
  visitorId,
  onUploaded = null
}) {
  const container =
    document.createElement(
      "section"
    );

  container.className =
    "recall-document-uploader";

  const title =
    document.createElement(
      "div"
    );

  title.className =
    "recall-document-uploader-title";

  title.textContent =
    "UPLOAD DOCUMENT";

  const input =
    document.createElement(
      "input"
    );

  input.type =
    "file";

  input.accept =
    RECALL_V13_FRONTEND_TEXT_EXTENSIONS
      .map(
        (extension) =>
          `.${extension}`
      )
      .join(",");

  const status =
    document.createElement(
      "div"
    );

  status.className =
    "recall-document-upload-status";

  status.textContent =
    "Maximum: 3 MB";

  const button =
    document.createElement(
      "button"
    );

  button.type =
    "button";

  button.textContent =
    "UPLOAD";

  button.addEventListener(
    "click",
    async () => {
      const file =
        input.files
          ?.[0];

      const validation =
        validateRecallFrontendDocumentFileV13(
          file
        );

      if (!validation.ok) {
        if (
          validation.error ===
            "FILE_TOO_LARGE"
        ) {
          status.textContent =
            "FILE TOO LARGE — 3 MB MAXIMUM";
        } else if (
          validation.error ===
            "UNSUPPORTED_FILE_TYPE"
        ) {
          status.textContent =
            "UNSUPPORTED TEXT FILE TYPE";
        } else {
          status.textContent =
            "SELECT A DOCUMENT";
        }

        return;
      }

      button.disabled =
        true;

      status.textContent =
        "READING DOCUMENT...";

      try {
        const result =
          await uploadRecallDocumentFromBrowserV13({
            apiBase,
            visitorId,
            file
          });

        if (!result.ok) {
          status.textContent =
            result.error ||
            "UPLOAD FAILED";

          return;
        }

        status.textContent =
          result.duplicate
            ? "DOCUMENT ALREADY REMEMBERED"
            : "DOCUMENT REMEMBERED";

        if (
          typeof onUploaded ===
            "function"
        ) {
          await onUploaded(
            result
          );
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(
          "Document upload UI failed:",
          error
        );

        status.textContent =
          "UPLOAD FAILED";
      } finally {
        button.disabled =
          false;
      }
    }
  );

  container.append(
    title,
    input,
    button,
    status
  );

  return container;
}


// =========================================================
// LARGE COMPOSER ENHANCER
//
// Existing textarea remains existing textarea.
//
// We merely:
//
// - remove tiny maxlength attributes;
//
// - install 3 MB validation;
//
// - add a counter;
//
// - preserve ordinary submit behavior.
//
// The caller provides the send callback.
// =========================================================

function enhanceRecallAudienceComposerV13({
  textarea,
  sendButton,
  onSend,
  counterElement = null
}) {
  if (
    !textarea ||
    !sendButton
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        "COMPOSER_ELEMENTS_REQUIRED"
    };
  }

  textarea.removeAttribute(
    "maxlength"
  );

  const counter =
    counterElement ||
    document.createElement(
      "div"
    );

  if (!counterElement) {
    counter.className =
      "recall-v13-composer-counter";

    textarea.insertAdjacentElement(
      "afterend",
      counter
    );
  }

  const update =
    () => {
      const validation =
        validateRecallComposerTextV13(
          textarea.value
        );

      counter.textContent =
        formatRecallComposerCounterV13(
          textarea.value
        );

      sendButton.disabled =
        !validation.ok;

      if (
        validation.error ===
          "MESSAGE_TOO_LARGE"
      ) {
        counter.dataset.state =
          "too-large";
      } else if (
        validation.large
      ) {
        counter.dataset.state =
          "large";
      } else {
        counter.dataset.state =
          "normal";
      }
    };

  textarea.addEventListener(
    "input",
    update
  );

  sendButton.addEventListener(
    "click",
    async () => {
      const validation =
        validateRecallComposerTextV13(
          textarea.value
        );

      if (!validation.ok) {
        update();
        return;
      }

      if (
        typeof onSend !==
          "function"
      ) {
        return;
      }

      sendButton.disabled =
        true;

      try {
        await onSend(
          textarea.value
        );
      } finally {
        update();
      }
    }
  );

  update();

  return {
    ok: true,
    textarea,
    sendButton,
    counter
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MINIMAL RECOMMENDED FRONTEND MARKUP
//
// Reference only.

// DO NOT paste this into the Worker JS as executable HTML.
//
// <div id="nameless-name">The Nameless Man</div>

// <div id="audience-input-counter"></div>

// <button id="audience-send">SEND</button>

// <div id="document-uploader-slot"></div>
//
// =========================================================

//             headers: {

//             },

//             body:
//               JSON.stringify({
//                 visitorId:
//                   VISITOR_ID,
//
//                 message

//           }
//         );
//
//       const payload =
//         await response.json();

//       applyRecallPublicIdentityV13(
//         payload,
//         {
//           namelessNameElement,

// const uploader =

// document

//   .appendChild(
//     uploader
//   );
//
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT ROUTER SURGERY FOR PART 8
//
// V12 begins with:
//
// export default {
//   async fetch(
//     request,
//     env
//   ) {
//     const url =
//       new URL(
//         request.url
//       );
//
//
//
// Part 8 will insert ONLY this immediately after the url:
//
// const v13Response =
//   await routeRecallV13RequestWithCors(
//     request,
//     env
//   );
//
// if (v13Response) {
//   return v13Response;
// }
//
//
//
// That is intentionally small.
//
// Everything complicated remains here.
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// PART 7 STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallV13Part7Status(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const [
    part5,
    part6,
    documents
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      getRecallV13Part5Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallV13Part6Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallDocumentV13Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  return {
    routes: {
      documentUpload:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_ROUTE,

      documentList:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_LIST_ROUTE,

      documentSearch:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_SEARCH_ROUTE,

      documentDelete:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_DELETE_ROUTE,

      status:
        RECALL_V13_V13_STATUS_ROUTE
    },

    composerMaximumBytes:
      RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES,

    documentMaximumBytes:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES,

    publicIdentity: {
      displayName:
        part5.displayName,

      rhymeMode:
        part5.rhymeMode,

      rhymeTurnsRemaining:
        part5.rhymeTurnsRemaining
    },

    combatInterruptions:
      part6.combatInterruptions,

    documentCount:
      documents.documentCount,

    part7Installed:
      true
  };
}
