const RECALL_V13_FINAL_BUILD_VERSION =
  "13.0.0";

const RECALL_V13_FOUNDATION_ROUTE =
  "/memory/foundation";

const RECALL_V13_FOUNDATION_STATUS_ROUTE =
  "/memory/foundation/status";


// =========================================================
// COMPATIBILITY ALIAS:
// CREATE MEMORY
//
// Parts 4/5 use createRecallMemoryV13().
//
// Part 1's canonical primitive is saveRecallMemoryV13().
//
// Keep both names valid.
// =========================================================

async function createRecallMemoryV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  memory = {}
) {
  return await saveRecallMemoryV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      characterKey:
        memory.characterKey ||
        memory.character_key ||
        "shared",

      memoryType:
        memory.memoryType ||
        memory.memory_type ||
        RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,

      subjectKey:
        memory.subjectKey ||
        memory.subject_key ||
        null,

      summary:
        memory.summary,

      detail:
        memory.detail ||
        null,

      exactQuote:
        memory.exactQuote ||
        memory.exact_quote ||
        null,

      sourceType:
        memory.sourceType ||
        memory.source_type ||
        "conversation",

      sourceId:
        memory.sourceId ??
        memory.source_id ??
        null,

      sourceEpisode:
        memory.sourceEpisode ??
        memory.source_episode ??
        null,

      importance:
        memory.importance ??
        25,

      confidence:
        memory.confidence ??
        0.75,

      reinforcementCount:
        memory.reinforcementCount ??
        memory.reinforcement_count ??
        0,

      isFoundational:
        Boolean(
          memory.isFoundational ??
          memory.is_foundational
        ),

      isResolved:
        Boolean(
          memory.isResolved ??
          memory.is_resolved
        ),

      tags:
        Array.isArray(
          memory.tags
        )
          ? memory.tags
          : [],

      metadata:
        memory.metadata &&
        typeof memory.metadata ===
          "object"
          ? memory.metadata
          : {}
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// COMPATIBILITY ALIAS:
// SUBJECT
//
// Parts 4/5 may call upsertRecallSubjectV13().
//
// Part 1's canonical primitive is
// touchRecallMemorySubjectV13().
// =========================================================

async function upsertRecallSubjectV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  subject = {}
) {
  return await touchRecallMemorySubjectV13(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      subjectKey:
        subject.subjectKey ||
        subject.subject_key,

      displayName:
        subject.displayName ||
        subject.display_name ||
        null,

      subjectType:
        subject.subjectType ||
        subject.subject_type ||
        "topic",

      importance:
        subject.importance ??
        25,

      metadata:
        subject.metadata &&
        typeof subject.metadata ===
          "object"
          ? subject.metadata
          : {}
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// FINAL COMPATIBILITY SCHEMA
//
// Part 1 deliberately created the initial document storage
// foundation.
//
// Part 4 subsequently added richer document behavior.
//
// This migration reconciles the two versions without
// deleting either existing table or any player data.
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  // -------------------------------------------------------
  // DOCUMENT COLUMNS REQUIRED BY PART 4
  // -------------------------------------------------------

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "document_key",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "title",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "filename",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "extension",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "character_count",
    "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "status",
    "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ready'"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "document_summary",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "first_seen_at",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_documents",
    "last_accessed_at",
    "TEXT"
  );


  // -------------------------------------------------------
  // DOCUMENT CHUNK COMPATIBILITY
  // -------------------------------------------------------

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_document_chunks",
    "start_character",
    "INTEGER"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_document_chunks",
    "end_character",
    "INTEGER"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_document_chunks",
    "normalized_content",
    "TEXT"
  );

  await recallV13AddColumnIfMissing(
    db,
    "recall_document_chunks",
    "token_hint",
    "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
  );


  // -------------------------------------------------------
  // OPTIONAL RELATIONSHIP CONTINUITY TABLE
  //
  // Part 3 knows how to retrieve this.
  // -------------------------------------------------------

  await db
    .prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_relationships (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        character_key TEXT NOT NULL,

        relationship_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'new',

        notes TEXT,

        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 25,

        interaction_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        first_interaction_at TEXT,

        last_interaction_at TEXT,

        metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,

        UNIQUE(
          visitor_id,
          character_key
        )
      )
    `)
    .run();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_relationships_visitor

      ON recall_relationships(
        visitor_id,
        importance,
        interaction_count
      )
    `)
    .run();


  // -------------------------------------------------------
  // FOUNDATION IMPORT AUDIT
  // -------------------------------------------------------

  await db
    .prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_foundation_import_log (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        imported_character_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        imported_memory_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

        source_label TEXT,

        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `)
    .run();

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// EXTEND PART-1 DATABASE INITIALIZATION
// =========================================================

const ensureRecallV13DatabasePart1 =
  ensureRecallV13Database;


ensureRecallV13Database =
  async function (
    env
  ) {
    await ensureRecallV13DatabasePart1(
      env
    );

    await ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
      env
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// FIX PART-3 SUBJECT RETRIEVAL
//
// Part 1 named the timestamp last_seen_at.
//
// Use that canonical column.
// =========================================================

loadRecallSubjectsForPromptV13 =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    limit =
      RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT
  ) {
    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    const result =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT *

          FROM recall_memory_subjects

          WHERE visitor_id = ?

          ORDER BY
            importance DESC,
            mention_count DESC,
            last_seen_at DESC

          LIMIT ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,

          clamp(
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              RECALL_V13_RETRIEVAL_SUBJECT_LIMIT
            ),
            1,
            100
          )
        )
        .all();

    return Array.isArray(
      result?.results
    )
      ? result.results
      : [];
  };


// =========================================================
// CORRECT PART-4 DOCUMENT STORAGE
//
// Part 1's initial recall_documents table has several
// required columns.
//
// This final implementation populates BOTH the Part-1
// foundation fields and the richer Part-4 fields.
// =========================================================

storeRecallDocumentV13 =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    payload
  ) {
    await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

    await ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
      env
    );

    const normalized =
      normalizeRecallDocumentPayloadV13(
        payload
      );

    const validation =
      validateRecallDocumentV13({
        filename:
          normalized.filename,

        text:
          normalized.text,

        byteSize:
          normalized.byteSize
      });

    if (!validation.ok) {
      return validation;
    }

    const contentHash =
      await hashRecallDocumentV13(
        validation.text
      );

    const existing =
      await findRecallDocumentByHashV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        contentHash
      );

    const now =
      isoNow();

    if (existing) {
      const db =
        requireWorldDatabase(
          env
        );

      await db
        .prepare(`
          UPDATE recall_documents

          SET
            last_accessed_at = ?,
            updated_at = ?

          WHERE id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          now,
          now,
          existing.id
        )
        .run();

      return {
        ok:
          true,

        duplicate:
          true,

        documentId:
          existing.id,

        documentKey:
          existing.document_key ||
          existing.document_id,

        title:
          existing.title ||
          existing.display_name ||
          existing.original_filename,

        filename:
          existing.filename ||
          existing.original_filename,

        chunkCount:
          integerNumber(
            existing.chunk_count,
            0
          ),

        byteSize:
          integerNumber(
            existing.byte_size,
            0
          )
      };
    }

    const chunks =
      chunkRecallDocumentV13(
        validation.text
      );

    if (!chunks.length) {
      return {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "DOCUMENT_CHUNKING_FAILED"
      };
    }

    const documentKey =
      buildRecallDocumentKeyV13(
        normalized.title,
        normalized.filename,
        contentHash
      );

    const persistentDocumentId =
      (
        "document-" +
        contentHash.slice(
          0,
          40
        )
      );

    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    const insertResult =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          INSERT INTO recall_documents (
            document_id,
            visitor_id,

            original_filename,
            display_name,
            mime_type,
            byte_size,
            storage_key,
            content_hash,

            extraction_status,
            indexing_status,
            extracted_character_count,
            chunk_count,

            importance,
            is_foundational,
            deleted,
            metadata_json,

            uploaded_at,
            indexed_at,
            created_at,
            updated_at,

            document_key,
            title,
            filename,
            extension,
            character_count,
            status,
            document_summary,
            first_seen_at,
            last_accessed_at
          )

          VALUES (
            ?, ?,
            ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
            'complete',
            'complete',
            ?, ?,
            55,
            0,
            0,
            '{}',
            ?, ?, ?, ?,
            ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
            'ready',
            NULL,
            ?, ?
          )
        `)
        .bind(
          persistentDocumentId,
          visitorId,

          normalized.filename ||
          normalized.title,

          normalized.title,

          normalized.mimeType,

          validation.byteSize,

          null,

          contentHash,

          validation.text.length,

          chunks.length,

          now,
          now,
          now,
          now,

          documentKey,

          normalized.title,

          normalized.filename,

          normalized.extension,

          validation.text.length,

          now,

          now
        )
        .run();

    const documentRowId =
      insertResult
        ?.meta
        ?.last_row_id;

    if (!documentRowId) {
      return {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "DOCUMENT_INSERT_FAILED"
      };
    }

    const statements =
      chunks.map(
        (chunk) =>
          db
            .prepare(`
              INSERT INTO recall_document_chunks (
                document_id,
                visitor_id,
                chunk_index,

                heading,
                content,

                character_start,
                character_end,

                token_estimate,
                importance,
                retrieval_count,
                last_retrieved_at,
                metadata_json,

                created_at,
                updated_at,

                start_character,
                end_character,
                normalized_content,
                token_hint
              )

              VALUES (
                ?, ?, ?,
                NULL, ?,
                ?, ?,
                ?,
                25,
                0,
                NULL,
                '{}',
                ?, ?,
                ?, ?, ?, ?
              )
            `)
            .bind(
              String(
                documentRowId
              ),

              visitorId,

              chunk.chunkIndex,

              chunk.content,

              chunk.startCharacter,

              chunk.endCharacter,

              chunk.tokenHint,

              now,

              now,

              chunk.startCharacter,

              chunk.endCharacter,

              chunk.normalizedContent,

              chunk.tokenHint
            )
      );

    const batchSize =
      40;

    for (
      let index = 0;
      index <
        statements.length;
      index +=
        batchSize
    ) {
      await db.batch(
        statements.slice(
          index,
          index +
            batchSize
        )
      );
    }

    await saveOrReinforceRecallMemoryV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        characterKey:
          "shared",

        memoryType:
          RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.DOCUMENT,

        subjectKey:
          documentKey,

        subjectDisplayName:
          normalized.title,

        subjectType:
          "document",

        summary:
          `The audience member uploaded a persistent document titled "${normalized.title}".`,

        detail:
          normalized.filename
            ? `Uploaded file: ${normalized.filename}.`
            : null,

        sourceType:
          "document_upload_v13",

        sourceId:
          persistentDocumentId,

        importance:
          55,

        confidence:
          1,

        tags: [
          "document",
          "uploaded",
          "persistent"
        ],

        metadata: {
          documentRowId,
          persistentDocumentId,
          contentHash,
          characterCount:
            validation.text.length
        }
      }
    );

    await touchRecallMemorySubjectV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        subjectKey:
          documentKey,

        displayName:
          normalized.title,

        subjectType:
          "document",

        importance:
          55,

        metadata: {
          documentRowId
        }
      }
    );

    return {
      ok:
        true,

      duplicate:
        false,

      documentId:
        documentRowId,

      persistentDocumentId,

      documentKey,

      title:
        normalized.title,

      filename:
        normalized.filename,

      chunkCount:
        chunks.length,

      characterCount:
        validation.text.length,

      byteSize:
        validation.byteSize,

      recommendedMaximum:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,

      hardMaximum:
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT LOAD FIX
//
// Part-1 chunks store document_id as the textual form of the
// owning document row id after the compatibility surgery.
// =========================================================

loadRecallDocumentChunksV13 =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    documentId,
    limit =
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
  ) {
    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    const result =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT *

          FROM recall_document_chunks

          WHERE
            visitor_id = ?
            AND document_id = ?

          ORDER BY
            chunk_index ASC

          LIMIT ?
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId,

          String(
            documentId
          ),

          clamp(
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
            ),
            1,
            RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
          )
        )
        .all();

    return Array.isArray(
      result?.results
    )
      ? result.results
      : [];
  };


// =========================================================
// DOCUMENT SEARCH FIX
// =========================================================

searchRecallDocumentsV13 =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    query,
    {
      documentId =
        null,

      limit =
        RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
    } = {}
  ) {
    await initializeRecallDocumentsV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    let documents =
      [];

    if (documentId) {
      const document =
        await loadRecallDocumentV13(
          env,
          visitorId,
          documentId
        );

      if (document) {
        documents = [
          document
        ];
      }
    } else {
      documents =
        await listRecallDocumentsV13(
          env,
          visitorId,
          30
        );
    }

    const candidates =
      [];

    for (
      const document
      of documents
    ) {
      const chunkResult =
        await db
          .prepare(`
            SELECT *

            FROM recall_document_chunks

            WHERE
              visitor_id = ?
              AND document_id = ?

            ORDER BY
              chunk_index ASC

            LIMIT ?
          `)
          .bind(
            visitorId,

            String(
              document.id
            ),

            RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_CHUNKS
          )
          .all();

      const chunks =
        Array.isArray(
          chunkResult?.results
        )
          ? chunkResult.results
          : [];

      for (
        const chunk
        of chunks
      ) {
        candidates.push({
          document,

          chunk,

          retrievalScore:
            scoreRecallDocumentChunkV13(
              chunk,
              document,
              query
            )
        });
      }
    }

    candidates.sort(
      (
        left,
        right
      ) =>
        right.retrievalScore -
        left.retrievalScore
    );

    return candidates.slice(
      0,
      clamp(
        integerNumber(
          limit,
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RETRIEVAL_LIMIT
        ),
        1,
        50
      )
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// FIX PUBLIC STATE WRAPPER
//
// Part 7 declared its wrapper as a function declaration.
//
// V12 itself performs later runtime assignments to this
// function.
//
// Make the V13 augmentation explicit here at the END of the
// file so there is no ambiguity.
// =========================================================

const buildPublicRecallStateBeforeV13Final =
  buildPublicRecallStateV12;


buildPublicRecallState =
  function (
    recallState,
    worldState
  ) {
    const base =
      buildPublicRecallStateBeforeV13Final(
        recallState,
        worldState
      );

    const combat =
      recallState
        ?.combat ||
      {};

    return {
      ...base,

      v13: {
        installed:
          true,

        finalBuild:
          RECALL_V13_FINAL_BUILD_VERSION,

        memoryVersion:
          RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

        composerMaximumBytes:
          RECALL_V13_COMPOSER_MAX_BYTES,

        documentMaximumBytes:
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES,

        documentRecommendedBytes:
          RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_RECOMMENDED_BYTES,

        combatInterruptionEnabled:
          true,

        combatActive:
          Boolean(
            combat.active
          ),

        lastCombatDamage:
          Math.max(
            0,
            integerNumber(
              combat.lastDamage,
              0
            )
          ),

        lastCombatAttack:
          combat.lastAttack ||
          null
      }
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// NAMELESS TEMPORARY NAME <-> V13 IDENTITY SYNCHRONIZATION
//
// V12 already has a successful temporary-name system.
//
// Do not throw it away.
//
// Rules:
//
// - before somebody deliberately assigns Nameless a name,
//   V12's random temporary names remain alive;
//
// - after Part 5 accepts a name, the persistent assigned name
//   wins;
//
// - the UI gets the same value.
// =========================================================

async function synchronizeRecallNamelessNameV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const identity =
    await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  const assignedName =
    normalizeString(
      identity
        ?.assigned_name,
      "",
      200
    );

  if (assignedName) {
    state
      .nameless
      .currentTemporaryName =
        assignedName;

    if (
      identity
        .display_name !==
      assignedName
    ) {
      const db =
        requireWorldDatabase(
          env
        );

      await db
        .prepare(`
          UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

          SET
            display_name = ?,
            updated_at = ?

          WHERE visitor_id = ?
        `)
        .bind(
          assignedName,
          isoNow(),
          visitorId
        )
        .run();
    }

    return assignedName;
  }

  const temporaryName =
    state
      ?.nameless
      ?.currentTemporaryName ||
    assignTemporaryNamelessName(
      state
    );

  if (
    temporaryName &&
    temporaryName !==
      identity.display_name
  ) {
    const db =
      requireWorldDatabase(
        env
      );

    await db
      .prepare(`
        UPDATE recall_nameless_identity

        SET
          display_name = ?,
          updated_at = ?

        WHERE visitor_id = ?
      `)
      .bind(
        temporaryName,
        isoNow(),
        visitorId
      )
      .run();
  }

  return temporaryName;
}


// =========================================================
// IMMEDIATE RHYME ACTIVATION
//
// If the audience requests rhyme while the HOST gets the
// first response, the instruction must still survive until
// Nameless speaks.
//
// Activate rhyme at the interruption boundary.
// =========================================================

const handleAudienceInterjectionV13Part7 =
  handleAudienceInterjection;


handleAudienceInterjection =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    let body =
      null;

    try {
      body =
        await request
          .clone()
          .json();
    } catch {
      body =
        null;
    }

    const visitorId =
      normalizeVisitorId(
        body
          ?.visitorId ??
        body
          ?.visitor_id
      );

    const message =
      typeof body
        ?.message ===
        "string"
        ? body.message
        : "";

    if (
      visitorId &&
      message &&
      recallV13RequestsRhyme(
        message
      )
    ) {
      await activateRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );
    }

    return await handleAudienceInterjectionV13Part7(
      request,
      env
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// V13 KNOWLEDGE PACKAGE INJECTION
//
// THIS IS THE MAIN MEMORY SURGERY.
//
// Current V12 generation already calls:
//
// buildTurnKnowledgePackage()
//
// and already injects historicalContext into the model.
//
// Therefore we extend THAT exact channel rather than
// rewriting the entire combat / history / Forward Vision
// generation engine.
// =========================================================

const buildTurnKnowledgePackageBeforeV13 =
  buildTurnKnowledgePackage;


buildTurnKnowledgePackage =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    {
      speakerKey,
      audienceMessage =
        null,
      previousSpeakerText =
        null
    }
  ) {
    const base =
      await buildTurnKnowledgePackageBeforeV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        {
          speakerKey,
          audienceMessage,
          previousSpeakerText
        }
      );

    await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

    await ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
      env
    );

    await synchronizeRecallNamelessNameV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

    const subject =
      (
        audienceMessage ||
        previousSpeakerText ||
        state
          ?.conversation
          ?.currentTopic ||
        ""
      );

    let continuityText =
      "";

    let namelessMechanicsText =
      "";


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // NAMELESS GETS THE FULL ADVANCED RELATIONSHIP MEMORY
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    if (
      speakerKey ===
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS
    ) {
      try {
        const continuity =
          await buildRecallPart6ContinuityInjectionV13(
            env,
            visitorId,
            subject
          );

        continuityText =
          continuity
            ?.text ||
          "";
      } catch (
        error
      ) {
        console.error(
          "V13 continuity injection failed:",
          error
        );
      }


      // ---------------------------------------------------
      // NAME CONFUSION / RHYME / DISPLAY IDENTITY
      // ---------------------------------------------------

      try {
        const mechanics =
          await processRecallNamelessTurnMechanicsV13(
            env,
            visitorId,
            audienceMessage ||
            "",
            {
              turnNumber:
                state
                  ?.conversation
                  ?.turnNumber ||
                0
            }
          );

        namelessMechanicsText =
          [
            RECALL_V13_NAME_CONFUSION_MASTER_DIRECTIVE,

            mechanics
              ?.text ||
            ""
          ]
            .filter(
              Boolean
            )
            .join(
              "\n\n==============================\n\n"
            );
      } catch (
        error
      ) {
        console.error(
          "V13 Nameless mechanics injection failed:",
          error
        );

        const rhyme =
          await getRecallNamelessRhymeModeV13(
            env,
            visitorId
          );

        if (
          rhyme.active
        ) {
          namelessMechanicsText =
            RECALL_V13_RHYME_DIRECTIVE;
        }
      }
    }


    // -----------------------------------------------------
    // HOST GETS LIGHT CONTINUITY ONLY
    //
    // The major "knows you for years" architecture belongs
    // to Nameless.
    //
    // Host can still see the existing recent transcript and
    // V12 memory architecture normally.
    // -----------------------------------------------------

    const v13Context =
      [
        continuityText,
        namelessMechanicsText
      ]
        .filter(
          Boolean
        )
        .join(
          "\n\n==============================\n\n"
        );

    return {
      ...base,

      historicalContext:
        combineKnowledgeContexts(
          base
            ?.historicalContext ||
          "",

          v13Context
        )
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// FINAL GENERATION WRAPPER
//
// Current V12 generation already contains:
//
// - combat action BEFORE speech;
// - 1985 history;
// - Forward Vision;
// - host death continuity;
// - active speaker progression;
// - transcript persistence.
//
// Preserve ALL of it.
//
// Only synchronize Nameless identity before generation,
// then update visible output and consume one rhyme turn after
// successful Nameless speech.
// =========================================================

const generateRecallTurnBeforeV13Final =
  generateRecallTurn;


generateRecallTurn =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    options
  ) {
    const speakerKey =
      options
        ?.speakerKey;

    if (
      speakerKey ===
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS
    ) {
      await synchronizeRecallNamelessNameV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );
    }

    const generated =
      await generateRecallTurnBeforeV13Final(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        options
      );

    if (
      speakerKey !==
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS
    ) {
      return generated;
    }

    const identity =
      await initializeRecallNamelessIdentityV13(
        env,
        visitorId
      );

    const displayName =
      normalizeString(
        identity
          ?.display_name,
        generated
          ?.speakerName ||
        RECALL_V13_NAMELESS_DEFAULT_DISPLAY_NAME,
        200
      );

    state
      .nameless
      .currentTemporaryName =
        displayName;

    const rhymeCompletion =
      await finalizeRecallNamelessSpokenTurnV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        speakerKey
      );

    return {
      ...generated,

      speakerName:
        displayName,

      displaySpeaker:
        displayName,

      publicIdentityV13: {
        displayName,

        rhymeMode:
          Boolean(
            rhymeCompletion
              ?.active
          ),

        rhymeTurnsRemaining:
          Math.max(
            0,
            integerNumber(
              rhymeCompletion
                ?.turnsRemaining,
              0
            )
          )
      }
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// FOUNDATIONAL CONVERSATION IMPORT
//
// This is deliberate.
//
// We DO NOT guess which random production conversation is
// the sacred first conversation.
//
// You explicitly import the original formative transcript.
//
// POST /memory/foundation
//
// {
//   visitorId,
//   transcript,
//   summary,
//   namelessFirstImpression,
//   sourceLabel
// }
//
// The transcript can later be supplied as the original
// historical conversation that first defined Nameless's
// impression of the player.
// =========================================================

async function importRecallFoundationalConversationV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    transcript,
    summary =
      "",
    namelessFirstImpression =
      "",
    sourceLabel =
      "canonical-first-conversation",

    forceReplace =
      false
  }
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const normalizedTranscript =
    normalizeString(
      transcript,
      "",
      1_500_000
    )
      .trim();

  if (!normalizedTranscript) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "FOUNDATIONAL_TRANSCRIPT_REQUIRED"
    };
  }

  const existing =
    await hasRecallFoundationalConversation(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  if (
    existing &&
    !forceReplace
  ) {
    return {
      ok:
        false,

      error:
        "FOUNDATIONAL_CONVERSATION_ALREADY_EXISTS",

      requiresForceReplace:
        true
    };
  }

  const analyticalInput =
    `
CANONICAL FIRST CONVERSATION:

${normalizedTranscript}


TASK:

Extract the most formative long-term memories from this FIRST conversation.

This conversation receives exceptional weight because it created the initial relationship.

Identify:

- the player's important claims;
- Nameless's important responses;
- Nameless's first judgments;
- accusations or challenges;
- unresolved questions;
- distinctive exact quotations when useful;
- important personal facts;
- arguments;
- promises;
- relationship-defining events;
- Nameless's own substantive positions.

Output the same JSON structure used by the V13 memory analyst.

Do not mark trivial dialogue important merely because this is the first conversation.

The conversation itself is foundational; individual extracted memories should still be selective.

Return JSON only.
`.trim();

  const raw =
    await callRecallModel(
      env,
      {
        instructions:
          RECALL_V13_MEMORY_ANALYST_PROMPT,

        input:
          analyticalInput,

        webSearchEnabled:
          false,

        maxOutputTokens:
          5000
      }
    );

  const parsed =
    recallV13ParseModelJson(
      raw,
      {
        memories: [],
        patterns: [],
        contradictions: [],
        judgments: [],
        nameless_positions: []
      }
    );

  const normalized =
    normalizeRecallV13AnalystResult(
      parsed
    );

  let memoryCount =
    0;

  for (
    const memory
    of normalized.memories
  ) {
    const saved =
      await saveFoundationalRecallMemoryV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          characterKey:
            memory
              ?.character_key ||
            "shared",

          memoryType:
            memory
              ?.memory_type ||
            RECALL_MEMORY_TYPES.OTHER,

          subjectKey:
            memory
              ?.subject_key ||
            null,

          summary:
            memory
              ?.summary,

          detail:
            memory
              ?.detail ||
            null,

          exactQuote:
            memory
              ?.exact_quote ||
            null,

          sourceType:
            "canonical_first_conversation",

          sourceId:
            sourceLabel,

          sourceEpisode:
            1,

          importance:
            Math.max(
              RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE,

              normalizeRecallV13Importance(
                memory
                  ?.importance,
                RECALL_FOUNDATIONAL_MEMORY_MIN_IMPORTANCE
              )
            ),

          confidence:
            memory
              ?.confidence ??
            0.9,

          tags: [
            ...(
              Array.isArray(
                memory
                  ?.tags
              )
                ? memory.tags
                : []
            ),

            "first-conversation",
            "foundational"
          ],

          metadata: {
            sourceLabel
          }
        }
      );

    if (saved) {
      memoryCount++;
    }
  }

  for (
    const pattern
    of normalized.patterns
  ) {
    await upsertRecallPatternV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        characterKey:
          CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

        patternKey:
          pattern
            ?.pattern_key,

        description:
          pattern
            ?.description,

        evidenceDelta:
          pattern
            ?.evidence_delta ??
          1,

        counterexampleDelta:
          pattern
            ?.counterexample_delta ??
          0,

        confidence:
          pattern
            ?.confidence ??
          0.5,

        importance:
          Math.max(
            50,
            normalizeRecallV13Importance(
              pattern
                ?.importance,
              50
            )
          ),

        metadata: {
          source:
            "canonical-first-conversation"
        }
      }
    );
  }

  for (
    const judgment
    of normalized.judgments
  ) {
    await evolveNamelessJudgmentV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        judgmentKey:
          judgment
            ?.judgment_key,

        judgment:
          judgment
            ?.judgment,

        confidence:
          judgment
            ?.confidence ??
          0.65,

        importance:
          Math.max(
            60,
            normalizeRecallV13Importance(
              judgment
                ?.importance,
              60
            )
          ),

        supportingEvidenceDelta:
          judgment
            ?.supporting_evidence_delta ??
          1,

        opposingEvidenceDelta:
          judgment
            ?.opposing_evidence_delta ??
          0,

        metadata: {
          foundational:
            true,

          source:
            "canonical-first-conversation"
        }
      }
    );
  }

  for (
    const position
    of normalized
      .namelessPositions
  ) {
    await saveNamelessPositionV13(
      env,
      visitorId,
      {
        topicKey:
          position
            ?.topic_key,

        positionText:
          position
            ?.position_text,

        confidence:
          position
            ?.confidence ??
          0.8,

        importance:
          Math.max(
            55,
            normalizeRecallV13Importance(
              position
                ?.importance,
              55
            )
          ),

        metadata: {
          foundational:
            true,

          source:
            "canonical-first-conversation"
        }
      }
    );
  }

  await establishRecallFoundationalConversation(
    env,
    visitorId,
    {
      transcriptSnapshot:
        normalizedTranscript,

      summary:
        normalizeString(
          summary,
          "",
          50000
        ),

      namelessFirstImpression:
        normalizeString(
          namelessFirstImpression,
          "",
          50000
        ),

      metadata: {
        sourceLabel,

        importedMemoryCount:
          memoryCount
      },

      forceReplace
    }
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_foundation_import_log (
        visitor_id,
        imported_character_count,
        imported_memory_count,
        source_label,
        created_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
      )
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      normalizedTranscript.length,

      memoryCount,

      sourceLabel,

      isoNow()
    )
    .run();

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    foundational:
      true,

    transcriptCharacters:
      normalizedTranscript.length,

    memoriesImported:
      memoryCount,

    sourceLabel
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FOUNDATION ROUTE
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallFoundationImportRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const parsed =
    await readRecallV13JsonBody(
      request
    );

  if (!parsed.ok) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Invalid foundational-memory request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromBody(
      parsed.body
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  try {
    const result =
      await importRecallFoundationalConversationV13(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          transcript:
            parsed.body
              ?.transcript,

          summary:
            parsed.body
              ?.summary ||
            "",

          namelessFirstImpression:
            parsed.body
              ?.namelessFirstImpression ??
            parsed.body
              ?.nameless_first_impression ??
            "",

          sourceLabel:
            parsed.body
              ?.sourceLabel ??
            parsed.body
              ?.source_label ??
            "canonical-first-conversation",

          forceReplace:
            Boolean(
              parsed.body
                ?.forceReplace ??
              parsed.body
                ?.force_replace
            )
        }
      );

    return jsonResponse(
      result,

      result.ok
        ? 200
        : (
            result.error ===
              "FOUNDATIONAL_CONVERSATION_ALREADY_EXISTS"
              ? 409
              : 400
          )
    );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "Foundation import failed:",
      error
    );

    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          error
            ?.message ||
          "Foundation import failed."
      },
      500
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// FOUNDATION STATUS
// =========================================================

async function handleRecallFoundationStatusRouteV13(
  request,
  env
) {
  const visitorId =
    resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
      request
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const foundation =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          visitor_id,
          first_conversation_id,
          last_conversation_id,
          summary,
          nameless_first_impression,
          established_at,
          locked,
          metadata_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at

        FROM recall_foundational_conversation

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  const count =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          COUNT(*) AS count

        FROM recall_memories_v2

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND is_foundational = 1
          AND is_archived = 0
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  return jsonResponse({
    ok:
      true,

    exists:
      Boolean(
        foundation
      ),

    foundationalMemoryCount:
      integerNumber(
        count
          ?.count,
        0
      ),

    foundation:
      foundation ||
      null
  });
}


// =========================================================
// EXTEND PART-7 PRE-ROUTER
//
// Part 7 already owns:
// documents
// V13 status
//
// Add foundation routes.
// =========================================================

const routeRecallV13RequestPart7 =
  routeRecallV13Request;


routeRecallV13Request =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const url =
      new URL(
        request.url
      );

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        RECALL_V13_FOUNDATION_ROUTE
    ) {
      return await handleRecallFoundationImportRouteV13(
        request,
        env
      );
    }

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        RECALL_V13_FOUNDATION_STATUS_ROUTE
    ) {
      return await handleRecallFoundationStatusRouteV13(
        request,
        env
      );
    }

    return await routeRecallV13RequestPart7(
      request,
      env
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// ROUTER WITH CORS MUST FOLLOW THE NEW ROUTER
//
// Part 7's implementation resolves routeRecallV13Request()
// dynamically, but make final behavior explicit.
// =========================================================

routeRecallV13RequestWithCors =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const response =
      await routeRecallV13Request(
        request,
        env
      );

    if (!response) {
      return null;
    }

    return addCorsToResponse(
      response
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// RELATIONSHIP PRIMITIVE
//
// This is available to future updates and to Nameless memory
// evolution without requiring another schema migration.
// =========================================================

async function touchRecallRelationshipV13(
  env,
  visitorId,
  {
    characterKey =
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    relationshipState =
      "developing",

    notes =
      null,

    importance =
      25,

    interactionDelta =
      1,

    metadata =
      {}
  } = {}
) {
  await ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
    env
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_relationships (
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        relationship_state,
        notes,
        importance,
        interaction_count,
        first_interaction_at,
        last_interaction_at,
        metadata_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
        ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?
      )

      ON CONFLICT(
        visitor_id,
        character_key
      )

      DO UPDATE SET
        relationship_state =
          excluded.relationship_state,

        notes =
          COALESCE(
            excluded.notes,
            recall_relationships.notes
          ),

        importance =
          MAX(
            recall_relationships.importance,
            excluded.importance
          ),

        interaction_count =
          recall_relationships.interaction_count +
          excluded.interaction_count,

        last_interaction_at =
          excluded.last_interaction_at,

        metadata_json =
          excluded.metadata_json,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      normalizeString(
        characterKey,
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,
        100
      ),

      normalizeString(
        relationshipState,
        "developing",
        1000
      ),

      notes
        ? normalizeString(
            notes,
            "",
            8000
          )
        : null,

      normalizeRecallV13Importance(
        importance,
        25
      ),

      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          interactionDelta,
          1
        )
      ),

      now,

      now,

      recallV13JsonStringify(
        metadata,
        "{}"
      ),

      now,

      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// FIX COMBAT INTERRUPTION KILL BOOLEAN
//
// Part 6 already performs authoritative damage.
//
// Host death helper returns a death result object.
//
// Preserve the existing damage operation while making the
// result's killedTarget field reflect death.died rather than
// merely the existence of an object.
// =========================================================

const applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13Part6 =
  applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13;


applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13 =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    options = {}
  ) {
    const result =
      await applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13Part6(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        options
      );

    if (
      result
        ?.applied &&
      result
        ?.death
    ) {
      result.killedTarget =
        Boolean(
          result
            .death
            ?.died
        );
    }

    return result;
  };


// =========================================================
// FINAL V13 STATUS
// =========================================================

async function getRecallV13FinalStatus(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await initializeRecallMemoryV13ForVisitor(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  await initializeRecallPart6V13(
    env,
    visitorId
  );

  await ensureRecallV13CompatibilitySchema(
    env
  );

  const [
    memory,
    documents,
    names,
    largeInput,
    foundationStatus
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      getRecallMemoryV13Part3Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallDocumentV13Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallV13Part5Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      getRecallV13Part6Status(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      hasRecallFoundationalConversation(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  const failures =
    [];

  if (
    memory
      ?.part3Installed !==
        true
  ) {
    failures.push(
      "memory-retrieval"
    );
  }

  if (
    documents
      ?.part4Installed !==
        true
  ) {
    failures.push(
      "documents"
    );
  }

  if (
    names
      ?.part5Installed !==
        true
  ) {
    failures.push(
      "identity"
    );
  }

  if (
    largeInput
      ?.part6Installed !==
        true
  ) {
    failures.push(
      "large-input"
    );
  }

  return {
    ok:
      failures.length ===
        0,

    version:
      RECALL_V13_FINAL_BUILD_VERSION,

    schemaVersion:
      RECALL_MEMORY_SYSTEM_VERSION,

    failures,

    foundationImported:
      Boolean(
        foundationStatus
      ),

    memory,

    documents,

    names,

    largeInput,

    maximumAudienceInputBytes:
      RECALL_V13_LARGE_INPUT_MAX_BYTES,

    maximumDocumentBytes:
      RECALL_V13_DOCUMENT_MAX_BYTES,

    features: {
      permanentMemory:
        true,

      firstConversationPrivilege:
        true,

      documentUpload:
        true,

      documentRetrieval:
        true,

      largeAudienceInput:
        true,

      nameConfusion:
        true,

      persistentNamelessName:
        true,

      rhymeMode:
        true,

      combatInterruptionDamage:
        true,

      contradictionTracking:
        true,

      patternRecognition:
        true,

      namelessJudgments:
        true,

      namelessSelfPositions:
        true,

      unresolvedThreads:
        true,

      reinforcement:
        true
    }
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FINAL STATUS ROUTE REPLACEMENT
// =========================================================

handleRecallV13StatusRoute =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const visitorId =
      resolveRecallV13VisitorIdFromUrl(
        request
      );

    if (!visitorId) {
      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          error:
            "Missing or invalid visitorId."
        },
        400
      );
    }

    try {
      return jsonResponse(
        await getRecallV13FinalStatus(
          env,
          visitorId
        )
      );
    } catch (
      error
    ) {
      console.error(
        "V13 status failed:",
        error
      );

      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          error:
            error
              ?.message ||
            "V13 status failed."
        },
        500
      );
    }
  };


// =========================================================
// FINAL INSTALLATION SELF-CHECK
//
// This does not perform network/model calls.
//
// It verifies that all eight major code-layer primitives are
// actually present when the module loads.
// =========================================================

const RECALL_V13_INSTALLATION_CHECK =
  Object.freeze({
    memoryDatabase:
      typeof ensureRecallV13Database ===
        "function",

    memoryExtraction:
      typeof maybeExtractAdvancedRecallMemoriesV13 ===
        "function",

    memoryRetrieval:
      typeof retrieveRecallMemoriesV13 ===
        "function",

    continuityInjection:
      typeof buildRecallPart6ContinuityInjectionV13 ===
        "function",

    documents:
      typeof storeRecallDocumentV13 ===
        "function",

    identity:
      typeof processRecallNamelessTurnMechanicsV13 ===
        "function",

    largeInput:
      typeof prepareRecallAudienceInterjectionV13 ===
        "function",

    combatInterruption:
      typeof applyRecallAudienceInterruptionDamageV13 ===
        "function",

    router:
      typeof routeRecallV13RequestWithCors ===
        "function",

    finalGeneration:
      typeof generateRecallTurn ===
        "function"
  });


// =========================================================
// INSTALLATION ASSERTION
// =========================================================

for (
  const [
    subsystem,
    installed
  ]
  of Object.entries(
    RECALL_V13_INSTALLATION_CHECK
  )
) {
  if (!installed) {
    throw new Error(
      `RECALL THY NAME V13 installation incomplete: ${subsystem}`
    );
  }
}

  "13.0.0";
